# WOS RST — notes for Claude

WOS RST (www.wosrst.com) is a free, unofficial **Whiteout Survival** planner. It tracks gear, charms, research, pets, experts and heroes, and plans the cheapest packs. Most visitors use it on phones, in English, Arabic (RTL) or Korean.

> This file is published with the site (`wosrst.com/CLAUDE.md`), like everything in this repo. Never put secrets or personal data here.

## How the owner works with Claude
- **Report first, change after.** When asked to review or check something, report the findings in plain language and wait for a go-ahead before editing.
- **Don't change functions or design** unless asked. Fixes and speed-ups must look and behave the same.
- **One focused PR per change**, never pushed straight to `main`. Each PR description includes a preview link for testing on a phone before merging:
  `https://raw.githack.com/BuildwithRaed/WOS-RST/<branch>/index.html`
- **The site deploys from `main`** (GitHub Pages, root folder, no build step; live 1–2 minutes after a merge).
- **If the site doesn't update after a merge**, check the "pages build and deployment" run in GitHub Actions. On 14 Oct 2026 GitHub's deploy step timed out ("Timeout reached") for PR #29 with no code problem; a new merge to `main` publishes again.
- **After the owner says "merged", check it really is** (`git fetch origin main` and look for the merge commit). The final "Confirm merge" click has been missed before. Then restart the working branch from the new `main`.
- **Never trim, crop, zoom or edit an image without asking the owner first** (owner, 19 Oct 2026). Show pictures as they are.
- **Previews in two steps** (owner, 6 Nov 2026): the first preview of a build is tested on ONE phone size only (iPhone 15, 393 px) so it can be checked faster; once the owner confirms it, test and send a second preview for all screen sizes (360 / 393 / Fold 585 / PC 1280, owner + visitor), then do the rest (notes, translations, PR).
- **Previews only when needed** (owner, 7 Nov 2026): changes that don't change the look (speed, fixes, clean-up) get no preview step: test them fully (all sizes, owner + visitor, old vs new), then open the PR. Changes that do change the look get screenshots sent in the chat (the owner may not have the iPhone 15 at hand); the PR description still carries the preview link.
- **Previews before approval** (owner, 9 Nov 2026): the first preview covers only what was asked, tested only at iPhone 15 (393 px) and only on the changed parts; translations, other screen sizes, notes and full tests come after the owner approves.
- **Same design as the other pages** (owner, 9 Nov 2026): anything added to a page reuses the site's existing components and look (Backpack bar, Pack cost window, stat boxes, switches …), never a new style.
- **Free first, then the cheapest** (owner, 9 Nov 2026): every plan uses what the person has / earns for free first, and only then the cheapest things to buy.
- **Mock-ups, then preview, then PR** (owner, 10 Nov 2026): mock-ups only (screenshots) until the owner says build; then a preview; PR only after the preview is approved.
- **Hold PR updates until the work is final** (owner, 9 Nov 2026): keep pushing to the preview branch, but don't open / update PRs until the owner says the work is final.
- **Never bring up gift codes, Scan pack, the New Gen heroes card or the Dashboard "Stats | Plan" tabs again** (owner, 19 Nov 2026).
- **Keep sending PRs without waiting for merges** (owner, 7 Nov 2026): each step gets its own branch `ccr-41bc4d90-fokvur-step<N>` (owner allowed extra branches), built on the step before it, PR into `main`; tell the owner the merge order (oldest step first). After a merge, the later PRs shrink to their own changes by themselves.
- **Run every job in the background** (owner, 6 Nov 2026) so Claude can still answer while it works.
- **When the owner says "wait" (e.g. "wait, I have a job"), stop at once** (owner, 20 Oct 2026): don't finish the current work first; say where it was left and ask what the job is.
- Explanations should be short and non-technical. Show before/after numbers or pictures when useful.
- **Phone sizes** (owner, 3 Nov 2026): readable on every size and not too cramped; no need to make one device look like another. Text 11 px or more, buttons about 40 px tall. Later the same day, after the wostools-sized round (12 px labels, 40 px buttons), the owner asked for "the font and boxes smaller a bit": labels 11 px, buttons 36 px (small 32), boxes to type in 32 px. Then "make them smaller by 2 for font and 4 for box": on phones every text is 2 px smaller (not under 10 px) and every small box 4 px lower (`phoneCompact`, see below). Later the same day "increase font size by 2 all pages but not dashboard": the 2 px smaller text now applies inside the Dashboard only; the 4 px lower boxes stay everywhere. General design rules (e.g. a skill's "48 px touch targets") are checked against how the page looks on the owner's iPhone 15 (393 px) and Fold before they are applied.
- **Design rules for every page** (owner, 3 Nov 2026, after comparing wostools.net on the Fold and iPhone 15):
  1. Mobile first: design for narrow phones (iPhone 15, 393 px) first; the Fold (about 585 px), tablets and PC follow with fluid sizes, not fixed ones.
  2. Follow the Chief Gear page pattern (G1-B): title with an (i) square for any description, answer card first, compact cards, sheets for details.
  3. Page / card descriptions go inside an (i) square like Chief Gear's, not as text on the page.
  4. Reduce clutter: fold away what isn't needed every time, and put details in pop-up windows.
  5. Pages may scroll; don't force a page onto one screen. Change the design when needed, but keep the site's general pattern.
  6. Main goal of the site: track stats, cost and upgrades.
  7. At most two things side by side on the iPhone when they hold text; lists become full-width rows (like wostools).
  8. Mock-ups / previews first; if the owner doesn't answer, follow the Chief Gear pattern and UI/UX rules. Translations wait until the redesign ships.

## Files
- `index.html`: the HTML shell (head scripts: viewport, menu style, `phoneCompact`; the `fitFix` style; the page skeleton). No framework, no build.
- `app.css` / `app.js`: the site's styles and code (moved out of index.html 6 Nov 2026, unchanged; about 5,000 / 35,000 lines), the site loads their packed (minified) copies `app.min.css?v=<hash>` / `app.min.js?v=<hash>` (plain classic files, same order as before; app.min.js is also `<link rel=preload>`ed in the head, or Chrome only fetches it once the head is done). **Edit app.js / app.css / admin.js / scan.js (never the `.min` copies), then run `node tools/stamp.js`** (now also writes the .min copies; once per checkout run `npm ci` in `tools/` for the pinned minifiers; `--check` only reports and fails when a .min copy is stale or a hash is wrong); a stale copy or hash means phones keep the old code. `phoneCompact` still reads app.css (as app.min.css) (render-blocking link before it; its cache key is the versioned link, used only when app.css loaded with its rules: a failed load builds without saving).
- `admin.js`: the Admin page's own code (owner only, ~4,600 lines), loaded (as its packed copy `admin.min.js`) by `loadAdminCode()` the first time Admin opens (`renderAdminPage()` in app.js is a small stub that loads it, then calls `renderAdminPageMain()`); visitors never download it. What other pages use from Admin stays in app.js: `ADMIN_ACTIVE_TABLE` / `ADMIN_SKIP_CONFIRM`, the unsaved-edit checks (`ADM_EDIT_DIRTY`, `admUnsavedCount`, `admConfirmLeave` and their listeners), the game rules registry (`gameRules`, `applyGameRules`, …), `applyPageVisibility`, `GAME_NAME_TEXTS`, `ADMIN_RESERVED_BUFF_CATEGORIES`. **A function another page calls must live in app.js**, never only in admin.js. admin.js carries its build id (`window.ADMIN_BUILD`, = the `admin.min.js?v=` in app.js, written by tools/stamp.js into admin.js and admin.min.js); GitHub Pages ignores `?v=`, so a tab open since before an update can get the new admin.js: the page then reloads once (sessionStorage `admin_reload_for`), and if it still doesn't match Admin says "The site was updated. Reload the page to open Admin." (`admin_update_reload`). While admin.js loads, the scroll place is kept (`RENDER_Y0`).
- `scan.js`: the Backpack's Scan (Clean-up step 10; ~1,000 lines: Tesseract loading, tile / gear / lab readers, the review and teach screens), loaded (as `scan.min.js`) by `loadScanCode()` the first time Scan is opened; `openBackpackScan()` in app.js is the stub that loads it, then calls `openBackpackScanMain()`. Same build-id pattern as admin.js (`window.SCAN_BUILD`, = the `scan.min.js?v=` in app.js, written by tools/stamp.js; sessionStorage `scan_reload_for`, still stale → toast `scan_update_reload`; no connection → toast `no_connection_retry`). What other pages use stays in app.js: `SCAN_SIZE_COLORS` (Packs), `bpItemTab` / `bpItemTabBuiltin` / `scanItemTab` and the lists they read (`SCAN_ITEM_NAMES`, `SCAN_RES_TAB`, `SCAN_TAB_ORDER`), `SCAN_LEARNED` / `SCAN_SKIP` / `scanLearnedLoad` (+ `scanB64ToFeat`, `SCAN_FEAT_W/H`, `SCAN_SUMMARY_ROWS`) for Admin → Backpack Items.
- `tools/stamp.js`: makes the packed copies (`admin.min.js`, `scan.min.js`, `app.min.js`, `app.min.css`) and updates the hashes above (see Speed plan Step 5). `tools/package.json` / `package-lock.json` pin its minifiers (terser, csso); `tools/node_modules/` is not committed. Not loaded by the site.
- `sw.js`: service worker. The page is network-first, but when the network hasn't answered within 3 s the saved copy is shown (the answer still replaces the saved copy, and its app.min.js / app.min.css are cached too so it opens offline); icons are cache-first; app.min.js / app.min.css / admin.min.js / scan.min.js are cache-first by their `?v=` address (a copy is kept only when it is that version: app / css by SHA-256 of the served file, admin / scan by their `ADMIN_BUILD` / `SCAN_BUILD`; storing one also drops the old unpacked `app.js?v=` copy) (older versions dropped when a new one is stored; the ones index.html links to are cached at install, so the site opens offline after the first visit); the pinned supabase-js and HiGHS URLs are cache-first. Only good copies (status 200, not opaque) are kept; an old opaque copy is fetched again and replaced (27 Oct 2026). Cache name `wosrst-v2`; don't bump it without a reason, since that makes every user re-download all icons.
- `vendor/`: this site's own copies of supabase-js 2.117.3 and tesseract.js 5.1.1 (with their MIT licences), used only when the jsdelivr copy can't load or fails its fingerprint check.
- `manifest.webmanifest`, `pwa/icons/`: installable app.
- `icons/`: game images. `icons/skins/opt/` holds optimized copies of the gallery images (see Images).
- `CNAME` (www.wosrst.com), `.nojekyll`.
- `robots.txt`, `sitemap.xml` (home + privacy page, www addresses): add a page there if the site ever gets another standalone page.
- `privacy.html`: Privacy Policy (owner, 27 Oct 2026, needed for Google's branding check), a standalone page in the site colours, no scripts; linked from the footer (`renderFooter`, T key `privacy_link`, `LANG_FILES_VERSION` 52). Deletion requests go to contact@wosrst.com (site_config `contact`). Update its date and text if what the site saves or the services it uses change.

## Backend: Supabase (project `hzourauekkiorojrhpko`)
- REST through `sb(path, opts)`. Reads over 1,000 rows page automatically; `sbAllPagesAtOnce(path)` fetches the first two pages in parallel.
- **Visitors** sign in anonymously. **The owner** is detected by account id (`IS_OWNER` = session user id is `PROGRESSION_OWNER_ID`; by login email until 7 Nov 2026) and gets Admin and editing. Row-level security enforces the same rule on the server (`(select auth.uid()) = '<owner id>'`).
- Game data belongs to `PROGRESSION_OWNER_ID`. Each user's own progress is in `calc_state` (one JSON state per `calc_name`) plus `skins` / `player_heroes`.
- Storage bucket `skin-images`: public read, **owner-only writes** (Claude can't upload or delete there).
- The Supabase connector can run SQL. Use read-only queries unless the owner asked for a change, and back up anything you change first.

## Key code patterns (keep them intact)
- **Routing:** `goToTab(tab)` → `render()`. Every page render function starts with `const seq = RENDER_SEQ;` and, after each `await`, does `if (seq !== RENDER_SEQ) return;` before drawing. That stops a slow page from drawing over the page the user switched to. New pages must follow this.
- **Translations:** `t("key")` uses the big `T` dictionary (English in the code; other languages come from `lang/*.json`, see Admin Step 5). There's also an automatic DOM translator (`UI_TEXT_I18N`, `DATA_NAME_I18N`, `localizeDom` + a MutationObserver). Add keys to `T` for new text.
- **Saved state:** `loadCalcState(name)` reads from a bulk-preloaded cache. `saveCalcState(name, state)` updates the cache at once and saves in the background through a per-state queue (one request at a time, newest wins). **Don't `await` saves before redrawing.**
- **Row updates from taps** (gallery/facility cards, roster stars): `patchRowQueued(table, id, patch)` / `patchSkinQueued(id, patch)`. Show the change at once and put it back if the save fails.
- **Data kept for the visit:** `dashCached(key, load)` (Dashboard tables, VIP, Hero Stars, island categories) and `loadExpertNodes(kind)` (expert tables). Anything that changes this data must clear it:
  - `dashDataChanged("skins")` after skin edits (already done in `patchSkinQueued` and Alliance Tech)
  - `dropKeptGameData()` after buff edits and when Admin opens
  - `dashDataChanged(key)` also clears every `key:*` copy; `sb()` drops the `keptRows` copies by itself on any non-GET to their tables (see Performance)
  - **Add the same when adding new writes to these tables.**
- **Ensure-loaders** (`ensureHeroGearData`, `ensureCharmData`, ...): callers share one in-flight load, and the "loaded" flag is set only once the saved state is applied.
- **Pack planner:** heavy work runs in a Web Worker built from function source text (`PLANNER_FN_NAMES`). Functions in that list must only use other listed functions/constants. Exact plans (owner, 16 Oct 2026): the worker loads HiGHS (`highs@1.15.3` from jsdelivr, `PLAN_SOLVER_URL`, ~1 MB wasm, cached cache-first by `sw.js`; every message waits for it, 20 s time-out) and `packExactPicks` builds a MILP (one integer per offer, a row per group's buy limit `caps`, a row per item: packs + trades ≥ need, trade lots ≤ weekly max × weeks, a tiny cost per trade) solved with a 3 s limit (1 s for the quick checks). `packCheapestPlanCompute` keeps it when cheaper than the greedy plan, with the solver's own trades (the greedy trade check misses mixes of trades and used to buy extra packs); with a plan from the solver the slow greedy multi-start tries are skipped, and `budgetCombinedCore` skips its merged / page-by-page tries (one exact plan over all pages is already the cheapest). No solver (offline, blocked) → the old greedy planner, unchanged. `plannerRun("solver")` says if it loaded. Option packs (Dawn of Wisdom, Artisans Trove, Dawn Market …, `optionPack` groups with 12+ offers) go to the solver step by step (`packArcSteps`: one integer per step option, step k ≤ step k−1, mapped back to the pack's offers; used only when every sequence maps to an offer giving at least as much at the same price), 17 Oct 2026: 810 → 230 choices on the owner's 4-page plan, pages 2–6× quicker, same prices; (PR #50). Since then (17 Oct 2026) `packExactPicks` also splits the plan into parts that share no item, trade or pack limit (Experts / Hero shards / Widgets / Chief Gear + Charm, which share only the Benefit Card) and solves each part on its own (`packExactPicksPart`, same 3 s limit per part): the owner's 4-page plan now gets the best price ($1,688.84, was $1,693.83) and shows in 17 s instead of 24 s; every page alone the same or cents cheaper. A background "keep improving" pass was tested and dropped (no better plan found). The roster widget prices (`heroWidgetCost`) now use the worker too. Tested 16 Oct 2026 against an exact solver on every page (light / heavy targets, Fastest / By target / Cheapest) and all pages together: equal (all pages within 0.2%); before, up to 17% over (owner's T12 Fastest) and the all-pages plan took minutes. The 2% "sooner" rule in `packPickCheapest` still picks a faster plan within 2% (e.g. Hero Gear by target +0.6%). Budgets plan store fingerprint is `v: 3` (was 2; bumped 17 Oct 2026 with the smaller planner message) so phones work the plans out again once.
- **Planner speed-ups** (17 Oct 2026, speed audit; plans, prices and dates unchanged): `packEarliestDaysCompute`'s `feasible(d)` calls `packCheapestPlanCompute(…, quick, feasOnly = true)`, which says yes as soon as the greedy plan covers the day (no HiGHS; those yes answers are not cached, full answers still go to `PACK_PLAN_CACHE`). The worker message sends each offer once (`plannerPayloadEncode` → `payload.offers`, groups point to them by number; `plannerPayloadDecode` is the first line of `plannerCombinedLocal` and is in `PLANNER_FN_NAMES`; `packGroupsForBuild` shares one gem-free copy per offer, `PACK_GEM_FREE` WeakMap): all pages 26.4 MB → 4.4 MB, decoded message identical; `budgetPlanFingerprint` hashes the smaller message. `packShortAfterTrades` uses a per-ctx priority index (`ctx._pri`; 5 million calls compared, 0 differences). `packLinkSetMembers` / My Purchases `setLead` read only the linkSet (`packLinkSetOf`, `packLinkRaw`). `newGenBaseDay` is memoised for the current piece of work (`NEW_GEN_BASE_MEMO`, cleared by a microtask, on date / state age / `PACK_LINKS` changes and by `budgetInvalidate` / `savePackLinks` / `afterGameRulesChange`; old body `newGenBaseDayCompute`). Planner requests go one at a time (`PLANNER_QUEUE`, `PLANNER_BUSY`, `plannerPump`); `plannerSupersede(p)` drops a request nobody wants (queued: removed; running: worker stopped, the rest re-sent via `plannerRestart`; its promise rejects with `err.superseded`, never falls back to the page); Budgets drops its previous plan when it starts a new one (`BUDGET_PLAN_TRACK`, `more.track`); `budgetForceRecalc` / `afterGameRulesChange` use `plannerRestart()` (waiting requests used to hang). `plannerWarm()` starts the worker (and its HiGHS load) as Budgets / My Purchases start loading.
- **Planner speed-ups 2** (owner, 10 Nov 2026: ticking Heroes Gears in Budgets took 63 s on a PC; same prices and dates): (1) *Yes / no straight from the solver*: the earliest-day search's `feasible(d)` → `packCheapestPlanCompute(…, feasOnly)` first asks HiGHS with no prices (`packExactPicks(…, feas = true)` → `packExactPicksPart`: objective `+ 0 x0`, 1 s limit, every number rounded the generous way with `numUp` / `numDown` so a "no" is never down to rounding): "Infeasible" → no plan (returns `{ infeasible: true }`; with several parts one "no" is enough), a plan found → counted again in full (`rem` ≤ 1e-6 for every item) → yes; no answer in time → the old steps (step-by-step run, then the priced solve) decide as before. (2) *Search around the best guess* (solver only): `packEarliestDaysCompute(groups, need, calcKey, hint)` finds the first step the packs' limits allow (`supplyOk`, cheap), starts at `hint` when given (combined plans: `ownHint(list)` in `budgetCombinedCore` = the slowest page's own earliest day, passed through `budgetPlanSet(…, hint)` and to the late plan) and looks 1, 2, 4 … steps away, then halves what is left; the "3 steps before" full-planner tries are skipped with the solver (its yes / no is exact). `hint` only moves where the search starts (`PACK_EARLIEST_CACHE` key unchanged). (3) *Big plans: solver first*: `solverFirst` (solver loaded, not yes / no, over 500 offers after pruning) skips the step-by-step `run(null)` before the priced solve and runs it only when the solver found nothing; smaller plans are exactly as before. (4) *Set-up kept between days*: `packPlanPrep(groupsIn, need, calcKey, ctx)` (free trades folded + `packPruneOffersFor`, the same for every day) is kept per pack list, `packPlanCacheKey` keeps the packs' long signature per pack list (`packGroupsSig`), and option packs' steps (`packArcSteps`) are kept per pack; all three live inside `PACK_PLAN_CACHE` under the keys `"__prep"` / `"__sig"` / `"__arc"` (WeakMaps keyed by the pack list / pack), so everything that clears the plan cache clears them too. No solver (offline, blocked) → the old greedy planner and its old search, unchanged (22 plans compared: identical). New names in `PLANNER_FN_NAMES`: `packGroupsSig`, `packPlanPrep`. Measured on the owner's data (PC Chromium, real HiGHS): open Budgets fresh (7 pages) 32.4 → 15.7 s, tick Heroes Gears (8 pages) 63.0 → 11.0 s, same totals (QAR 26,225 / 111,774); 37 replayed plans (all pages, single pages, pairs, targets none / 10 / 25 / 60 / 120 / 300 / 700 days, My budget on, each page's Pack cost, two goal-date plans) 864 → 230 s in all, every earliest day and finish date equal, prices equal or within the solver's own run-to-run noise (0.03 – 0.1%, both directions); a side-by-side run (the new code also working every answer out the old way, with the solver's answers held fixed) found 0 differences in ~700 yes / no answers and plans, also with the solver given 1/5 of its time. What is left (about 13 of the 15 s) is the solver's fixed time limits: four 3 s solves and one 1 s solve that are still finding cheaper plans when stopped (main Fastest, main Cheapest, the by-target part, the after-target Fastest and its quick far plan). The next three steps (two planners side by side, parts shown as they come in, the last few plans kept) are the next entry. Found on the way (already so on main): the limits are wall-clock time, so a device where the solver runs slower stops on an earlier, dearer plan (owner's all-pages plan with 1/5 of the time: +3 to +7%); the "My budget" candidate plans use the 1 s quick solve and vary most (one was 7.8% apart between two runs).
- **Planner speed-ups 3** (owner, 10 Nov 2026: "do 1 to 3"; same prices and dates): (1) *Two planners side by side*: the planner manager has two lanes (`PLANNER_LANES` = [{ w, queue, busy, dead }] × 2, `PLANNER_OFF` when workers can't start; `plannerWorker(li)`, `plannerPump`, `plannerRestartLane(li, drop)`, `plannerRestart(drop)` = both, `plannerSupersede(p)` stops only that request's lane, `plannerWarm()` starts both). Lane 0 gets every kind of request one at a time, as before; lane 1 is used only by `plannerRun(kind, payload, { lane: 1 })` and only when `plannerLanes()` is 2 (workers exist, lane 1 not dead, `navigator.hardwareConcurrency` ≥ 4 or unknown). `budgetCombinedPlanAsync` sends two requests when there is a target date and two or more planned pages: lane 0 `{ ...payload, par: true }` = the whole plan without the split (`budgetCombinedCore(…, { noSplit })`, answer marked `splitElsewhere`), lane 1 `{ ...payload, splitOnly: true }` = only the split around the target (`opts.splitOnly`: the main plan's earliest day through `planSet`, a split tried when the target is sooner than it, returns `{ split, inc, dropped }`), merged as `{ ...a, split: wantsSplit(a) ? b.split : null }` (`wantsSplit` = the old rule: target set, no `byTarget`, a `chosen`, 2+ pages, so a split the main plan doesn't want is dropped). Both flags need the solver in that worker: without it lane 0 works everything out the old way (no `splitElsewhere`; lane 1's request is superseded) and lane 1 answers `{ skipped: true }` (then, or when lane 1 fails, lane 0 is asked for the split too). The fingerprint is made from the payload without the flags, so kept plans stay valid. `budgetTrackDrop(track)` supersedes both requests (`track.req`, `track.req2`). (2) *Parts shown as they come in*: the worker posts `{ id, part }` before its answer (`plannerRun(…, { onPart })`; `plannerCombinedLocal(payload, onPart)`): `{ split: { target } }` when the by-target part of the split is ready, `{ main, step: "set" }` when the plan is in but the Cheapest card still needs its own solve (target within reach), `{ main, step: "views" }` before the "My budget" plans. `budgetCombinedPlanAsync(…, { onPart })` hands the page a partial plan each time (same shape as the answer, `pending: true`, `have` = { set, views, split: true | "target" | false }); Budgets keeps it in `BUDGET_DATA.combined` with `partKey` = the plan key and redraws (`partRedraw`: `keepFolds` so an opened line stays open, `saveBoxScroll(content)` so a window left open over the page stays scrolled where it was (the finished plan's redraw too), and, only once the person has scrolled (`y > 0`; at the top the page stays at the top), when the total card ends in the top half of the screen or above it, the page tile on screen (`[data-b3page]`) keeps its place while the card grows (`infoize(content)` runs right after the redraw, before measuring: the page description sat under the title for a frame and made the card look 78 px taller; before 24 Nov 2026 every part scrolled a page at the top down, on iPhone creeping 0 → 78 → 312 → … → 546 px); a part doesn't redraw while a row is being dragged (`.ds-lift`) or a box on the page is being typed in / picked from, the next part or the finished plan does). In `renderBudgetsPage`: `pendingAll` / `have`, `splitWait` (the "by target" view waits for the split), `viewsWait`, `viewReady(view)`; `planPending` now means the view on screen isn't ready, and while it is true everything below sees no plan as before (`combined` emptied); only the plan cards (a card whose plan isn't in shows "…") and the split's lines (the by-target line, then "After your target …") show the parts. Goals with their own dates: `budgetDeadlineFor` is asked once the whole plan is in (`!pendingAll`; the planner is busy until then, and with the split still to come the pages' order in its request could change, which worked the dated plan out twice). No new texts or styles. (3) *The last few plans kept*: `budget_plans_v2` = { list: [{ key, day }] } (most recently used first, `BUDGET_PLANS_KEPT` 5, other days' plans dropped) with each plan in `budget_plan_v2:<key>` (`budgetPlanKept(key)` moves a read plan to the front, `budgetPlanKeep(key, res)` drops the oldest when the phone's storage is full, `budgetPlansForget()` for ↻ Recalculate); the single `budget_plan_v1` plan from before is still read. Measured on the owner's data (PC Chromium, real HiGHS; live site → step 2 → now): open Budgets fresh (7 pages) 32.4 → 15.7 → 10.1 s with the by-target line at about 6 s and Fastest / Cheapest at about 7 s; tick Heroes Gears 63.0 → 11.0 → 6.9 s; back to ticks or a date worked out today 0.1 s; a target every page reaches: the total at 6.3 s, the cards at 9.3 s; My budget: its card last (8.5 s after the rest). One planner (2-core phones): as step 2, with the by-target line early. 23 combined plans replayed one planner vs two: the same earliest days and finish dates, prices within the solver's own noise (two solves now run at the same time, so on this 4-core test machine the totals moved a few QAR more often between runs: 26,225 / 26,227 / 26,237 / 26,245, under 0.1%). Browser checks passed: far target, no target, My budget on, ticks changed quickly (older requests dropped), ↻ Recalculate, the second planner failing, the old kept plan, storage full.
- **Planner speed-ups 4** (owner, 11 Nov 2026, after ticking Heroes Gears Set 2 on the phone took over 30 s with nothing shown; same prices and dates): measured with the owner's own setup (6 pages, a set that can't make the target, My budget on, goals with their own dates). All of the time is the solver running to its time limits (two 3 s plans before anything showed, eight 1 s My budget plans, two 3 s plans for the goals' dates) and the second planner sat idle after its split. (1) *The plan shown first is worked out first*: `budgetPlanSet(…, early)` hands over the by-target plan before the fastest one (which only decides the 2% "sooner" pick), or the fastest one when the target is out of reach; `budgetCombinedCore`'s `early0` posts it as a part: step "set" with `early: true` (the pick follows as the final "set", which also carries the Fastest card: `views.fastest`, no solve of its own), or step "fast" (the Fastest card, `inc`, `noTarget`). Solver only; no early parts when a page was left out (`dropped`). (2) *The split*: `lateFor(plan, groups, shares)` plans the pages after the target; with `earlyPlan` the by-target part and then the whole split (`{ split, early: true }`) are handed over before the pick's fastest plan is worked out, and the split is worked out again only when the pick changes (never seen in 23 test plans). (3) *Page* (`budgetCombinedPlanAsync`): `acc.fast` / `acc.early` / `acc.splitEarly`, `have.fast`, `sameSplit` (no redraw when the final split is the early one), `wantsSplit` accepts `noTarget`; `renderBudgetsPage`: `startOnly` (target out of reach, only the Fastest card in): the split is the plan on screen as soon as it is in, without waiting for the Cheapest card's plan. (4) *Goals' dated plan*: asked for as soon as the plan on screen and the split are in (with a target), on the second planner (`plannerRun("deadline", payload, { lane: 1 })`); `budgetDeadlineDrop()` drops one still running when a new plan starts or other goals / dates are asked for (`BUDGET_DEADLINE.req`). Measured (PC Chromium, real HiGHS, that setup; before → now): first prices 7.9 → 4.5 s, total 8.2 → 4.9 s, goal dates 22.8 → 14.3 s, everything 22.8 → 16.0 s; with the set unticked (target in reach): total 6.3 → 3.3 s, goal dates 24.5 → 10.3 s. Ideas not built: share the My budget plans between the planners (8 s on one of them), skip the pick's fastest plan with a bound, stop a solve once it is within 0.3% of the proven best (on a PC the solver has its best plan after 1 – 2.5 s and spends the rest of its 3 s proving it; phones don't get that far).
- **A data change while the plan runs restarts it** (23 Nov 2026): Budgets' `planKey` (`renderBudgetsPage`) is `${targetDays}|${selSig}|${wantCands}|${PACK_GROUPS_GEN}`; `budgetInvalidate()` bumps `PACK_GROUPS_GEN`, so a goal / hero target / tick change while the combined plan is still being worked out gives a new key: the redraw drops the old request (`budgetTrackDrop`) and starts the new one. Before, the key stayed the same, no new plan started and the old one's answer was thrown away (`BUDGET_DATA.pages` changed), so the page said "Working out the cheapest packs…" for ever. `BUDGET_PLAN_FAILED` uses the same key (a data change tries a failed plan again); kept plans / the fingerprint don't use it.
- **Keeping the place on redraws** (owner, 17 Oct 2026): `render()` on the same page (not via `goToTab`) keeps the page height and scroll (`LAST_RENDER_TAB`). Helpers: `keepPlace(fn)` (page scroll + height while `fn` redraws), `holdPageHeight(on)` (Budgets holds its height while the plan is "Working out…"), `saveBoxScroll(root)` / `keepBoxes(root, fn)` (scroll of the boxes in `KEEP_BOX_SEL`: week rows, suggestion lists, pop-up tables, calendar chips, Admin tables …), `keepFolds(root, fn)` (open `<details>` by class + summary text without numbers), `admRedrawKeep(fn)` (Admin tables after adding rows). My Purchases shows "Loading…" only on the first draw and keeps the suggestions box's height / scroll (`PUR_SUG_KEEP`); the Pack Cost card keeps its height while working out. New redraws of the same page should use these. Tested: Packs' `contain-intrinsic-size:auto 600px` does not move the page (a smaller value did), so it stays.
- **Sorting = drag only** (owner, 4 Nov 2026: "only use drag for anything that has an option to sort order"): one shared pointer drag `dragSort(handle, o)` (touch and mouse; hold the ⋮⋮ handle `dsHandleHtml`, the row lifts, a blue line shows where it lands, the scroll box scrolls by itself near its edge, ↑ / ↓ (← / → for columns) on a focused handle move it one place). Used by Order of goals, the quick pages sheet, the upgrade order sheet, Packs column order in Arrange, Admin Menu & pages, Rules tables, Dashboard sources, Skins / Islands category chips (hold, then drag) and every `wireAdminReorder` list / table. Every ▲▼, ◀ ▶, Move left / right and "⇅ Sort" button is gone (Admin item lists open their sheet on a row tap, no ✎); orders that save by themselves show "✓ Order saved" (`dsSaved`, not after an error note, `TOAST_ERRS`). New sortable lists use `dragSort`, not buttons. **Drag only after "⇅ Arrange"** (owner, 17 Nov 2026: "Drag always working when I scroll down and up … I only like an arrange button to enable drag to sort order"): every list above starts still, its ⋮⋮ handles hidden; a small "⇅ Arrange" button (the Packs `pkb_arrange` look, `.arr-btn`) turns dragging on for that list (handles show, the button reads "✓ Done"; ↑ / ↓ on a handle only then), Done turns it off. One switch per list key, kept for the visit (`ARRANGE_ON`): helpers `arrangeBtnHtml(key)`, `arrangeBarHtml(key, hint, cls)` (button + a hint shown only while on), `arrangeHostAttr(key)` / `arrangeHost(el, key)` on the list's box (`data-arrhost`, `data-arron` while on; CSS hides `.ds-h` / `[data-goaldrag]` inside a host that is off), `arrangeSet(key, on)`, one capture click handler on `[data-arrbtn]`; `dragSort(h, { arrange: key })` does nothing while that key is off (touch, mouse, the chips' hold, keys). Keys: `goals`, `quick`, `upgrade_order`, `adm:menu`, `adm:dashsrc`, `rule:<rule key>` (the Rules table's ⋮⋮ column is hidden while off), `adm:cats:<page>` (Skins / Islands chips: a hold no longer drags while off; dashed chips while on), and `adm:<section>:<row attr>` (`admArrKey`) for every `wireAdminReorder` list / table, which puts the button above the list itself unless its template did (`admListHtml`); in tables the order column stays, empty, while off. The phone level tables' "Reorder" chip turns Arrange on with the table and off with the list. The Packs page's own Arrange and the pack editor's column Arrange are unchanged (no key). New sortable lists pass `arrange` and add a button.
- **Swipe cards:** `wireSwipeGesture` / `wireTroopSwipe` / `renderSwipeCounter` set `el.onpointerdown = ...` on purpose (no listener stacking on re-render).
- **Performance choices to keep:**
  - Packs editors are built on first Edit (17 Oct 2026): a closed card carries only an empty `<div class="pk-body" hidden data-pk-lazy>`; `buildPkBody(panel)` fills it from `pkBodyHtml(t, ti)` and wires that panel only (`wireTable([panel])`, `wirePackLinkEditors(null, body)`, visitors `applyPacksReadOnly(panel, true)` + `refreshTotalsIn`). Tables in `PACKS_OPEN` are built when drawn. Anything new inside an editor must be wired in `wireTable(panels)` / `wirePackLinkEditors(boxes, root)`, not by a whole-list query. Same HTML as before (owner + visitor, 390 / 1280).
  - Rows kept for the visit (17 Oct 2026): `keptRows(table, path)` = `dashCached("<key>:<path>")` handing each caller a deep copy; used by Skin Gallery, Islands, Facility, Alliance Tech (`skins`), Tundra Album / Collection Gallery (`lv:<table>`) and `packTablesRows()` (one `pack_tables?select=*` read shared by Packs, My packs, Pack calendar and `ensurePackTables`). `sb()` drops the copy before and after any write to `skins`, `skins_catalog`, `pack_tables`, `tundra_album_levels`, `collection_gallery_levels` (`keptRowsWritten`); new tables join through `keptRowsKey`.
  - Dashboard cold start: `dashFixedReads()` (buff table, skins, heroes, player_heroes, star / widget stats, VIP) starts in `boot()` right after `preloadCalcStates()` when the first tab is the Dashboard; the buff and VIP tables are one request each with embedded columns / rows (VIP keeps `order=sort_order` on both). If `BUFF_TABLE_ID` is already known or nothing comes back, the old path runs.
  - Packs column dropdowns are built with only their shown option (`data-pt-lazy`) and filled on first touch/focus.
  - Owner "Used for" boxes are built on first open (`data-packlinklazy`).
  - `packResourceChoices` caches its sorted list.
  - Hero Gear / T12 / Research Center update only the changed values instead of redrawing.
  - Budgets: built pack groups are kept for the visit (`PACK_GROUPS_CACHE`, cleared by `budgetInvalidate()` and when the pack tables reload), also per table (`packTableGroups`) and per option pack (in `packOptionLadderGroups`) so the ~10 pages share one build (copies, never edited in place: the gems strip makes copies); only the ticked pages' packs are sent to the planner worker; `ensurePackTables` shares one load; `renderBudgetsPage` starts the Dashboard stats, pack tables, trade/marks rates and saved needs together and works the pages out alongside the Dashboard stats. The page is drawn as soon as the pages are worked out; the combined packs plan runs in the background (`BUDGET_PLAN_RUNNING` = its key) and the page redraws with prices when it finishes ("Working out the cheapest packs…" until then). Each page's "Materials (Still needed / Full amount)" uses the same `.bud-mat` chips (picture, still needed in red or ✓, "/ full" small). "Materials still needed" is a button (starts closed, `BUDGET_MATS_OPEN` kept for the visit) that opens a row of chips in place (`.bud-mat`: item picture + short amount, name and exact number in the title).
  - Packs tables (`[data-packtable]`) use `content-visibility:auto` so off-screen tables skip layout.
  - Budgets packs plan kept on the phone (owner, 16 Oct 2026): `budgetCombinedPlanAsync` saves the planner result in localStorage `budget_plan_v1` with a fingerprint (`budgetPlanFingerprint`: cyrb53 of everything sent to the planner worker + trade rates + game rules + user + today), so a page refresh with nothing changed shows prices at once (used by Budgets and My Purchases suggestions). Any change to a need, pack, purchase, tick, target, trade or the day gives a new fingerprint. "↻ Recalculate" on Budgets (`budgetForceRecalc`) drops the saved copy, the planner worker and every kept pack / trade / needs copy and works everything out again.
- **Planner page layout** (T12 design, used by T12, T11, Research Center, Chief Gear, Charms, Heroes Gears, Pets, Pet Refine, Experts Level/Research): `plxShellHtml({title, settings, amountsId, needId, needTitle, timeId, tabsId, topExtra, mainHtml, sideExtra})` builds two columns on PC (side: Settings, "Amounts from your Backpack" tiles with Trade items and Pack Cost; main: "Total resources needed" squares, title + troop tabs, the page's content) and stacks them on phones (settings, totals, content, amounts). Helpers: `plxTimeHtml` (research time box), `plxNodeHtml` / `plxSectionHtml` (NOW − n + / TARGET nodes in folding sections), `plxTabsHtml` (tabs), `capSettingsHtml` (level cap in Settings), `navLabel(tab)`. Page title sits on top (`.plx-head`) with "Max everything" on its right (`maxId`). Stats: `plxStatBoxesHtml` (boxes with of-max bar) and `plxStatTabsHtml(id, groups)` (several stat groups behind tabs, one click handler on `document`, chosen tab kept in `PLX_STAT_TAB`) — used by the planner pages, Dashboard troops, Alliance Tech, VIP, Tundra Album, Collection Gallery. `renderPickStrip` = picture/name strip to pick a pet or expert. `plxAmountModal` = the tap-to-type pop-up for tiles outside the shared bar (Experts Level). `renderChiefGearResourcesBar` puts the needed squares into `BAR_SUMMARY_HOST[calcKey]` and shows tiles unless `BAR_SHOW_ALL[calcKey] === false`. Other pages use `.panel`, which has the same card look.
- **Escape** database/user text with `esc()` before putting it in HTML.
- **Security hardening** (owner, 27 Oct 2026, after a security audit; nothing on screen changes):
  - Outside scripts are fingerprint-checked (SRI sha384, worked out from the npm packages, which jsdelivr serves unchanged): supabase-js `<script integrity crossorigin>` with a `document.write` fallback to `vendor/supabase-js-2.117.3.js`; Tesseract `tesseract.min.js` the same way (fallback `vendor/tesseract-5.1.1.min.js`); HiGHS in the planner worker is fetched with `integrity` (`PLAN_SOLVER_SRI` for highs.js and highs.wasm), run from a blob (`importScripts`) with `wasmBinary`; a changed file → no solver → the greedy planner. **Changing a version means new hashes** (`openssl dgst -sha384 -binary file | base64`) and new vendor copies.
  - CSP: `script-src` / `connect-src` name the exact jsdelivr paths (supabase-js@2.117.3, highs@1.15.3, tesseract.js@5.1.1, tesseract.js-core@5.1.1, @tesseract.js-data/eng@1.0.0) instead of the whole host; `script-src` also has `blob:` (the solver). A new CDN file must be added there.
  - Admin-typed texts: `safeLangText` (in `injectLangPack` / `applyEnglishOverrides`) removes tags other than `<br>`, `on…=` and `javascript:`; `safeImgUrl` lets only `https://` links or the site's own files through for `site_config.pet_images` (`petImage`), and those `<img>` now use `esc()`.
  - Backup & Restore checks every row before restoring (`backupRowOk`: key filled, only today's columns, owner id on calc_state / buff_tables, no page code `BACKUP_UNSAFE`); rows that fail are left out and counted in the preview. The owner's real data (6,484 rows) all pass.
  - Password reset asks in a hidden password box (`openNewPasswordModal`, was a plain `prompt()`).
  - Server row-level security was checked table by table the same day: shared game data is owner-only to write, each person's data is their own.
- **Sign-in** (owner, 27 Oct 2026): passwords stay at least 6 characters (`PASSWORD_MIN`, `password_short`; owner kept the Supabase minimum at 6 to match). Supabase sends its emails through Resend (custom SMTP, sender noreply@wosrst.com, domain wosrst.com verified with DNS records at Spaceship) and "Confirm email" is on. Account emails come in the site language: `syncAccountLang()` (at start and in `setLanguage`) and sign-up save `user_metadata.lang`; the Supabase templates (Confirm signup, Reset Password, Change Email Address, Magic Link) pick Arabic / Korean with `{{ if eq (printf "%v" .Data.lang) "ar" }}`, anything else English. "Continue with Google" at the top of the sign-in / create-account box (`fillGoogleButton`, `.acc-google`, "or with email" line `.acc-or`), shown only when Google is switched on in Supabase (`authSettings()` reads `/auth/v1/settings`, `external.google`). A guest links Google to the account they already have (`linkIdentity`, same user id, everything saved is kept; needs Supabase "Allow manual linking"); if that Google account already has an account here it comes back with `identity_already_exists` and `googleReturnCheck()` (in `boot()`, flag sessionStorage `google_link`) signs in to it instead (`signInWithOAuth`). Return address = the page's own address, so it must be in Supabase → Authentication → URL Configuration. T keys `google_continue`, `or_email`, `google_failed`, `google_in_use` (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 51).
- **Sign-in setup outside the code** (owner, 27 Oct 2026; no keys or secrets here): Google Cloud project "WOS RST" → Google Auth Platform: one Web client (redirect URI = the Supabase `/auth/v1/callback`, origin www.wosrst.com), app **published** (In production, not Testing: in Testing only listed test users can sign in), branding (name WOS RST, snowflake logo, home page, privacy page, authorized domain wosrst.com) **verified and shown**. wosrst.com is verified in Google Search Console with a TXT record at Spaceship (`google-site-verification=…`, keep it: removing it undoes the verification); the site Google account is an Owner there. Resend: domain wosrst.com (region Ireland) verified with DKIM / SPF / DMARC records at Spaceship; Supabase custom SMTP = smtp.resend.com:465, user `resend`, sender noreply@wosrst.com "WOS RST" (30 emails an hour, raise under Rate Limits if needed). Supabase Auth: Google provider on, "Allow manual linking" on, "Confirm email" on, minimum password length 6. Domain DNS is at Spaceship (Advanced DNS), not Cloudflare. Owner chose no two-step login (MFA) for the admin account (27 Oct 2026).
- **Backpack page** (`renderInventory`): square tiles of every item the site uses, with tabs (All + each group; speedup groups share one Speedups tab). The item list is site_config `backpack_items` (Admin → Backpack Items; default `BACKPACK_DEFAULT_GROUPS`). Amounts are per user in calc_state `backpack`. **Planner pages fill their owned boxes from it** (`BACKPACK_PAGE_LINKS`, `backpackAmountFor`, applied in `ensureMaterialOwnedCache`, the Experts pages, `budgetOwnedFor` and the general speedup pool) once the person has anything in the Backpack; typing in a box keeps that number (calc_state `backpack_manual` = { calcKey: { item: true } }, "Use Backpack" link puts it back). Converted: XP Component = XP 10×10 + XP 100×100; speedup minutes = counts × minutes. Chests are shown read-only (`BACKPACK_PAGE_CHESTS`): Custom Resource Chests Lv.1–3 (+ Secured) count as your choice of Meat/Wood/Coal/Iron on the research pages (hidden rules `BACKPACK_RES_CHEST_RULES`); Chief Gear Chest counts as 400 Alloy + 4 Polish + 1 Design (`ownedWithChests`, noted as assumed); chance chests (Random Chief Gear/Charm, Lucky Gear, FC Chest/Crate) are shown but not counted. "?" chests are not in the Backpack. The old `items` / `player_inventory` tables are no longer used. The All tab is one grid with no headings, in the game's own Backpack order (`BP_GAME_ORDER`: Resources tab items incl. resource chests, then Other tab items, then hero shards), with all speedups last under a "Speedups" heading (`BP_SPEEDUP_ORDER`). Hero shards: the 3 General Hero Shards are in the default list; each person adds the heroes whose shards they track ("+ Add hero" per rarity, list saved in calc_state `backpack_heroes`, amounts saved as "<hero name> Shards"). **Scan screenshot** (`openBackpackScan`; the code is in scan.js since Clean-up step 10): reads game screenshots on the phone (never uploaded) with Tesseract.js 5.1.1 from jsdelivr, loaded only when Scan is used (that is why the CSP allows `'wasm-unsafe-eval'` and jsdelivr in `connect-src`). It reads the "Resource & Speedup Summary" Resources tab (last column = Total Resources, city + backpack; resource chests aren't counted there) and the Backpack Speedup tab (tiles found as same-size squares on the blue panel; size from the tile colour, type from the corner mark compared with `icons/scan-marks.png`, amount read from the bottom-right number). A check screen lists every value with "please check" marks before saving. Resources/Other tab tiles are recognised by picture: `scanTileFeature` (30×17 colour grid) compared with the rows of `icons/scan-items.png` (`SCAN_ITEM_NAMES`, learned from the owner's PC and phone screenshots; a tile further than 20 is skipped, over 12 or a close second is marked to check; Custom Resource Chest Lv.1/Lv.2 share a picture and are told apart by tile colour, green = Lv.1, blue = Lv.2). Numbers are read on a wide strip, then on a tight crop if nothing was read; a number made only of thin "1" shapes is counted from the shapes. Learned 16 Oct 2026 from the owner's phone screenshots: FC Chest, Holger Sigils, Marks of Valor, Mystery Points, a Gen's widget box ("Gen Widgets": the cream "S14" on the box is read by `scanGenLabel`, saved as "Gen 14 Widgets", always marked to check), and 16 heroes' own shards (Cara, Ligeia, Karol, Hervor, Rufus, Eleonora, Gregory, Fred, Sonya, Bradley, Renee, Wu Ming, Norah, Hector, Ahmose, Logan; the game lists them by Gen, newest first). Hero shards need a closer match (≤ 11, sure ≤ 8: two heroes' pictures can be only 14 apart). Shards of a hero not on the person's list, or a Gen widget box not shown yet, are offered on the check screen ("will be added to your Backpack") and put on the list on Save (`openBackpackScan(…, more)` with `canAdd` / `added` from `renderInventory`). Amounts: `scanTileAmount` counts the digit shapes; when the reader gives fewer digits (a thin "1" lost: "31" → "3") it also reads the tight cut (single word, then line) and marks the number to check if still short. Items not in any screenshot (owner, 16 Oct 2026): the check screen ends with "Not in your screenshots — set to 0?" listing items with an amount here that Scan can read (`SCAN_ITEM_NAMES` / "Gen N Widgets" when an item tab was read, speedups when the Speedup tab was read; Summary resources never), ticked; Save sets the ticked ones to 0 (`seen`, `sawItems`, `sawSpeed`, `[data-scanzero]`). **Mythic Gear** (owner, 20 Oct 2026: "All orange pieces count, 1 box = 1 Mythic Gear"): the Mythic Gear box in the Other tab is learned (last row of `icons/scan-items.png`, loaded as `?v=2` — bump the `v` when rows are added, icons are cached cache-first; `scanMatchItem` skips names past the picture's rows); a Backpack **Gear tab** screenshot is recognised by the light-blue troop badge at each tile's top-left (`scanGearTiles`, kind "gear", before the item match), and each orange tile counts as 1 Mythic Gear (red Lv.10 / +19 and purple tiles not counted). The check screen adds both (`mgParts` { box, gear }, several Gear screenshots add up, always "please check") and shows "2 boxes + 3 orange gear pieces" (`scan_mg_parts`, "?" for a tab not read); a Gear-tab-only scan doesn't fill the "set to 0" list. Justus Sigils learned 27 Oct 2026 from the owner's phone screenshot (last row of `icons/scan-items.png`, now `?v=3`; it used to come out as "unknown", nearest Romulus at 20.4); the other tiles of that screenshot and the Items example screenshot read exactly as before. Scan fixes (owner's phone screenshots, 31 Oct 2026): (1) thin "1"s lost among other digits ("171" read as "7"): `scanTextMask` keeps which digit shapes are thin (`c.thin`), and when the reader gives exactly the wide digits `scanTileAmount` puts a "1" back at each thin shape (marked please check); (2) a protected chest's shield touching its number: the mask is cut to the number's own band before the shapes are counted, and a mark that ends above the digits' line isn't a digit (140 was read as 2 / 1140); (3) "Custom Resource Chest Lv.3 (protected)" learned (new row of `icons/scan-items.png`; it used to match the plain Lv.3 and overwrite its amount); (4) Gear screenshots that overlap (scrolled with rows in both) count those rows once: `scanGearTiles` returns `{ orange, seq }` (each gear tile's picture grid in reading order), `scanGearOverlap(prev, next)` finds the longest run (a row at least, average difference < 8) at the end of one screenshot matching the start of the other and takes its orange pieces off (`gearSeqs` in `openBackpackScan`; owner's 2 screenshots: 24 + 11 → 27, either order; the same screenshot twice counts once). Every other screenshot on hand reads the same as before. (5) Gareth Sigils (Scan read it as Cyrille at 18.5 and could overwrite Cyrille's amount) and Amber learned from the owner's Other-tab screenshot (rows of `icons/scan-items.png`); Rare General Hero Shard learned the same day (blue puzzle piece, Other tab; last row, `?v=6`). Pet Food, Chief Stamina and Hero XP come from the Summary screen (owner: leave those as they are). (6) "Set to 0?" only for the tabs scanned (owner, 31 Oct 2026): each item Scan reads has its game tab (`scanItemTab`: Resources = `SCAN_RES_TAB` (FC, Refined FC, FC Shard, FC Chest, FC Crate, the six Custom Resource Chests, the plain Resource Chest; owner's answers), Speedup, everything else Other) and its order in that tab (`SCAN_TAB_ORDER`, from the owner's screenshots and the order the items were learned); each item screenshot covers one stretch of its tab (first to last item it showed, `shotSpans`); stretches that share items join up (`rng` in `showReview`), and a missing item is offered only when it sits strictly inside a stretch — so scanning Resources never zeroes Other items, a half-scrolled tab keeps what's further down, and rows skipped between two screenshots are kept (owner's test: Sail of Conquest / Fiery Heart between the first Other page and the sigils page); the Mythic Gear box sits right after Pet Chest in the Other tab, next to the Gen widget box (from the owner's older Other-tab screenshot; it was first placed after Mithril, which made a scan of only the top Other page offer the whole tab); the Gear tab's Mythic Gear (pieces) doesn't count as a seen Other item (it made Design / Polish / Alloy / Mithril show up); items with no known place (Marks of Valor, Gen widget boxes, heroes' own shards) are never offered; Mythic Gear only with the Gear tab read too (its amount is box + gear pieces). The check screen says "Tabs read: Resources · Other …" (`scan_tabs_read`, `scan_tab_*`, `LANG_FILES_VERSION` 56). (7) A Gen's widget box with other things pictured inside (another Gen than the learned S14 box) is still found: a tile further than 20 from every learned picture whose top-left corner has the cream "S" + number (`scanGenLabel`) counts as "Gen N Widgets" (owner, 31 Oct 2026: any box like that is a Gen's widgets, S14 = Gen 14); the three Gen 14 hero widgets (95 each, a rifle, a bunny box and curved swords, opened from the S14 box) are Cara / Dominic / Elif, but which picture is whose isn't known yet (owner to check in the game), so they aren't learned; all screenshots on hand read the same, and an S14 box with another picture pasted inside reads as Gen 14 Widgets. Owner's answer on the other unknown Other-tab tiles (a brown bag, a purple "?" chest, a chest with a gold lock, a spiked silver chest, a red chest after the S14 box that is NOT the Mythic Gear box, a dark red crystal on purple): not tracked, leave them. (8) **Teach Scan on the site** (owner, 31 Oct 2026): tiles Scan doesn't know are kept while reading (`scanItemTiles` `keepUnknown`: picture, picture grid, colour → rarity orange Legendary / purple Epic / blue Rare, the known item before it, the screenshot's tab) and listed at the end of the check screen in a fold "Tiles Scan doesn't know yet (N) — teach them" (`#scanLearn`, `learnHtml` / `learnPicker`, `unknownList`). "What is this?" opens a picker: Hero shards (heroes of the tile's rarity, "Show all heroes"), Hero widgets (mythic heroes) and Backpack items (only items in the Backpack list whose game tab is the screenshot's tab; nothing outside the Backpack). **Only the owner teaches** (owner, 31 Oct 2026): visitors don't get the section (their unknown tiles are counted in the "N tiles skipped" line as before) and all Scans use the owner's taught tiles. The pick reads the amount (`scanTileAmount`), Save stores it and the tile: site_config `scan_learned` = { items: [{ n, f (base64 grid), tab, after }] }, used by everyone. `scanLearnedLoad` (at each Scan) fills `SCAN_LEARNED`; `scanMatchItem` compares them too; `scanTabOrder(tab)` puts a taught item after the item it followed so "set to 0" works for it (heroes' own shards / widgets have no place); `scanKnowsName`. New hero shards / widgets also join the Backpack list (`more.canAdd` / `more.added` now know "<hero> Widgets"). **Game tab** per Backpack item: Admin → Backpack Items sheet "Game tab" (Not set / Resources / Speedup / Gear / Other / Not in the game Backpack), saved as `tab` on the item in site_config `backpack_items`; `bpItemTab(n)` = that, else `bpItemTabBuiltin` (what Scan knows); `scanItemTab` uses it; the list line shows "game tab: …" and "Scan taught N×", and the sheet has "Forget the N picture(s) Scan was taught". Follow-up (owner, 31 Oct 2026, after the owner taught resource bundles as Meat / Gems / Wood): (a) tiles with a size label printed on top ("1K", "10K", "100", "1M", "10": Meat / Wood / Coal / Iron / Gems bundles, Hero XP bottles, Chief Stamina, the fish can) are never listed (`scanTopLabel`: white digit shapes across the top centre; checked on every tile of the owner's screenshots, no real item flagged); (b) items read from the Summary screen (`SCAN_SUMMARY_ROWS`: Gems, Meat, Wood, Coal, Iron, Steel, Chief Stamina, Hero XP, Food) are never offered in "Backpack items", and any taught entry for them is ignored by `scanLearnedLoad` (14 such entries were saved by mistake on 31 Oct 2026 in site_config `scan_learned`; deleted the same day with the owner's OK, backup in owner calc_state `backup_scan_learned_20261031`; undo: copy its `items` back); (c) "Skip" per tile and "Skip all not chosen": saved as `n: "__skip__"` (`SCAN_SKIP`) in `scan_learned`, a match to one is skipped silently; Admin → Backpack Items "Ask about them again" forgets them. T keys `scan_learn_*` (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 59). (d) Taught hero widgets / own hero shards can be set to 0 (owner picked option 2, 31 Oct 2026, after used widgets still showed 285): each taught tile also saves `gap` (how many tiles sit between it and the known item before it); `scanItemTiles` returns `seq` (each tile's item name in reading order, null = not read), kept per screenshot in `shotSeqs`; `spotShown(n)` = a screenshot shows the known `after` item and reaches `gap` + 1 tiles past it. Such an item (not a Gen widget box or General shard) is offered in "set to 0?" only when an Other-tab screenshot shows its spot and the tile isn't there. Lessons saved before this have no `gap`, so they are never offered: teach them again once. Tested: owner's widget screenshot with a Cara Widgets lesson → read 95; the same with that tile painted over → "Cara — Hero widgets · Now 95 → 0"; cut off above that row → not offered; other screenshots read the same as before. Tested on the owner's hero-shard screenshot: 11 unknown tiles, a purple one offers the 9 Epic heroes, picking Jasser reads 3 and saves; the next scan finds "Jasser Shards = 3" by itself; a visitor's check screen has no teach section; all other screenshots read the same. Not learned yet (no screenshot): FC Crate, other heroes' shards, a single hero's widgets, Widget Chest, Mythic General Hero Lucky Box.

## Helper agents (`.claude/agents/`, owner asked 17 Oct 2026)
Six project subagents, instructions only (no secrets; this folder is public like the rest of the repo):
- `tester`: syntax, lint, read-only browser run, old vs new
- `mockup-maker`: phone-size design options as PNG
- `data-checker`: read-only Supabase questions
- `page-auditor`: sweeps all pages for one kind of problem
- `translator`: `lang/*.json` + `LANG_FILES_VERSION`
- `notes-keeper`: keeps this file right
Hand jobs to them to run in parallel; they never push or write data.

## Testing approach used so far
The cloud environment allows `hzourauekkiorojrhpko.supabase.co` and `www.wosrst.com`. It can't reach `cdn.jsdelivr.net`, so tests serve a local copy of `@supabase/supabase-js@2.117.3`.
1. Run `node --check` on app.js (and admin.js, sw.js). Run ESLint for unused variables and real-bug rules.
2. Use Playwright with the preinstalled Chromium (`executablePath: '/opt/pw-browsers/chromium'`), real data, **read-only**: mock `/auth/v1/` to return a session whose token is the public anon key, and block every non-GET request to Supabase.
3. Compare old (`origin/main`) vs new: page HTML across all tabs at 390 px and 1280 px, plus before/after timings with CPU throttling ×4 and +150 ms per request.

## Images
- Large repo icons were optimized (resized to 2–3× display size, palette PNG via libimagequant) with **file names and formats kept**, so database links keep working.
- `skins_catalog.image_url` for 88 gallery images points to `icons/skins/opt/<file>`. The originals stay in Storage `skin-images` as the backup. Revert:
  ```sql
  update skins_catalog
  set image_url = 'https://hzourauekkiorojrhpko.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/skin-images/' || split_part(image_url, '/', -1)
  where image_url like 'icons/skins/opt/%';
  ```
- Hero portraits (`icons/heroes/`, 17 Oct 2026): the 36 PNG / JPG over 200 px were resized to 200 × 200 like the `.webp` ones (names and formats kept; folder 2.49 MB → 1.0 MB; biggest display ~110 px in the roster picker).
- New images uploaded through Admin still go to Storage. `cropUploadImage` crops them first (see-through borders off, keeping 99% of the picture, max 384 px; JPEGs untouched), so they match the others.
- Gallery/Islands/Experts/Facility pictures in the repo are cropped the same way (Oct 2026). The space around them is set in CSS: `.gallery-card .thumb.gallery-thumb` (Cosmetics, 17%; `gallery-thumb-big` 6% for the categories in `GALLERY_BIG_THUMB`: City Skin, Teleport Skin, Private Chat, Rally; since 17 Oct 2026 the Rules `galleryBig` list / ⋯ Category "Big pictures" switch, built-in list as default; since 17 Nov 2026 only the switch: `galleryBig` is off the Rules screens (`ADMIN_RULE_OFF`, `admRulesShown`, `admRulesChangedN`: kept in the registry, its saved value still applies, the switch saves through it)), `.island-thumb` (Islands, 5%), `.island-thumb.facility-thumb` (Facility, 10%), and the inline padding on the Experts portraits.

## Site-wide redesign (merged 16 Oct 2026, PR #36)
Built on branch `claude/youthful-einstein-n9eoau`, now live. What it contains:
- T12 redesign: two columns (Settings, "Amounts from your Backpack" tiles, Trade items and Pack Cost cards on the side; "Total research resources needed" squares with the research-time bar, troop tabs and folding node sections in the main column); phones stack, settings on one line. FC→FC Shard trade 10→13, up to 500 a week, counted in totals; trade card shows only RFC/FCS still short (exact amounts); Pack Cost cards without "Full amount" or $.
- Same layout on T11 Helios, Research Center, Chief Gear, Charms, Heroes Gears, Pets Level, Pet Refine, Experts Level, Experts Research.
- Page title on top; "Max everything" at the top right (T12, T11, Research Center, Pets).
- Stats as boxes with an of-max bar; groups behind tabs (troops on Heroes Gears, Chief Gear, Charms, Pet Refine, Alliance Tech, Dashboard; Troops/Foundry/SvS/Frostfire/Tundra on Experts Level; All troops + troops on Experts Research).
- Pets/Experts: ‹ 1/15 › arrows replaced by a picker strip (expert pictures; pets have no pictures, so a coloured letter).
- Experts Level owned amounts as tap-to-edit tiles; expert sigils as picture tiles (Experts Level and Research).
- "Set all" shows the chosen level when all pieces match (Chief Gear, Charms; new Set all row on Heroes Gears).
- Extra Bonus pages: titles on VIP and Heroes Special Skills; VIP, Tundra Album, Collection Gallery use the new stat boxes; Alliance Tech stats as troop tabs.
- Every other page: `.panel` has the same card look, bigger page titles.
- Round 2 (15 Oct 2026): each gear piece / charm shows its own stat now → target (`plxPieceStatHtml`, filled in the update*CurrentStats functions); stats in one row (`plxStatBoxesHtml(list, true)`) on Heroes Gears, VIP, Collection Gallery, Tundra Album, Alliance Tech; pet pictures from whiteoutsurvival.wiki in `icons/pets/<slug>.webp` (`petImage`, Abyssal Shelldragon has none yet → letter); Pet Refine advanced calculator in a pop-up (`refineAdvOpen`, closed by `render()`); Research Center targets (calc_state `research_center_target`, missing = max); Research Lab redesigned (replaced 4 Nov 2026, see the redesign round; was: summary squares, tappable schedule cards with Best value / Fastest, side card for mode + FC⇄FCS, Lab tiers folded); Alliance Tech sections with − / + level nodes saved through `patchSkinQueued`; Tundra Album / Collection Gallery level chips (`plxChipRowHtml`) with a +0% choice.
- Round 3: Tundra Album / Collection Gallery back to dropdowns, side by side in one row (`plxSelectHtml`, +0% kept). Owner picked Dashboard A (three troop cards with bars; tap a stat → same breakdown pop-up, now via `breakdownFor(troop, stat)`; roster unchanged and moved under them; "Biggest gains left" removed at the owner's request; breakdown pop-up redesigned: summary box with bar, pages with something left first as bar rows, maxed ones folded), Budgets B (side: settings + page list with each page's cost; main: total, plan cards, packs-to-buy list open, then per-page details) and Packs B (each table a summary card, Edit opens the full editor in place; open tables kept in `PACKS_OPEN` for the visit; two cards a row on phones; category buttons in one sideways-scrolling row `.pk-cat-row`; the visitor "you can change the amounts" note sits inside each opened editor). `renderCategorySwitcher` (Backpack, Cosmetics, Islands and now Packs) = ☰ full-list menu + one row of buttons that scrolls sideways (`.pk-cat-row`); takes names or { key, label, count } items; the chosen button is scrolled into view; Backpack and Packs tabs show counts. Page pop-ups (breakdown, refine calculator) close on page change.
- Pack Cost "Shared with Budgets" on the card pages now looks like "This page only" (owner, 16 Oct 2026): plan cards (this page's share + "All pages" → Budgets) and one folding row per pack with this page's part of the price (`sharedSections && cards` in `renderPackCostBlock`).
- Admin redesign (owner, 16 Oct 2026; plan Steps 1–7: 1 health check done, 2 layout done, next 3 Menu & pages, 4 Rules & Timeline editor (code constants → site_config with code values as defaults, "Reset to default"), 5 Texts (T overrides en/ar/ko), 6 fill gaps from the health check, 7 Backup / Restore). Layout: `ADMIN_GROUPS` + `ADMIN_TABLES` (group, icon, desc, words for search); PC = sidebar (`.adm-side`) + section on the right; phones (<900px) = home cards (`.adm-cards`), a section opens full screen with a ‹ back button; `ADMIN_ACTIVE_TABLE` null = home; search filters sidebar and cards (Enter opens the first match). Sub-tabs are chips (`.adm-chips` / `.adm-chip`). Sections draw one at a time (`ADMIN_DRAWING`, `drawAdminSection`) so a slow one can't draw or wire buttons over the next; leaving Admin calls `dropKeptGameData()` (the Admin tab is `adminpage`).
- Admin Step 3 done: "Menu & pages" (`renderAdminMenu`): rename groups / pages (EN + AR / KO), order (drag ⋮⋮), move a page to another group, add / delete (empty, custom) groups, page notes (EN/AR/KO, shown in `.page-note` under the page title by `render()` via `pageNoteFor`). Saved in site_config `menu_layout` = { groups: [{ key, label }], pages: { tab: { group, label } }, order: [tabs], notes: { tab: {en,ar,ko} } } (only changes from the built-in menu are stored); `applyMenuLayout()` runs at start (after `ensureSiteConfig`) and after saving (`afterMenuChange`), rebuilding the menu from `menuDefaults()` (the built-in HTML menu) + the saved layout; built-in labels kept in `NAV_GROUP_LABELS_DEFAULT` / `NAV_PAGE_LABELS_DEFAULT`. "Reset to default" saves {}. Hiding pages stays in Visibility.

Each rule: Save (tried first, not saved if it fails) / Reset to built-in. New unlock rows only add the 🔒 note on their page until a feature reads them.

Missing texts show in English. Arabic / Korean were moved out of the code by script (code 1,562 KB → 1,401 KB); every AR / KO page compared before / after: identical (58 page views). Admin → Languages & Texts (`renderAdminLanguages`, `renderAdminTexts`, `openTextUpload`): add / hide / remove languages, right-to-left, texts editor (search, Not translated / Changed filters, 40 a page, ↺ back to the file), CSV download (key, English, translation; UTF-8 BOM) and upload with a check screen ({placeholders} must match; only fills or changes texts).

The old "Add a row" forms are hidden (`addForm`) once a table has a row. Other "Add …" panels (new pet, new hero …) fold into "+ Add …" buttons (`foldAdminAddPanels`, observer in `drawAdminSection`).

Backup = one JSON file (`kind: "admin-backup"`, ~1.2 MB). Restore: compares the file with today's data per table (changed / deleted since / added since), the admin ticks parts, optional "also delete rows added since"; downloads `wosrst-before-restore-….json` first, upserts (`on_conflict` = the table's key) parents first, deletes children first, then reloads. Admin plan Steps 1–7 all done.

- Pet Refine advanced calculator pop-up redesigned (`.rfa`): totals on top, one row per stage (colour dot, − value +), Adjustment row, buttons at the bottom; still a pop-up.
- Budgets total = design A (owner, 16 Oct 2026): one card with the total and "Done <date>", a 3-way switch Fastest / By target / Cheapest (`.bud-seg`, `data-budgetview`, price + short date each), then one line per part (`lineHtml`, `.bud-line` = coloured bar, pages, price, "N packs ▸", tap opens the packs): "By <target>" (green) + "After your target" (red, uses `BUDGET_SPLIT_LATE` = cheapest; its own Fastest / Cheapest cards were removed), or one "Packs to buy" line when there is no split.
- Shared Backpack amounts in Budgets (owner, 16 Oct 2026): pages using the same item from the same owned amount (Experts Level + Experts Research: Books, Sigils; Research Center / T11 / T12: resources, Steel, FC Shards) each took the full amount off their own need, so two ticked pages counted the Backpack twice. `budgetSharedOwnedExtra(pages)` adds what's still short over the pages together (minus what they already ask for) to the page needing the item most; used in `budgetCombinedInput` (plan, also My Purchases suggestions; a page can join the plan through it) and in the "Materials still needed" total. Owned amounts that differ between pages (typed per page) are left alone. Test: Level 30,000 + Research 20,000 Books with 25,000 owned → 25,000 to buy (was 5,000).
- Budgets page list: each page has its own tick (owner, 16 Oct 2026; it used to switch the whole `PACK_PAGE_GROUPS` group: Experts Level + Experts Research, the three Research pages).
- Pack cost has no "Still needed / Full amount" switch any more (owner, 16 Oct 2026; it was admin-only and still showed on the older Pack cost box, e.g. Experts): `PACK_COST_MODE` is always "still".
- Pack Cost card: "Shared with Budgets" / "This page only" switch is now for everyone, not just the owner (opens on "This page only"; the shared plan is only worked out when tapped).
- Round 4: menu group "My Profile" (My Purchases, Budgets, Backpack, My packs; Overview = Dashboard + Packs) — later changed (owner, 16 Oct 2026): Overview = Dashboard + Backpack; group "Marketplace / Budgeting" (`data-group="Marketplace"`) = Budgets, Packs, My packs, My Purchases (owner's order). New page **My Purchases** (`renderPurchasesPage`, tab `purchases`, visitor access follows `budgets`): packs bought / will buy saved in calc_state `pack_purchases` = { list: [{id, table, row, count, date, status: "planned"|"bought"}], newGen: {last, gen} }. Planned packs (date today or later) count as coming items on the planner pages and Budgets (`packActiveCardSupply`); planned or recently bought packs use up that pack's limit (`packPurchaseUse` at the end of `packGroupsFor`; bought counts while inside its reset, or always for one-time packs). New Gen Heroes tables: everyone sets each pack's start date (saved in `my_pack_dates`), plus "last new heroes" date + Gen → next appearances and the calendar show "Gen N" (`packNewGenLabel`). "My pack dates" box moved from Packs to this page. Packs cards show a "Used for (N) ▸" button (`details.pk-sum-used`, `packUsedForNames`) that opens the page names as small chips (owner, 16 Oct 2026). Budgets total shows a "My Purchases: N coming packs counted" button. The Pack calendar is shown to visitors since 16 Oct 2026 (see data change below).
- Dashboard roster is always shown (owner, 16 Oct 2026: closed, the stats stretched to fill the phone screen); the old open/close arrow and localStorage `dashboard_roster_collapsed` are gone.
- Dashboard roster = design C: three troop cards (`.rc-card`) sitting on the page, no outer box (`.rc-wrap`), only the % (line 1 ATK & DEF from stars, line 2 LETH & HP from the widget), stars unchanged (pieces still tappable) plus − / + buttons that move one shard (`data-starstep`, 0–30 steps), short "Widget N" dropdown.
- Hero shards: `heroShardPlan` (roster heroes' stars from `player_heroes`, cost per step from `hero_gen_star_stats.shards`, fallback `HERO_SHARD_CHART`; own "<hero> Shards" first, then the rarity's General Hero Shard shared in troop order). Shown as "🧩 N short" on the Dashboard roster cards, with a green "can reach 4★ 1/6 now" line under it when the shards you have reach further (`rc_sh_reach`). The Backpack's "Roster heroes: shards & widgets needed" card was removed (owner, 16 Oct 2026: it only repeated the roster cards and Budgets).
- Widgets: mythic heroes in the Backpack get a second tile "<hero> Widgets" (⚙ Widget badge). `heroShardPlan` also works out widgets from the roster widget level to 10 with `WIDGET_LEVEL_COST` 5, 10 … 50 (275 total; confirmed by the owner) and `WIDGET_PACK` $4.99 for 5 (no longer shown); the "⚙ N short · $" on the roster cards now comes from the Budgets planner (`heroWidgetCost` → `budgetForPage("hero_shards", …)` with that hero's widget need, "*" = all heroes; `widgetCostSpan` placeholders filled by `fillWidgetCosts`, kept in `HERO_WCOST`, cleared by `budgetInvalidate`).
- My Purchases "Suggested packs (from Budgets)": `budgetSuggestedPlan()` (reuses `BUDGET_DATA.combined.chosen` when Budgets is ready, else works out the same combined plan; kept in `PUR_SUGGEST`, cleared by `budgetInvalidate`). Plan rows now carry `row` (offer row) so "+ Will buy" adds them as planned purchases; `packPlanSchedule(rows)` spreads each row's count over the pack's appearances (`packResetOffsets`, perCycle each; stacked cards: `atOnce` first, then one per card length; rows of one pack sharing its limit share each appearance; rows carry `window`), each card shows "8 dates · Now → Nov 30", Buy now / Later split on top; "+ Will buy" opens a pop-up (`purSugModal`) listing every date with a tick box (all ticked, Pick all / Clear, total) and adds one line per ticked date. Suggestions show only the pack name (`purPackName` drops the "Category · " part); choice purchases also save `choice.name` so Coming / Bought cards show the same name. "Pick from table" in Add a pack (`purTablePopup`) shows the pack's table in a pop-up: a slim ▲ n ▼ per row (since 21 Nov 2026, see "My Purchases pickers and Other prices"), or for buy-in-order packs a tick on the last tier (earlier tiers included); packs with options get dropdowns in each price row: N for "pick N items" packs (Mix & Match; item · amount), one for option sets (Myriad Bazaar, Benefit Cards, Dawn of Wisdom: the set's tables, "tag · first items") and for column-option packs; free-step sets (Benefit Cards) use ▲ / ▼, the others tick up to a tier; the name is the planner's pack name without its option list; the tick / − + column is first and sticky; Add saves all picks with the form's date and status (choice packs as `choice`). The suggestions list shows 3 packs and scrolls inside the box (`.pur-sug-list`, height set from the 4th card). Coming / Bought are grouped per pack (same table and tier): one card with total count and price, "Dates" folds open the date rows (✓ bought / ↺ back / ×, open cards kept in `PUR_OPEN`), "All bought" and × (asks first when several dates); every pack gets the button: plan rows carry `tables`, and packs where you choose what you get (option / pick / column-option) save the choice on the purchase (`choice: { label, price, gives }`, read by `purchaseGives` / `purchasePrice`; grouped by table + choice label). In "Add a pack", packs with options have no long Choice dropdown any more (owner's choice): the Choice field is a "Choose in the pack table" button that opens `purTablePopup` (its Add saves); plain packs keep the Tier dropdown + Add, with "Pick from table" as an extra. Dawn Market and Dawn of Wisdom stay linked sets where each tier can be a different table (owner confirmed this matches the game). In the Add a pack list (the P4 picker window since 21 Nov 2026), a linked set of option tables is one entry named after the pack (Dawn Market, Dawn of Wisdom, Myriad Bazaar, Benefit Card; `setLead` / `setName`), the table pop-up picks the table per tier. Every option dropdown in the table pop-up starts empty ("— choose —"); Add stays off until each ticked / counted row is chosen. "My packs" (visitor `my-` tables and the owner's "My packs" group) with no "Used for" ticked count for every page (`packTableServesPage`; shown as "All pages (automatic)"), so new ones are in Budgets and in the My Purchases list right away. `recordBudgetNeeds` compares needs by item name (the saved copy comes back from the database with keys in another order), so opening a page no longer clears Budgets / the suggestions when nothing changed. The plan already counts the page's purchases, so the list shrinks as packs are added.
- Suggested packs by week = design C (owner, 16 Oct 2026): week tabs (`.pur-wk-chips`, `data-purwk`, chosen week in `PUR_SUG_WEEK` for the visit): This week (to Sunday), Next week, Week 3 …, Target week (the week of the Budgets target; no target = 8 weeks), After target. Each tab shows that week's price and dates; the list shows only that week's buys of each pack. "+ Will buy" opens the date pop-up for that week only; "+ Will buy all of <week>" (`data-purwkall`) adds every date of that week at once. Top squares: This week / Until target.
