- New Gen Heroes follow the person's "last new heroes" date (My Purchases, `PACK_PURCHASES.newGen.last`): `packNewGenAnchor` sets Hero Ascension Training to that day and Exclusive Gear Forge 28 days later (`packNewGenOffset`: title with "forge", else the gap between the admin's own dates), before any own date or the admin's date. With the date set, the per-pack start inputs are replaced by "Comes with / 4 weeks after the new heroes". The My Purchases New Gen card lists one line per event (owner, 16 Oct 2026): tables sharing the name before ":" (e.g. "Hero Ascension Training" and "…: Value Hero Pack") show once under that name (`ngShown` / `ngBase`), and an own start date moves all of them (`ngSame`).
- Game events (`GAME_EVENTS`): SvS (seen 5–10 Oct 2026) and KOI (seen 21–26 Sep 2026), each every 28 days for 6 days, Lucky Wheel (hero wheel) day 2–4 (`heroWheels`). New generations (`newGenStarts`): from the base day (person's "last new heroes", else the admin's Training date, `newGenBaseDay`), new heroes come on the first Monday when at least 80 days, 5 Lucky Wheels (SvS or KOI) and 3 SvS wheels have all passed (owner's rule, `NEW_GEN_MIN_DAYS` / `NEW_GEN_WHEELS` / `NEW_GEN_SVS`; memoised in `NEW_GEN_MEMO`): Sep 28 → Dec 21 → Mar 15 → Jun 7 … (84 days). The SvS / KOI dates are the site's own (`GAME_EVENTS`), not shown as inputs (owner: "for us to calculate"); the New Gen card only asks for the last new heroes date and its Gen. `newGenAppearances` / `packNewGenAnchor` place Hero Ascension Training on each start and Exclusive Gear Forge 28 days later; Gen labels count generations. The New Gen tables' reset is the gap between the next two generations (`newGenGapDays`) in `packLinkForBase`, so the planner matches. Calendar: "Events" chip shows SvS / KOI bars, 🎡 Lucky Wheel (the hero wheel, day 2–4 of each event) and ⭐ new heroes days; New Gen bars (`keep`) never go to the all-week strip.
- State age (server age): per person in calc_state `state_age` = { day, on } (`ensureStateAge`, `stateAgeDay` = day + days since `on`, `saveStateAge` clears plan caches). Dashboard top card (`stateAgeCardHtml`: day, current Gen, next Gen date, next Lucky Wheel; ✎ / Set opens `openStateAgeModal`: day number or opened date + the wiki's 3 ways). Gen by state day from `STATE_GEN_DAYS` (Discord timeline, Gen 1–18); `stateGenInfo` gives the Gen and its start (snapped to that week's Monday). `newGenBaseDay` = own "last new heroes" date > state age > admin date; `newGenGen` gives the Gen number. My Purchases New Gen card shows "From your state age …" with "Know the exact date? Set it yourself" and "Use my state age instead".
- Unlocks by state age (`STATE_UNLOCKS`: T11 day 220, T12 700, Legendary Chief Gear 180 (note only), pets and experts by day from the Discord timeline): `unlockedFor` (pet / expert tick boxes) and `ensureResearchPageCountedData` (T11 / T12 "Count toward Dashboard", now with `manual` per box) start unticked for what isn't open yet when the person never set that box; a box they tick / untick keeps their choice. `render()` adds a "🔒 … opens around day N — in about D days" note under the page title (`stateLockNotesHtml`) on T11, T12, Chief Gear, Pets, Pet Refine, Experts pages. Since 20 Nov 2026 there are no ticks: the state age alone decides (old saved choices are ignored), see "Step 76".
- State age pop-up: three ways — Monument (Beast Hunting "Goal Met" date − 6 days, or Kindling Embers − 3; default when nothing is set), day number, or the date it opened; a "Moved or merged states?" tip (use the current state's Monument, not VIP days). Optional "Your own days played" (VIP login days) saved as `own` / `ownOn` in `state_age` (`ownDaysPlayed`), shown on the Dashboard card as "you: N days". Both count up daily by themselves.

A page's own "This page only" Pack cost still uses every pack.

- Pack table editor = A1 + settings S1 (owner, 17 Oct 2026). A1: each column header is the item's picture (`pkHeadHtml`, `pkColItem`, `pkItemIcon`; price "$" and Gems stay fixed, Gems with a small ×); the owner taps a picture to open the item list sheet (`pkOpenItemSheet`: search, pictures beside names; it sets the hidden real dropdown `.pk-hd-ctl select[data-pt-colpick]` and fires its change, so the old handler does the work). In "⇅ Arrange" mode the columns are dragged by a handle (`dragSort`, was ◀ ▶) (`PK_ARRANGE`, `.pk-arrange .pk-arr`). The toolbar is the title, Arrange and a "⋯" menu (`.pk-more` / `.pk-menu`: Group, Fill from Row 1 switch, Column + −, Row + −, Restore, Duplicate, Delete table; same data-pt-* buttons; visitors get neither). Totals of 100,000+ show short (444K, 1.11M, `pkShortNum`, full number in the title). Closed cards show what the table gives as picture chips with totals (`.pk-chip`). S1 (`packLinkEditorFullHtml`): summary "Pack settings · Used for (N)" (`packLinkSummaryText`), bundle notes as small chips with ⓘ, then sections (`.bset-sec`): Used for (page chips `.pks-chip`, amber "none picked" tag `data-pknonetag`), Columns (the old item-for-column dropdowns `data-packcol`), Buying (3-way segment `.pks-seg` of the `data-pkhow` radios; picks stepper `data-pksstep`, "Prices in order" switch), Schedule (Comes back / Next on / Lasts / Buys per reset rows; `.pks-opt` rows hide with their input; trigger and own-card blocks unchanged), Visitors (switches), More (Daily rewards, Same pack as folding rows). Long explanations are behind ⓘ (`data-pksinfo` → `data-pksinfotext`, one delegated handler on the list). Every input keeps its data attribute, so the save code is unchanged. The old "⚙ Widget" tile badge is gone. All mythic heroes' widgets are ONE "Widgets (N)" box in the Backpack (owner, 16 Oct 2026). Rows are a whole Gen ("Gen 14 widgets", saved as "Gen 14 Widgets", usable for any hero of that Gen; the roster heroes' Gens are there by default) or one hero ("<hero> Widgets", this hero only). The add menu is grouped by Gen (the Gen row first, then each hero). calc_state `backpack_widgets` = { hide, add, gens, genHide } (`openWidgetsBox`, `widgetRows`, `rowKey`). `heroShardPlan` uses a hero's own widgets first, then its Gen's widgets (`genWPool`, shared in troop order). Dashboard roster / star changes now call `budgetInvalidate` (Budgets kept the old roster before). Hero shard tiles show only the heroes added by hand ("+ Mythic heroes"); the Widgets box also lists the roster heroes.
- Packs page note at the top (`.pk-zone-note`, key `pk_zone_note`, owner's request): pack contents are from states in the 600s; other states can differ with state age; open a pack with Edit to change its amounts.
- 4-week packs (owner, 16 Oct 2026: "30 days means 4 weeks"): the "Monthly" reset preset is now 28 days, shown as "Every 4 weeks" (`PACK_RESET_PRESETS`; the old calendar-month logic still runs for any 30 left in data); New Gen windows default to 28. Data (live, owner calc_state `packs_links`): Myriad Bazzar ×2, Wishfull and the "Wishful type" template reset 30 → 28; Hero Ascension Training and Exclusive Gear Forge window 30 → 28. Undo: set those back to 30.
- Bazaar date (owner, 16 Oct 2026): "My pack dates" for the Bazzar group is on the Packs page above Pack prices (`#packsMyDates`, `onlyGroups`; the `PACK_DATES_ON_PACKS_PAGE` list and its Rules entry "Pack groups dated on the Packs page" (`packDatesGroups`) were removed 17 Nov 2026: nothing read them any more), and left out of My Purchases (`skipGroups`); "Reset all" only clears the packs in that box. `packLinkForBase` returns `newGen` so `packIsNewGen` still works on links after the "Heroes packs" rename (that was broken for a few commits: New Gen packs showed in My pack dates). One-time windows (reset ≥ `PACK_ONE_TIME_DAYS`) read "One time" in the plan lists.
- "My pack dates" is editable for the admin too (owner, 16 Oct 2026; was a locked "Visitor preview"): weekly cards save the admin's own `my_pack_dates` / `my_cards_active` (same as each pack's settings); a dated pack (Bazaar) saves the pack's `resetDay` in `packs_links` for the whole group (also visitors' default). "Open settings" stays.
- Purchases and pack limits (owner, 16 Oct 2026): `packPurchaseUse` counts a purchase only for the group of the row it bought (each price row of a choice pack like the Benefit Card is its own group; ladders take any row), and also records `usedNow` = buys made in the running copy (`packCurrentCycleStart`); plan rows carry `usedNow` / `usedAt` ({ appearance offset: buys }, also for "Will buy" on a later appearance) and `packPlanSchedule` leaves that much less of each appearance, so a pack already bought this week is suggested from next week and a planned week is skipped. Packs bought once a day while they run (buys ≤ window, e.g. Hall of Heroes 3 over Sun–Tue) get one date per day. Audit 16 Oct 2026 with the owner's real data: Budgets total = My Purchases suggested total, every purchase on its own row, planned widget packs taken off the widget need. Pack "Used for" says "Hero shards" / "Hero widgets" from what a hero pack gives (`packHeroUseLabel`; both → "Hero shards & widgets").
- Widgets in Budgets (owner, 16 Oct 2026): a Gen's widgets ("Gen 14 Widgets") are no longer given out in troop order before planning: `heroShardBudget` owns them as their own item with a trade to each hero of that Gen, and the widget items are listed lowest widget level first (most still to go), so shared widgets (Gen widgets, Mystery Points, Widget Chests) go to that hero and the others keep their cheap Forge packs. The roster cards still share them in troop order (owner: keep the roster as it is). Buy-in-order packs bought / planned only part way keep their later steps on offer for that run (`packPurchaseUse` adds a one-off group per run, `from` on its offers / plan rows; "Will buy" saves `from`, read by `packPurchaseGives` / `packPurchasePrice`; the card says "Tier 5 ($19.99)"). Not for option / choice packs (Bazaar). Owner's data: total $309.48 → $294.50 (Elif's Forge $19.99 step instead of extra Mystery Badge / Widget Chest packs).
- My Purchases (owner, 16 / 17 Oct 2026; the folds below were replaced by A2 + O1 on 4 Nov 2026, the reload restore stays): "Add a pack", "New Gen Heroes", "Coming (will buy)" and "Bought" fold open / closed (`<details data-purlist>`, kept in localStorage `pur_list_a` / `pur_list_n` / `pur_list_c` / `pur_list_b`). Suggested packs show short date lines (`whenShort`: days in a row "Dec 7 → Feb 21 · every day (77 days)", evenly spaced "every 4 weeks (3×)", else first → last · N dates). Phones may reload a page left in the background: the page reopens on the same tab (`last_tab`), scrolled where it was (sessionStorage `scroll_pos`, saved on visibilitychange / pagehide, restored once the page is tall enough), with the Add a pack form (`pur_form`) and an open pack table pop-up with its picks (`pur_tbl_draft`, saved on every draw, cleared on Close / Add; `purTablePopup` `opts.restore`).
- Visitors can change a row's Max (how many times it can be bought) only on rows that have one; kept in their browser like their amount edits (localStorage `pack_my_rowmax`, `packMyRowMax` / `packRowMaxWithMine` inside `packLinkForBase`); the owner's Max handler skips visitors. "My pack dates" shows dated packs of one group that move together as one row ("Myriad Bazzar (2 tables)").
- Owner data change (live, 16 Oct 2026, owner's request): both Myriad Bazzar tables (`506958c7-…`, `2f6e5479-…`) got `visitorDate: true` in owner calc_state `packs_links`, so everyone sets the day the Bazaar comes to their state (My Purchases → My pack dates) and it repeats every 30 days from there. Undo: remove `visitorDate` from those two entries (or untick "Visitors can set their own date").
- Owner data change (live, 16 Oct 2026, owner's request after checking the in-game Regular Packs screenshots): renamed `pack_tables` to the game's names — `f254bab8-…` "Pet Food Pack" → "Pet Serum Pack" and `5e41b8ca-…` "Pet Serum Pack" → "Pet Food Pack" (the names were swapped: the first gives Serum, the second Food), `5fbcbf08-…` "Expert Gift Pack" → "Expert Gift Set", `acabdaa8-…` "Experts Mark Pack" → "Expert Mark Pack", `c4bad4ed-…` "Mithril" → "Gear Empowerment". Added two Regular Packs (owner's `packs_links` entries: weekly, resetDay 2026-09-14): "Refined Fire Crystal Pack" (`941e8b57-…`, $4.99 … $99.99 ladder like every Regular Pack (owner: all have the same ×2 / ×4 / ×10 / ×20 steps, owner, later: these two can't repeat the last step, so no `rowMax`): $4.99 = 1 Refined FC, 10 FC Shard, 8,000 Steel, 4 × Speedup 1h + 48 × Speedup 5m, 2,500 Gems; Used for T11 / T12; it makes a very fast (and costly) T12 plan possible) and "Charm Craftsman Pack" (`8309b3f9-…`, the same ladder: $4.99 = 18 Charm Guides, 6 Charm Designs, 2,500 Gems, ×2 / ×4 / ×10 / ×20 up to $99.99, last step once; Used for Charms; a Google answer the owner sent said 6 Designs + 2 Guides, but the owner's game screenshot shows 18 Guides + 6 Designs, kept). Expert Gift Set last step 5 → 10 times (`rowMax` 4:10, owner; undo: back to 5). Checked with an exact solver: the other in-game Regular Packs (FC, FC Shard, Steel, Tech, Charm Design, Chief Gear Tool / Enhancement, Mastery Forging, Hero Gear, Wild Mark) never lowered a plan's price or date, so they were not added. Undo: rename back, delete the two rows and their `packs_links` entries.
- Owner data change (live, 16 Oct 2026, owner's request "show calendar for all"): `ui_visibility` `page:packcalendar` hidden true → false, so visitors with Packs access see the 📅 calendar button (undo: set `hidden` back to true, or the owner's hide button).
- Dashboard fits one phone screen (owner, 16 Oct 2026; tested at 416 × 570): phone-only CSS under `.dash-fit` (max-width 700px): page title hidden, state card one short block (no icon / label, chips in one swipeable row), smaller roster cards (36px picture, 17px stars, the two % on one line via `.rc-pct span`), stat rows with "+N%" on the value line, "Click for full breakdown" hidden. Taller phones: `fitDashToScreen()` (after drawing, on resize, after roster redraws) sets `--sx` on `.dash-fit` so the roster pictures / stars and the stat rows grow to fill the screen down to the bottom (no empty gap; footer below). PC unchanged.
- Dashboard on narrow phones (under 440 px, e.g. iPhone 15 at 393; owner, 3 Nov 2026: "readable for all sizes and not too cramped"): each stat's "+N%" has its own line under the number (it ran into the number and out of the 104 px column), and no Dashboard text under 11 px ("/ max", "+N%", troop headers, roster card troop / Gen / shard / % lines, state chips, quick row names; two labels carry an inline 10 px, overridden with !important); the roster shard line wraps instead of being cut ("⚙ 95 short · QAR 242"). `fitDashToScreen` still grows the page to fill the screen. 416 × 575: the page is 70 px taller than before.
- Dashboard: the owner-only "AVG Attack / Defense / Lethality / Health bonus" boxes and the owner-only "TOTAL" table were removed (owner, 16 Oct 2026); the troop stats breakdown pop-up still shows every source.
- Dashboard stats in one row under the roster (owner picked design A, 16 Oct 2026; a Cards / One row switch was tried and dropped): `.dash-troops.cols` = three narrow columns also on phones, short ATK/DEF/LETH/HP with "/ max" next to the label, bar and "+N%" left. `statsHtml()` draws it; one `sections.onclick` handles the breakdown taps.
- State-age ticks reach Budgets too: T11 / T12 with "Count toward Dashboard" off are shown in Budgets as not counted (no plan; `budgetForPage` → `notCounted`, the page toggles call `budgetInvalidate`); Chief Gear needs / plan lines stop at `chiefGearCapIdx()` (Legendary off → Mythic T2), and `loadChiefGearRows` waits for the state age; Pets / Pet Refine / Experts / Experts Research saved needs carry `ticks` (`budgetStateSig`: which state-age unlocks were open) and Budgets asks to open the page again when that changed (`stale`). Dashboard and breakdown already used the same ticks / cap. (Since 20 Nov 2026 T11 / T12 have no Count box: not open in the state = not counted, see "Step 76".)
- Pack calendar redesign (replaced by C2 on 4 Nov 2026): items are collected first (packs, SvS / KOI, Lucky Wheels, ⭐ new heroes with Gen and state day, 🔓 state-age unlocks), then shown as a List (by week, "now" / "this week" badges; default on phones) or Month grid (default on PC, no forced width there; milestones in a gold strip with full text per week). Month name with ‹ › and Today, Month / List switch (`packCalView`, kept in localStorage `packcal_view`), filter chips in one swipeable row (`.pc-chips`).
- Text fits its boxes (owner, 16 Oct 2026): "Total resources needed" values shrink with the square (`container-type:inline-size`, `13cqi`) and long times wrap at spaces; Cosmetics names wrap to 2 lines instead of "…"; Dashboard cards a little tighter under 380 px. Checked with a script that flags text sticking out of any bordered / coloured box on every page (owner + visitor, 360 / 390 / 416 / 600 / 1280 px, Arabic, Korean): none left (320 px phones still 3–8 px over on the Dashboard stats). Scan shows the reader's download % and gives up with an error after 2 minutes without progress (`SCAN_PROGRESS`, stall timer in `scanWorker`). Its two item pictures (`scanMarkData` / `scanItemData`) load with `crossOrigin = "anonymous"` and catch errors: the raw.githack.com preview hands them over from another address, and without CORS the canvas was blocked and the error was lost inside `onload`, so Scan waited forever on the preview (live site unaffected). The box shows "reader ready ✓ · item pictures N/2" while waiting; each picture gives up after 45 s. Scan window examples (owner picked A, 16 Oct 2026): each step has a small example picture (`.scan-ex`, tap → full size `.scan-ex-view`, tap to close): `icons/scan-ex-summary.jpg` / `-speedup.jpg` / `-items.jpg`, cut from the owner's screenshots with every amount blurred.
- Budgets Settings box = design A (owner, 17 Oct 2026): a settings list, label left / control right, equal rows with thin lines (`.bset-row`, `.bset-l` with a small line under it e.g. "49 days left", `.bset-c`); My budget is a section (`.bset-sec`) with an amber "🔒 Admin only" tag (full note on hover, short "Visitors get My budget…" line at the bottom), rows Amount (number, currency and the week · month switch on one line, owner) / "Keep what I don't spend" as a switch (`.bset-sw`, still the `[data-spendcarry]` checkbox). Currency dropdown via `currencySelectHtml` (the old `currencyPickerHtml` had no other user and was removed). Phone inputs 16 px (no iPhone zoom; the date / number boxes only reached 16 px on 17 Oct 2026, when the phone rule named `input[type=date]` / `input[type=number]`).
- My budget (spending limit, owner picked C + D, 17 Oct 2026): Budgets Settings has "My budget" (amount in the shown currency, a week / a month, "Keep what I don't spend for next time"), saved in calc_state `budget_spend` = { amount, cur, per, carry } (`BUDGET_SPEND`, `ensureBudgetSpend`, `spendSettings`, `spendAmountLocal` converts if the currency changed). Admin only unless Admin → Visibility → Visitor access "spendbudget" is ticked (`spendFeatureOn` = `visitorCan("spendbudget")`). With an amount set, the planner worker also returns `budgetCands` (plans for the fastest, target, cheapest and earliest + 14 / 28 / 56 / 84 / 112 / 168 / 224 / 280 days, quick solves; `payload.spendCands`, so the plan fingerprint changes only when the box is first used); the page buys each candidate under the limit (`budgetSpendSimulate`: each pack on its sale days from `packRowSlots`, the one with the fewest chances left first; purchases in My Purchases count as spending, bought ones only in the current week / month, `budgetSpendFixed`) and keeps the one that finishes soonest, then cheapest (`budgetSpendBest`, memo per plan + settings). Changing the amount needs no new plans. Nothing to plan (no packs needed) gives `budgetCands = []` (set in `budgetCombinedPlanAsync`, also on the saved copy) and `budgetSpendBest` returns null for an empty list: before 17 Oct 2026 Budgets froze in an endless redraw for anyone with an amount set and nothing to buy. Shown as a 4th "My budget" choice (`BUDGET_VIEW = "budget"`) with one line per month / week (spent, bar, "kept for next month", that period's packs; 8 lines, then "Show all"); "—" and a message when it can't finish within 3 years. My Purchases suggestions follow that plan's days (`plan.spendSched`, `budgetSuggestOf`) and show the chosen week's (or its month's) budget bar (`.pur-wk-bud`). Admin only (owner, 17 Oct 2026): with My budget and a target both set, Suggested packs compare the two plans: a switch "By your target / My budget" picks which plan the list follows (localStorage `pur_follow`; `budgetSuggestOf` adds `targetPlan` = `byTarget` or the split target plan), each week tab also shows the other plan's cost, and every pack gets a label: both (the same pack in both plans that week; a different step or count is noted, e.g. "target: up to QAR 184.99 (4 of 5 steps) ×1"), target (only to make the target), budget (only in My budget) (`.pur-cmp`, `rank`, `tagHtml`). The other plan's own packs are listed after ours with their own "+ Will buy"; "+ Will buy all of <week>" only adds the followed plan. Weeks stay (month tabs were tried in a mock-up and dropped). Without a target (nothing to compare), a "Following: My budget (QAR 3,000 a month)" line sits under the title (`.pur-following`). That line (and "· following My budget" in the admin layout) is tappable (owner, 17 Oct 2026; `data-purgospend`, "Change ›"): it opens Budgets scrolled to the My budget box, which flashes (`BUDGET_FOCUS_SPEND`, `.bud-spend.flash`). The setting stays on Budgets (owner asked, kept there). "+ Will buy" on a "both" pack the plans buy differently (another step or count) opens with a choice of version on top ("My budget" / "By your target", price, step × count; `vers`, `openVer`, `data-pmver`), each with its own dates. Admin layout of Suggested packs (owner, 17 Oct 2026; visitors keep the old one): `compactHtml` = a short summary of the chosen week (week + dates, "following …", big total + "N packs", ✓ month budget spent / limit, "<other> plan would be QAR X", label counts, ⇄ switches the followed plan), big week boxes (`.pur-wb`: name, amount, dates, other plan's amount), then "Tick what you'll buy" and one line per pack in a box that shows about 4½ lines and scrolls (`.pur-cl-list`): tick box (the followed plan's packs start ticked; `PUR_SUG_TICK`, for the visit), picture, short name × count, label with "*" when the plans buy it differently, price, ＋ (the Will buy pop-up for that pack: pick dates / version); one "+ Will buy (N) · QAR X" button adds every date of the ticked packs that week (`data-purtickbuy`). Tapping a line opens its items, its date and step / option, and the other plan's version as a note (`PUR_SUG_ROW`); the My budget / By your target version is chosen only in the ＋ pop-up (owner, 17 Oct 2026). The ＋ pop-up (admin) has "Buy up to" step buttons for packs bought in steps (`stepsOf`: option ladders from `r.steps`, plain buy-in-order ladders from `packOffersFor` rows `from`..`row`; not for "+ N×" repeats): fewer steps save an option pack as its first steps (`choice` label / price / gives) or a plain ladder as `row` = the last step bought (later steps stay on offer for that run; option packs don't reopen). Will buy pop-up = design B (owner, 17 Oct 2026, admin only; replaced the "Buy up to" buttons): `stepModel(r, tb)` gives every step of the pack's run with its versions — option ladders (`r.tables` > 1 with `r.steps`: per step the set's tables' offers, tags from `packLinkSetTags` / `packOptionTag`, the plan's version found by nearest gives `pickOf`), pick-one cards (Benefit Card: one step, switch the card) and plain buy-in-order ladders (rows from `from`); one line per step (`.pur-ms-row`: tick `data-pmtick`, price, version switch `data-pmopt`, all items with pictures, gems left out), bought in order (tick k ticks 1..k, untick k unticks k..), steps past the plan can be ticked too; an amber note lists what differs from the plan ("Budgets will plan the rest again"); My budget / By your target are two boxes side by side (`.pur-ms-vers`: name small, price big, steps · count; the chosen one lit blue); the price and the version switch sit on one line per step (`.pur-ms-l`, the switch scrolls sideways if too wide; Benefit Card's cards wrap instead, `.pur-ms-row.card`), items on the line under it. Unchanged picks save exactly as before; changed ones save `row` = last step (plain ladders: that row) and `choice` {label "QAR 17.99 Exquisite · …", price, gives}. Each date can buy other steps (owner picked A, 17 Oct 2026): the top list is "Set all dates"; each date line has its own control — packs bought in order (option ladders, plain ladders, ordered Mix & Match `pickladder`) a native "up to N · price" select (`data-pmdk`), "any price" packs (pick-N packs without order, kind `pick`: every price with its own tick and N item dropdowns `data-pmpick`) a "17.99 + 74.99 ▾" button that opens a tick list of prices under that date (`data-pmdopen` / `data-pmdt`); dates set apart are in `dsel` (cleared when the top ticks change), "dates differ" tag + note; Add saves one purchase per date (per price for `pick`). Item picks are picture slots (owner picked A, 17 Oct 2026; no names, name on long-press / hover): N slots on the price line (`.pur-slots` / `.pur-slot`, `data-pmslot`), tapping one opens a strip of every item's picture under it (`.pur-slot-strip`, `data-pmslotopt`, open slot in `openSlot`); every item in the pop-up is a picture (`itemIc`: speedup pictures by type, `EXPERT_INVENTORY_ICONS` (Books, Affinity, Sigils…), `materialIconSrc`, then Sigils / Books / Essence / XP 10 · XP 100 by name); checked on every item of every pack table (all rows and columns, chests opened): none without a picture. The pop-up is at most 82% of the screen with a ✕ in its sticky header (`.pur-m-head`) and the total / Close / + Will buy row stuck to its bottom; the steps list (30vh) and dates list (26vh) scroll inside it. Date pickers (max 160 px, tighter under 380 px) show "up to N · 129.97" without the currency, and "× 1" is left out on those rows so the price fits a phone. Ordered Mix & Match picks its items once for every step (slots key "all", label "… · each step: 2× A + 1× B" like the planner's). Visitors see the old pop-up (until 17 Oct 2026: now visitors get the same Suggested packs list, Will buy pop-up and plan compare as the admin, owner's request; the old visitor layout and its switch `PUR_NEW_FOR_ALL` were deleted in Clean-up step 9; My budget itself still follows Admin → Visibility "spendbudget"). Money in the pop-up and on the list lines is added up in store prices (`packConvert` per step / `packRowLocalUnits`), e.g. QAR 17.99 + 36.99 = 54.98. Redraws for the same week keep the list's scroll place (`data-week`, `keepTop`); another week starts at the top. Each line's picture is its main item (O1, owner 17 Oct 2026: it used to be the first item, so Hero Rally looked like a speedup buy): `shownOf(r, n)` (replaced `mainOf`, 17 Oct 2026) picks the item this row gives the biggest share of across all plan rows plus the chips the opened line shows (rounded amount > 0, no Gems, at most 6, main always among them), a "+N" badge (`.pur-cl-more`, = shown chips − 1) counts the others, and in the opened line each item is tagged under its chip (T1, owner 17 Oct 2026): main (outlined), needed (green), extra (grey, chip dimmed) — needed = in the plan's `need` (`budgetSuggestOf` puts `combined.need` on the plan), a source of one of the plan's trades, or a chest whose contents are needed (`isNeeded`); since 17 Oct 2026 also items a silent trade of a ticked Budgets page turns into a needed item, chained (`tradeRatesFor(page).filter(silent)` over `BUDGET_PAGES` in `BUDGET_SELECTED`: Mythic General Hero Shard, Marks of Valor, Widget Chest, Mystery Points, Gen widgets, General Sigils, Sail of Conquest / Fiery Heart / Compass, Advanced Marks, General Speedups; `plan.trades` drops silent trades, so these used to show as extra and Hero Rally's main item came out as Research Speedups); the main item is picked among the needed ones; single-item packs get no tags. Line names are shortened (`purShortName`: option lists dropped, "Hall of Heroes: Marks of Valor" → "Hall of Heroes", "Exclusive Gear Enhancement Special" / "Exclusive Gear Pack" → "Exclusive Weapon"; display only) and show the chosen option of a pick-one pack ("Benefit Card · Chief Gear", `optOf`). Owner's data, $400 a month with carry-over: done Jan 29, 2027, $1,317 (Oct $399, Nov $398, Dec $334, Jan $395).
- Audit fixes (17 Oct 2026, PR #64): Admin is owner-only in `render()` too (a visitor on `adminpage` goes to the Dashboard and `last_tab` = overview; Log out also resets it). `t12FormatTime` and the Hero Shards `fmtMin` round the total first (no "2683d 24h" / "60m"). Pack Cost plan dates in another year get a short mark (`packYearMark(d)`: "Jan 8 ’28 · 448d"; a full year didn't fit phone cards).
- Design fixes D1–D10 (owner approved, 17 Oct 2026, PR #66): Arabic keeps number order (`bidiNum(s)` wraps in LRI … PDI only when `dir="rtl"`; used by `packFmtMoney` / `fmtMoneyIn`, so every price, the "×N" counts in My Purchases, `plxStatBoxesHtml` values, research time, planner squares, Budgets stat boxes; Budgets "now → after" is one isolate). Backpack `.bp-name` 2 lines at 10px; tiles with a Lv badge drop "Lv.N" / "(protected)" from the visible name. Phones ≤700px: tap targets at least 32px (Pack Cost / calendar chips and arrows, `.pk-bud`, Used for, research − / +, `.plx-stat-tabs button` …); text under 10px grows to 10px (`.pk-tm`, Dashboard `.dash-fit` labels; the Dashboard "/ max" and "+N%" stay 10px with letter-spacing −.4px and don't grow with `--sx`, fits 360–430 px). Budgets stat increase = `statTableHtml(deltas, id)` troop tabs (`plxStatTabsHtml("bud_stat_<id>")`, green +gain, now → after; since 18 Oct 2026 all stats in one row, `.plx-stats.one.bud-st`, text sized to the box with `cqi`, "now →" / "after" on two lines); `.bud-partial` "Counts N of M ticked pages…" (`budgets_total_partial`) when ticked pages have no saved plan. Planner totals say "X short" without a minus (`bsShortHtml`, `.bs-sw`; also research time and tile status). Experts Level / Research: "Count" tick (`label.exp-count`, `expert_count_tick` / `expert_count_tip`), skill selects in `.exp-skl`, General speedups in the research pages' `rs-row` markup, Pack Cost card (the same card layout as every page). Skin Gallery / Islands / Facility stats = `plxStatBoxesHtml` in a "Troops Stats" card (Islands: `plxStatTabsHtml("islands")`). `.plx-stat span` / `.plx-pick` names 2 lines; Pets Current / Target one per row ≤480px. Admin on phones: language line wraps, Currencies "Add a currency" `.adm-cur-add`, Heroes Gen chips "Gen 17 · 3 heroes". (The Experts "Count" ticks were removed 20 Nov 2026, see "Step 76".)
- Heroes Special Skills + Dashboard (17 Oct 2026, PR #63): the page's heroes = every buff-table column that isn't an app category (`ADMIN_RESERVED_BUFF_CATEGORIES`) or a flat Dashboard source (`specialSkillHeroNames`); `renderExtraBonusHeroes` draws one section per hero (Jero / Natalia keep `SPECIAL_HERO_META`, others use the heroes table picture). Dashboard: each hero counts by stars (calc_state `hero_stars_<name lowercase>`.__shared__): max = its value in the "<troop> <stat>" buff row, now = max / 5 × stars (was fixed Jero 15 / Natalia 10, same numbers today). Collection Gallery / Tundra Album max read their level tables (`dashCached` "gallery_levels" / "album_levels", released levels; old constants as fallback).
- Admin round 1 (17 Oct 2026, PR #63; nothing saved = site unchanged): **Level unlocks** — Rules & Timeline → Unlocks → + Add row → kind "level" → which levels (`LEVEL_LOCK_TARGETS`: charms, chief_gear, hero_mastery / hero_enhance (Enhance 101+ = Mithril), t11, t12, research_center, pets, expert_level, expert_skill, expert_research, vip) → first locked level → state day → Save (rows in `game_rules.unlocks` with `table`, `level`). `releasedLastIdx` also stops before a waiting lock (`levelLockIdx`; Dashboard T11 / RC `levelLockedRow`, T12 `levelLockTop` in `t12Rel`), so Max / Max everything / Dashboard max / default My max stop there; skipped when the person is already past it (`inUse`); locked levels stay pickable, tagged "· Upcoming", with the 🔒 note; `LEVEL_LOCKS_ON` keeps the work off when none exist; `budgetStateSig` covers Pets / Experts locks (`BUDGET_LEVEL_LOCKS`). Built-in unlock rows now carry a `key` (`stateUnlockDay` matches `key || name`, `stateUnlockBuiltinKey` restores it; kind / name read-only, label / day editable). Not targets: Pet Refine, Tundra Album, Collection Gallery, expert hero level. **Expert skill rename** (Experts → Roster → ✎): asks, renames in `experts` + `expert_skill_nodes`, appends { hero, from, to, at } to site_config `expert_skill_renames`; `renameExpertSkills` applies `EXPERT_SKILL_RENAMES` then that list (`expertSkillRenameList`); a name the expert ever had is refused. **Dashboard sources** (Admin → Dashboard sources, Game data): site_config `dashboard_sources` = { extra: [flat buff categories], order: [source keys] }; drag for the breakdown order (`dashSourceOrder`, default `DASH_SOURCE_ORDER_DEFAULT`, new heroes after Natalia, extras last); "Add a simple bonus source" makes a buff column, ✎ = troop × stat grid saved into the "<troop> <stat>" buff_cells, ✕ stops counting it (data stays); extras count as both now and max.
- Admin round 2 (17 Oct 2026, PR #61): **Game names in Texts** — `gameNameTexts()` (heroes, experts, skills, talents, pets, skins / categories, pack titles, buff columns / rows, pack resources, Backpack items, T12 / RC labels; names already translated elsewhere skipped) joins `textsFor(code)` for the list counts, CSV and the Texts editor ("Game names" filter); saved as `data:<name>` in `lang_<code>` (coverage % drops: ~500 names now listed). Owner (4 Nov 2026): game names (skins, islands, heroes, packs, expert skills, bonus groups — ~400, why Arabic / Korean show 86%) stay in English for now; if translated later, copy the game's own names from screenshots of the game in that language rather than guessing. **English texts** — site_config `lang_en` = { "t:<key>", "ui:<text>" } loaded with the bulk site_config, applied by `applyEnglishOverrides` (built-in kept in `EN_BUILTIN_T` / `enBuiltinText`; `enUiText`, `uiLocOff()`); Admin → Languages English row has Texts / Download / Upload, ↺ or typing the built-in text removes the override; untranslated texts in other languages show the changed English. **Research trees** (Rules & Timeline): `t12Tree` (T12 items: Id, Section, Shown as, cost rows by tier name, Max, Stat, % per level; saved levels are keyed by Id), `rcTracks` / `rcTiers` (Research Center tracks per tab and tiers, rows matched "<track> <tier>"), in `game_rules`; Budgets picks them up the next time the page is opened.
- Admin round 3 (17 Oct 2026, PR #62): **Items** (Admin → Items, Packs & Budgets group): one sheet per item — picture, short tile name, speedup minutes, which planner pages fill from the Backpack, read-only chest pages, "Counts as" (same as Pack Resources "Gives"), Reset to built-in; site_config `item_registry` = { name: { icon, short, minutes, pages, chests, count, from } } (changes only; defaults `BACKPACK_PAGE_LINKS` / `BACKPACK_PAGE_CHESTS` / `BACKPACK_FIXED_CHESTS` / `BP_MINUTES` / `MATERIAL_NAME_SHORT`); readers `itemPageMaps()`, `itemReg`, `itemRegIcon`, `itemShortName`, `itemMinutes`, `bpKeyFor`, `chestGives` (every item picture reader checks it first). A new speedup size = name "<Kind> Speedup 24h" + minutes. Renaming a linked Backpack item can keep its links (`from: <old>`). "<X> Sigils" can get its own picture. **Rules → Pages / more rules**: `galleryBig` (big gallery pictures, also "Big pictures: on/off" in ⋯ Category), `globeSkins` (`GLOBE_LEVEL_EXTRA_SKINS_GLOBAL`), `packDatesGroups` (Bazzar; removed 17 Nov 2026, unused), `ngForge` (`NG_HERO_DAYS`), `affinity` (`AFFINITY_ITEM_VALUE`, also updates `PACK_BUNDLES` and the experts trade); widget levels follow the Rules table (`widgetMaxLevel()`); Packs editor gems / Amount ladder follow `MP_GEMS`. Skin Gallery categories follow `skin_page_categories.sort_order`. Admin → Pets "Pet order" (⇅, site_config `pet_order`, `petOrderSort`). Helpers `saveGameRule(key, value)`, `openAdmSheet` `deleteLabel` / `half` / `note`. Skipped on purpose: heroes' Max stars (30 star steps are wired everywhere; the data has no rows for more).

- Pinch zoom on (owner, 18 Oct 2026): viewport is `width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0`; a small head script adds `maximum-scale=1.0` on iPhone / iPad only (iOS still allows pinch with it, and it stops the zoom into boxes under 16 px). An iPhone shrink (owner, 3 Nov 2026: "font and images look too big on iPhone"; the page laid out at 440 px and shrunk to fit) was tried and removed the same day (owner): every page uses the plain `width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0` line (iOS only adds `maximum-scale=1.0`); pages size themselves to the phone instead (Chief Gear first, see its Responsive A–D). Why the iPhone looked tighter: the owner's Fold shows the phone layout about 570 px wide, the iPhone 15 only 393. Swipe cards use `touch-action: pan-y pinch-zoom` and ignore a second finger (`e.isPrimary`).

- VIP daily shards (owner, 18 Oct 2026, from the owner's VIP screenshots): the VIP Daily Free Bundle gives Mythic General Hero Shards a day — `VIP_DAILY_SHARDS` = { 7: 1, 8: 1, 9: 2, 10: 2, 11: 3, 12: 6 } by tier number (`vipTierNumber`, `vipShardsFor`; Rules & Timeline → Heroes → "VIP daily shards", rule key `vipShards`). `heroShardBudget` adds daily × days (`budgetFreeDays`: up to the Budgets date, 30 without one, same as the free Mystery Points) to owned "Mythic General Hero Shard" for the tier saved in calc_state `vip_tier` (only when a mythic roster hero needs shards), which trade 1:1 into the heroes' shards; it returns `vip` = { tier, daily, days, total }, kept on the page as `pg.vip`, shown as a green note in Budgets' Hero shards & widgets details (`.bud-vip`). VIP page: a "Daily Free Bundle" card under the tier picker (`#vipBundle`, `showBundle`); picking a tier calls `budgetInvalidate()`. For everyone (visitors pick their own tier). The Dashboard roster cards are unchanged (owner: no need there).
- Order of goals (owner picked A + C, 18 Oct 2026; PR 1 of 3, admin only — `IS_OWNER`; replaced 10 Nov 2026 by per-upgrade goals, see "Goals, Free now and the dated plan"): a card on Budgets under the total (`goalsHtml` in `renderBudgetsPage`, `wireBudgetGoals`). Main goals = the ticked pages' own targets (one row each, from Budgets' page list); mini targets = an in-between level on a page (for now Charms and Chief Gear: `GOAL_MINI_PAGES`, `goalMiniLevels`, `goalMiniTotals` = the page's needs with every piece capped at that level; `goalTargetLabel` for "→ Lv 18"). Saved in calc_state `budget_goals` = { order: [goal ids "page:<calcKey>" / "mini:<id>"], minis: [{ id, page, level }] } (`ensureBudgetGoals`, `saveBudgetGoals`); `budgetGoalList` keeps the saved order, puts new goals in their default place and keeps a page's own goals in level order. Dates: `budgetGoalDates` = the shown plan's packs bought on their first chances (`packPlanSchedule`), the plan's own trades made as their items arrive, then `packShortAfterTrades`; goal k is reached once goals 1..k's needs of its items have arrived (binary search over buying days, then weekly to the plan's end); "$ so far" = money spent by then. The plan itself is unchanged (green line "Same packs as your plan, only the order changes"), so order only moves shared items between goals. drag ⋮⋮ only (`dragSort`), ✕ on minis, fold (`BUDGET_GOALS_OPEN`, for the visit). Next: PR 2 "By" deadlines per goal (next SvS / KOI / new Gen / date) + Roadmap card; PR 3 hero targets per hero + "push 2 per Gen" (with the New Gen heroes card).
- Goals PR 2 (18 Oct 2026, admin only; replaced 10–11 Nov 2026: By picker → K4 pop-over, Roadmap card gone, dated plan kept as `budget_deadline_v2`, see "Goals, Free now and the dated plan"): each goal has a "By" chip (BUDGET_GOALS.by[goal id] = "svs" / "koi" / "gen" / "date:yyyy-mm-dd", missing = main target; `goalDeadlineDay` = next event start (`gameEventStarts`) / next new heroes (`newGenStarts`) / the date; `goalByName`, `goalByIcon`; picker under the row, `BUDGET_GOAL_BY_OPEN`). `budgetGoalDue` gives each goal's deadline (a later same-page goal's earlier date counts for its minis). With any date set, `budgetDeadlineFor` sends the stages (goals grouped by deadline, needs from `budgetGoalSegs`, the rest by the main target or the plan's own end) to the planner worker (`plannerRun("deadline")` → `plannerDeadlineLocal`, in `PLANNER_FN_NAMES`): each stage planned by its day with the earlier stages' pack limits used up (`g.used`, rows matched back to offers by `offerKey`, since a plan's picks point into a pruned copy) and their spare items carried; a stage that can't make its day is planned for its earliest day (`parts[].ok / earliest`). Result kept in `BUDGET_DEADLINE` + localStorage `budget_deadline_v1` by fingerprint. Rows show ✓ ready / ⚠ "By SvS: N% · fully done <date>" (`pctDue` from `budgetGoalDates(…, due)`; a dated goal's day comes from its stage). Summary: "With your dates: total X (+Y) · without them Z". The Budgets total card is unchanged (still the plain plan). Roadmap card (`.bud-rm`, `BUDGET_RM_OPEN`, `BUDGET_RM_WITH`): With priorities / Without switch (without = the plain plan in the default order), table of goals (with, without, deadline ✓/⚠), then packs by week (Mon–Sun, one line per pack by short name `purShortName(purPackName(…))`, top 5 + "N more packs", tap → items + "for: goal"), purple SvS / KOI bars when a goal uses them, flags where goals are reached, first 3 weeks open, the rest under "N more weeks". My Purchases does not follow the dated plan yet.
- Goals PR 3 (18 Oct 2026, admin only; since 10 Nov 2026 a hero's stars / widgets are upgrade goals; the Hero targets fold and `HERO_TARGETS` stay in the goals card, see "Goals, Free now and the dated plan"; since 24 Nov 2026 Push and "push 2 per new Gen" are gone (their saved keys `push` / `pushGen` are kept, unused), see "Gen 15 heroes in Budgets goals"): Hero targets (calc_state `hero_targets` = { heroes: { heroId: { push, stars 1–5, widget 0–10, later, wNow } }, extra: [heroId], pushGen }, `ensureHeroTargets`, `HERO_TARGETS`). `heroShardPlan` takes `targets` / `extra` (target step `tStep` = stars × 6, widget target `tW`; Later = nothing planned; extra heroes = beyond the roster, widget level from `wNow`, default 0); only `heroShardBudget` passes them (admin), so the Dashboard roster cards and the Backpack keep 5★ / 10. `HERO_PLAN_LAST` = the last plan's rows + heroes for the card. Order of goals: Hero shards & widgets becomes one goal per hero ("hero:<id>", `items` = "<hero> Shards" / "<hero> Widgets", `grp` keeps them independent of each other's order), named "Elif 5★ + widgets 10". "Hero targets" fold in the card (`heroEdHtml`, `BUDGET_HT_OPEN`): per hero push tick / stars / widgets / Later, ✕ for added heroes, "+ Add a hero", switch "Each new Gen: push 2 heroes first" — a push moves the hero's goal to the top and, with the switch on, sets "By next SvS"; when the newest Gen (`newGenGen`) has fewer than 2 pushed heroes, a chip row asks which 2 (`data-htpick`, adds them to `extra`). Any change → `budgetInvalidate()` + redraw. Rows: `.bud-g-t` is `flex:1 1 0` so a row stays on one line and only the By picker wraps under it.

- Mini targets on every page (owner, 18 Oct 2026, admin only like the rest of Order of goals; replaced 10 Nov 2026: goals no longer use minis (`miniGoals`, `GOAL_MINI_PAGES` gone); `setPageMinis` still saves `budget_needs[calcKey].minis` and `goalMiniLevels` / `goalMiniTotals` remain only for `budgetGoalSegs`' old branch, see "Goals, Free now and the dated plan"): `goalMiniLevels(page)` gives options { value, label, grp, grpLabel, chain, idx, totals? }. Worked out live: Charms / Chief Gear (whole page), Hero Gear (per track, Mastery / Enhance; every 10th row when a table has over 40), Hero shards (per hero: whole stars on the way to its target from `heroShardPlan` row `cum`, and widget levels; chains `hero:<id>:s` / `:w`, they belong to that hero's goal). The other pages save their own minis with their needs (`setPageMinis(calcKey, cands, full)` → `PAGE_MINIS`, stored in `budget_needs[calcKey].minis` by `recordBudgetNeeds`): T12 "Up to <section>", T11 "All tracks to Lv N" (`computeNeeded(troop, track, cap)`), Research Center "Up to Tier N", Pets per pet every 10 levels, Pet Refine "Up to <stage>", Experts Level per expert every 10 levels, Experts Research per expert "All paths to Lv N" (every 20); same basis as the page's own needs. A page that saved its needs before this shows "Open these pages once…" in the add form (`goal_open_pages`). Goals: `miniGoals(page, opts)` (each mini has `chain`, `owner` = "page:<key>" or "hero:<id>", `lvl`); `budgetGoalList` keeps each chain in level order and a target after its minis; `budgetGoalSegs` goes per target: each mini takes what its level adds over the chain's previous mini, owned amounts pay for the first goals, the target takes the rest. `budgetGoalDue` passes a later deadline back along the chain / to the target's minis. Add form: one select with an optgroup per pet / expert / track / hero.
- Roadmap page (owner picked the separate page + design 2, 18 Oct 2026; admin only; removed 10 Nov 2026, see "Goals, Free now and the dated plan"): menu Marketplace / Budgeting → Roadmap (tab `roadmap`, owner-only in `render()` like Admin). It is drawn by `renderBudgetsPage` from the same plan, goals and dates (`budTab()` = Budgets or Roadmap; on the roadmap tab the goals block fills `rmPageHtml` and `drawRoadmapPage(html)` draws it instead of Budgets): summary boxes (this week, next goal, all done), Goals card (With priorities / Without switch + table; tap a goal → its week), week chips (first 4 weeks, then one per month; a week not drawn yet is drawn first, `ROADMAP_JUMP`), week cards (This week "now" / Next week / dates, price, packs count, "spent so far", top 5 packs + "N more packs", SvS / KOI bars and goal flags under the card, "· · · N weeks · · ·" between far-apart weeks), "Load more weeks" (+6, `ROADMAP_WEEKS`). Two columns on PC (≥900px). Budgets' big Roadmap card was replaced by one line; since B3 (3 Nov 2026) the This week tile does that job (the line's code was removed in Clean-up step 7). Goal lines (owner, 18 Oct 2026): each week's pack goes to the first goal in order that needs what it gives (or what trades into it: `itemsOf` follows every page's trades + the plan's) and isn't reached before that week (`forGoalAt`, `goalPacks`); a goal's flag shows "its packs <price>" and opens its packs with their weeks (`.rm-flag` details); packs for a goal reached that week come first in its card with a blue edge (`.rm-hot`).

- My Purchases Coming / Bought = compact rows A + groups B (owner, 19 Oct 2026; now the O1 lists, see the redesign round): one short line per pack (`groupHtml`: `.pur-rw` / `.pur-row` = main item picture `mainIcon`, short name `purShortName` × count, date, price, ✓ `data-purgmove` on Coming, ▸); tapping the line (`data-purrowtap`, open state "o|<id>" in `PUR_OPEN`) shows the old details under it (`.pur-row-d`: tier / choice, item chips, Used for, Dates / ×, the date rows). - Menu split (owner picked option 1, 18 Oct 2026): the "Marketplace / Budgeting" group (key `Marketplace`) is now **Packs** (Packs, My packs) and a new group **Planning** (key `Planning`: Budgets, Roadmap, My Purchases; Roadmap removed 10 Nov 2026), in the built-in HTML menu and `NAV_GROUP_LABELS`; `nav.g:Marketplace` / `nav.g:Planning` in every lang file (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 26; 27 `pur_updating`, 28 `pur_wk_past` / `pur_1_pack`; 33 once PR #88 and #89 were both in). site_config `menu_layout` was empty, so nothing saved needed moving.
- My Purchases picks without the wait (owner picked B + A, 19 Oct 2026): every purchase change still makes a new plan, but (B) a pick that is exactly what the plan suggested (same pack, step / choice, some of its dates; "+ Will buy" pop-up unchanged, "+ Will buy all of <week>", the tick list when every ticked line is the followed plan's own version) sets `PUR_SUG_OVERLAY` = { plan, taken: [{ ref "b:<row>" / "t:<row>", off, count }] } (`takeAsPlanned`): the next draw shows the same plan without those dates at once (`drawPlan(plan, taken)`), and the new plan is worked out quietly, then the page redraws with it (`redraw()`; the solver may land a few dollars cheaper and shift a line, e.g. owner's data $3,987.22 → $3,972.25 after a $9.99 pick). (A) Any other change keeps the last plan on screen, still tappable (owner, 19 Oct 2026: keep picking while it updates), a little faded (`.pur-upding`) with an "Updating…" pill (`.pur-upd`, `t("pur_updating")`): `drawPlan(PUR_SUG_SHOWN.plan, …, updating)`; picks from it work like (B) on that plan. Only when there is no drawn plan the old box is copied as a faded, inert picture (`.pur-stale`, `PUR_SUG_KEEP.html`). `budgetInvalidate` clears the overlay; `PUR_SUGGEST_DONE` lets a finished plan draw without a frame of waiting; `PUR_SUG_TRACK` drops an older suggestions request (`plannerSupersede`), whose rejection is ignored (`err.superseded`). Only the page's own `redraw` keeps the faded list (a `render()` from outside starts the box fresh).
- Planner page order (owner, 19 Oct 2026): Settings (compact) → Total resources needed → **Troops Stats** → the page's pieces → (side / bottom) Amounts from your Backpack → Pack Cost. `plxShellHtml` takes `statsHtml`, drawn right after the "Total resources needed" card (`.plx-stats-top`); used by Charms, Chief Gear, Heroes Gears, Pets (`#petsTotals`), Pet Refine (own `#refineStats`, now titled "Troops Stats"; its Marks card stays in `#refineTotals` below), Experts Level (title "Troops Stats", first tab "All troops"), Experts Research. Experts Level / Research stat boxes in one row (`plxStatBoxesHtml(…, true)`). Settings card compact: "⚙ Settings" with a small "i" (`.set-info`, `data-setinfo`, one delegated handler) that shows the explanations (`p.desc`) and the long "My max · …" label (`.cap-l`), hidden by default. T12 / T11 / Research Center have no Troops Stats card (each node shows its own stat). "Total resources needed" stays at the top (it is the page's answer; the Backpack tiles fill themselves from the Backpack and are rarely touched) and Pack Cost stays last (need → have → buy). `LANG_FILES_VERSION` 29 (`set_info`).

Purchases added before have no tag.
- Bought stays until the pack's copy ends (owner picked A + B, 19 Oct 2026): `purBoughtUntil(p)` = { until: last day } or { once } — the last day that copy is on sale (owner, 19 Oct 2026: "when the pack ends this week / month", not when the next copy comes) — repeating packs: copy start (anchor `resetDay` + k × reset; no anchor = Mon-week; 30 = calendar month) + its "Lasts" days (`window`, else the whole reset), daily = that day, "unlimited" = that day; New Gen packs (`ng-s/ng-w-…` and `packIsNewGen`): the end of their 4-week window after the new-heroes day (Training weeks 1–4, Forge `NG_HERO_DAYS` later), or already over when that hero is maxed (5★ for shards, widget max for widgets; from `HERO_PLAN_LAST`); triggered packs: stay until the expert is past that level; other one-time packs stay. Bought rows show "Until Oct 25 · 6 days" (amber at ≤ 2 days, "last day"; one-time "∞ One time · stays"; `.pur-until`). A row whose date has passed (or `hidden`) leaves the list (`boughtShown`); its data is erased once its week AND month are over too, so My budget's spending for the running week / month and the pack limits stay right. ✕ on a Bought row opens a sheet (`openBoughtRemove`, `#purRmSheet`): pack box + its items (raw table columns, `rawGives`; choice packs their saved gives), "Are this pack's items in your Backpack already?", amber note when the Backpack was last saved before the purchase (`CALC_STATE_UPDATED`, now loaded with `updated_at` in `preloadCalcStates` and set by `saveCalcState`); Add the items to my Backpack (adds them to calc_state `backpack`; speedups in minutes go in as 1-minute speedups) / Yes → `hidden: true` (off the list, still counted as bought until its date) / No → removed (the plan needs the items again). - **How many up to the game's limit** (owner, 21 Nov 2026: "I want to buy more than the plan"): the Will buy pop-up's "How many" (`openVer`) now goes up to what the game allows on that date, not the plan's count (`purGameMax(r, tb, s0, d)`, `maxes[d]`): stacking cards (`packStackInfo` of the base link) = (cap − reward days still running that day) ÷ card days, rounded down, where the running days start from the Weekly cards' days left (`packActiveDays`, owner / `visitorDate` like `packLinkFor`) and add the card-days of planned / bought cards up to that date and of the pop-up's earlier dates (e.g. 3 days left today, Nov 24, cap 370, 7-day cards → 52); other repeating packs = the run's buys (`perCycle`) − what is bought / planned in that run (`usedAt`) − the pop-up's other dates in the same run; one-time packs = buys − purchases of that row; any-time packs 99; never below 1 or the plan's count. Above the plan the hint reads "Plan: N · more is fine, Budgets plans with the extra" (`pur_cnt_plan_more`); a count other than the plan's is not `takeAsPlanned` (Budgets plans again), and the extra cards count as coming purchases (`packPurchaseUse`, `packActiveCardSupply`). "Mark as bought" stays capped at the planned count. `LANG_FILES_VERSION` 122.

- What your Backpack can upgrade now = design B (replaced 10 Nov 2026 by Free now on Budgets + marks on the pages: `reachPlan`, `UPGRADE_ORDER` / calc_state `upgrade_order`, `BAR_HAVE` / `BAR_CONV` stay; `reachMarkHtml`, `reachBarHtml`, `reachWaitHtml`, `reachTradeText`, `openReachAllSheet` / `REACH_LAST` and the order sheet `openUpgradeOrderSheet` are gone, see "Goals, Free now and the dated plan") (owner, 2 Nov 2026; admin only for now: each fill function returns for visitors, `IS_OWNER`): on Heroes Gears, Chief Gear, Chief Charm, Pets Level, T12 Research and Experts Level each piece / charm / pet / node / expert level and skill shows a green "✓178" mark between Now and Target (`reachMarkHtml`, `.rch-mark`) and a bar "6 of 28 levels" / "Backpack covers it ✓" / amber "0 of N · <item>" (`reachBarHtml`); a summary line above the pieces "With your Backpack: N of M reach target · <item> runs out first · N left" with the switch My order | Best value and "Edit order ›" (`reachSummaryHtml`, `[data-rchsum][data-rchpage]`, one document click handler; since 4 Nov 2026 shown as the "Can do now" button beside Max instead, see below). Engine `reachPlan(tracks, have, mode, rows)`: each page lists tracks { id, troop ("all" for Pets / Experts), from, to, cost(i), gain(i, lv) }; it spends the amounts the Backpack boxes count (`BAR_HAVE[calcKey]`, set in `renderChiefGearResourcesBar` after chests / hidden trades, which then calls `REACH_HOOKS[calcKey]`), always inside the targets; "order" = the owner's 12 rows (one troop + one stat each, `UPGRADE_ORDER_DEFAULT`: Infantry HP, Infantry DEF, Marksman LETH, Marksman ATK, Lancer LETH, Lancer ATK, Marksman HP, Marksman DEF, Infantry LETH, Infantry ATK, Lancer HP, Lancer DEF; owner: DEF and HP on separate rows, the same order on every page), each row taking the runs of steps (up to 60 ahead) that give that stat the most per share of the Backpack; "best" = the most stat % per item over all troops; equally good steps go by the owner's troop order Infantry > Marksman > Lancer (`REACH_TROOP_RANK`), then the lowest level first (owner, 2 Nov 2026: before, the first piece in the list won every tie, so Lancer charms went to 18 while the others stayed at 17.1); steps raising no stat (expert skills, gaps before a milestone) are filled last, lowest first. Saved per person in calc_state `upgrade_order` = { mode, rows } (`ensureUpgradeOrder`, `saveUpgradeOrder`, sheet `openUpgradeOrderSheet`, drag / Reset). Per page: Heroes Gears `heroGearReachTracks` / `fillHeroGearReach` (set on screen, all 3 troops; gain from piece stats with Mastery × Enhance and the milestones), Chief Gear `fillChiefGearReach` (ATK & DEF per piece, set bonus not counted), Chief Charm `fillCharmReach` (all 18 charms, LETH & HP), Pets `fillPetsReach` (counted pets), T12 `fillT12Reach` (research time paid with research + general speedups), Experts `fillExpertsReach` (levels with Affinity + Sigils, General Sigils counted for each expert; skills with Books + expert speedups, banked EXP / Affinity taken off). "See all troops ›" (owner picked compact design C, 2 Nov 2026; Heroes Gears, Chief Gear, Chief Charm, T12): a small sheet (`openReachAllSheet(key)`, `#rchAllSheet`, `.rcs-*`) with one line per troop in the order Infantry, Marksman, Lancer ("6 charms go up · +6% LETH · HP", `reachTroopGain` / `reachGainText`; tracks of one piece share `grp`), tap a troop to open its pieces ("Jacket ×3 16.8→17", "Goggles M 15→15.4 · E 119→142"; one troop open at a time, `REACH_ALL_OPEN`), "Go to <troop> ›" switches the page (`REACH_LAST[key].go`, `reachGoTroop`) and scrolls to the first piece that goes up, My order | Best value at the bottom. Pages keep their last plan in `REACH_LAST[key]` = { plan, tracks, unit, go }; tracks carry `name`, `short` (M / E), `lab(i)`. A troop with nothing planned says "This troop is at its targets" (`rch_sum_done`) so the button stays. Not on T11, Research Center, Pet Refine, Experts Research yet. T keys `rch_*` (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 66). Choice chests (owner, 2 Nov 2026: Abyssal Shelldragon stopped at 30 with 796 Pet Chests unused): the boxes split chests by the whole target's needs (all 796 → Manual), so "can reach" now gets them closed: `reachRefresh` puts only one-way hidden trades into `BAR_HAVE` and the hidden rules of a chest with several outcomes (`/Chest/`, e.g. Pet Chest → 7 Manual / 2 Potion / 1 Serum, Custom Resource Chests) into `BAR_CONV[calcKey]`; `reachPlan(…, conv)` opens them into what each step is short of (`settle` → { draw, lots, extra } or { fail: item }, `applySettle`, weekly caps kept in `convUsed`; the weight counts the chests used). Owner's data: Abyssal Shelldragon ✓30 → ✓80 (by hand: 80, 34 chests left); Chief Charm, Chief Gear, Heroes Gears, T12 unchanged. Weekly trades (owner, 3 Nov 2026: "do you consider trade here?"): the page's trades with a weekly limit (Chief Gear Design / Polish / Alloy / Amber, Chief Charm …; not hidden, `max` > 0, page trades switched on `TRADE_APPLY`) also go into `BAR_CONV` with `trade: true` and this week's limit as the cap; `reachPlan` uses them only where they let more steps happen (sources sorted by the share of what you hold, `convTo`), records them per track (`plan.trades`) and in all (`plan.tradeSum`); the summary line says "⇄ Uses this week's trades: 720 Design → 72 Amber · …" (`.rch-trades`, `reachTradeText`) and a Chief Gear piece that used them shows a green "⇄ with trades" (`.rch-trade`, full list in its title). The Trade items card and the Backpack boxes are unchanged. Owner's data, Chief Gear My order: nothing → Jacket ★1 (5 of 15 steps) with 416 Polish → 20,800 Alloy and 720 Design → 72 Amber; Best value: one piece ★2.1 (7 of 31); Pets, Chief Charm (Jewel Secrets can't be traded for), Heroes Gears, T12 unchanged. T keys `rch_trades`, `rch_with_trades` (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 68).
- Research unlock rules (owner picked Admin design A + page notes, 2 Nov 2026; admin only like "can reach"): Admin → Rules & Timeline → **Research unlock rules** (`ADMIN_TABLES.reslocks`, `renderAdminResearchLocks`, `RLK_PAGE`; also listed in Rules & Timeline → Research trees, rule `link`): page chips T12 / T11 / Research Center, one card per group with its rules in plain words ("Lv 6–10 · needs all Skills II at 40"), ✎ / "+ Add rule" open a sheet (`#rlkSheet`: which node or "All of <group>", from Lv (a rule runs until the next rule of the same node), needs (node or whole group + level, ✕, "+ Add another need"), All needs / Any one, preview, Delete / Cancel / Save), "Reset to built-in" per page. Same rules for all troops. Saved for everyone as game rule `researchLocks` = { t12, t11, rc: [{ node: key | "group:<name>", from, any?, needs: [{ node, lv }] }] } (`RESEARCH_LOCKS`, `researchLocksSet`, `researchLockNodes(pg)` (T11 keys use "{troop}" in place of the troop name, RC keys "<base> <tier>", groups Tier I…), `researchLockRulesAt`, `researchNeedsOf`, `researchNeedsText`). Used on T12 now (`fillT12Reach`); T11 / Research Center rules are saved and wait for "can reach" on those pages. - Pop-ups keep the page behind them still (owner, 3 Nov 2026: "I can still scroll the page behind it"): `syncScrollLock` (a MutationObserver on the body, checked once a frame) puts `scroll-lock` on `<html>` (`overflow:hidden` on html and body) while any overlay in `SCROLL_LOCK_SEL` is shown (`.modal-overlay`, `.pk-sheet-back`, Chief Gear's `.cg-bd`, `.scan-ex-view`, the phone Menu `#mobileMenuOverlay`); the pop-ups use `overscroll-behavior:contain`, so scrolling inside one doesn't run on into the page. Tested: page stays put under the Chief Gear sheets, the piece sheet, Budgets' sheets and the Menu; it scrolls again once they close. New overlay kinds should join `SCROLL_LOCK_SEL`.
- **Expert levels: plain N vs N.1, event tabs** (owner, 3 Nov 2026: "Justus shows 15% max at 100, not 100.1"): the `expert_affinity_nodes` rows at 10, 20 … 100 cost sigils and their stats already hold the Advance's jump, so a plain level N (affinity full, Advance not paid) now gets the stat one normal step past N − 1 (a straight line through the levels since the last Advance, `expertPreAdvance`) and only N.1 gets the row's value; the talent (`stat_value2`, flat between Advances) the same way. One helper for every reader: `expertLevelStat(rows, level)` → { row, v, v2 } and `expertAddLevel(totals, rows, level, talent, sign)` (rows indexed per array, `EXP_LV_INDEX`), used by the Experts page (Troops Stats, the card's stat line `levelStatLine`, "can reach" `statAt`), the Dashboard (`computeExpertsTroopStatsGlobal`) and Budgets' stat increase. Outside the Experts page, whole levels saved before the ".1" levels existed are read as N.1 until that page moves them (`expertSavedLevel`, flag `__advLevels`). Justus: 99 = 14.33%, 100 = 14.40%, 100.1 = 15.00%; Kathy's talent 27% at 100, 30% at 100.1. Experts Level Troops Stats: the SvS / Foundry / Frostfire Mine / Tundra Trade tabs count only that event's own bonus (`eventHeader`: Valeria Battle Concerto + Conqueror's Spirit; Fabian Heightened Firepower + Craftsman of War; Kathy Child of Frost; Ronne Trade Dominion) against its own max, with only the stats it gives (Frostfire LETH / HP, Tundra ATK / DEF; "{name} isn't counted" when that expert's Count is off); before, each tab added the All stats too. The card's (i) lists what each tab counts (`EXP_STAT_TABS`, T keys `exp_*_d`, `exp_tabs_note`, `exp_ev_off`, English only for now; the per-tab texts all went into one (i) before, which then showed only the last one).

(4) Top box header with Hide / Show (`.g1-hd`, `.g1-fold`, `data-g1fold="top:<tab>"`, `g1FoldSync`, one document click handler): title "Troops Stats" ("Can do now · Backpack" on the research pages, `g1_can_bp`), folds everything under the stats (Can do now, Backpack, Pack cost); Chief Gear's answer card has the same button on its "ATK & DEF" line (under 440 px before the set chips). Tested at 393 px on Chief Gear, Chief Charm, Heroes Gears, Pets Refine, Pets, T12 and at 1280 px.
- **Phones: text 2 px and boxes 4 px smaller, made from the CSS** (owner, 3 Nov 2026: "you haven't resized the font and the small boxes, make them smaller by 2 for font and 4 for box"; later the same day "increase font size by 2 all pages but not dashboard": each font rule `phoneCompact` writes is now scoped to the Dashboard wrapper `.dash-fit` (`onDash` / `splitSel`: every selector of a list gets the prefix, commas inside (), [] or quotes kept; selectors already holding `.dash-fit` stay; html / body / :root ones are left out), the typed-on sizes too (`.dash-fit [style*="font-size:…"]`), and body's 14 px is `.dash-fit`'s; the box rules still apply everywhere. So on phones every page but the Dashboard has its text back at the sizes before this rule, the top bar included (same on every page); the Dashboard keeps its smaller text. Checked all 29 pages at 393 and 360 px: no sideways scroll, nothing sticking out): a small script right after the main `</style>` (`phoneCompact`) reads every CSS rule once at load and writes `<style id="phoneCompact">` inside `@media (max-width: 700px)`: each font size in px is 2 px smaller (never under 10 px; calc / clamp sizes get `max(10px, calc(… - 2px))`), each height / min-height from 24 to 100 px is 4 px smaller (and a width / min-width equal to that height, so square buttons stay square), except picture-like selectors (img, svg, icon, ic, pic, ring, logo, thumb, avatar, photo, portrait, emoji, ::before / ::after, scrollbar); media blocks that can't match a phone (min-width over 700 px) are skipped, the others are kept around their rules; the new rules keep the old ones' order and !important, so the same rule still wins. Sizes typed on elements (`style="font-size:12…28px"`) get −2 px too, and body text is 14 px. New CSS follows by itself. PC unchanged. Checked all 29 pages at 393 px: no sideways scroll, nothing cut off, no errors; before / after picture of Dashboard, Chief Gear, Packs, Budgets, Chief Charm sent to the owner.
- **Menu D was tried and reverted** (owner, 3 Nov 2026: "revert back to what it was, but show me some different mock ups closer to what it was"): the 10 groups and the phone picture tiles are back exactly as before (`renderMobileMenu`, the built-in `#topnavGroups` HTML). Round 2 mock-ups closer to the tiles (M1 4 a row, M2 groups share rows, M3 groups fold, M4 your pages on top) were shown; the owner answered "Dont change the menu" (3 Nov 2026): the Menu stays as it is. Its code is the same as main again (the `applyMenuLayout` tweak from the Menu D try was put back too); only the phone-wide 2 px / 4 px smaller layer and the scroll lock behind the open Menu touch it.

Max keeps the current levels / targets (calc_state `<key>_before_max` = { cur, at }) and puts both on the highest open level; with everything at max it reads Reset and puts them back; greyed when everything is at max with nothing saved.
