**Speed fixes (owner approved, 6 Nov 2026)** (same look and behaviour):
- **Phones redraw loop fixed**: `g1FoldSync` compares the button's text, not `innerHTML` (the ▾ / ▴ entities never matched), and sets `aria-expanded` / `.g1-can` `hidden` only when they change. Before, 9 planner pages redrew about 60 times a second at ≤ 900 px.
- **Packs → Budgets keeps the built packs**: leaving Packs sets the built pack tables / groups aside (`packTablesStash`); `ensurePackTables` takes them back (`packTablesUnstash`) only when nothing changed: no save since (`CALC_SAVE_SEQ`, counted in `saveCalcState`), the same kept `pack_tables` rows, links, My packs, language, currency and localStorage `pack_my_edits` / `pack_my_rowmax` / `my_store_prices` / `packs_currency` / `display_currency`; groups only when `PACK_GROUPS_GEN` (bumped by `budgetInvalidate` / `afterGameRulesChange`) is the same. Every `PACK_TABLES_CACHE = null` drops the stash too. Budgets after a Packs visit 818 → 534 ms (CPU ×4).
- **phoneCompact / pcBigger kept in the browser**: the generated CSS is saved in localStorage `css_compact_v1`, keyed by a hash of every inline stylesheet + the script's source + the user agent (rebuilt when any of them changes; both sheets are always there). Head script about 165 → 35–55 ms (CPU ×4).
- History-style comments taken out of the code and CSS (kept conservative); the dated stories now live only in this file.
- Duplicate T key `rl_use_daily` removed (same text).

**Speed plan Step 1 (owner approved, 7 Nov 2026):**
- **No page jump on first load**: the footer is hidden (`html:not(.page-drawn) .site-footer{visibility:hidden}`, it keeps its space) until the first page draw; `pageDrawn()` adds `page-drawn` to `<html>` at the end of the first `render()`, in its catch / "No connection" path and in boot's session-error path. Layout shift on a cold phone load 0.13 → ~0 (all sizes). The footer also stays hidden while the first settings load is still retrying (up to ~2.5 s), then shows.
- **Supabase preconnect renewed**: the app.js `<link rel=preload>` `onload` adds a fresh crossorigin preconnect (Chrome drops an unused one after 10 s on slow loads); not confirmable here, check the next PageSpeed run.
- **Grey `--muted` #8694a3 → #8a98a7** (app.css + privacy.html; contrast on cards 4.98 → 5.24); `.cando-t small.off` no longer dimmed (it was 3.89:1).

**Speed plan Step 2 (owner approved, 7 Nov 2026; nothing visible changes except notes when something fails):**
- **Time limits**: `sbRequest` → `sbFetchOnce` with an AbortController, 25 s for reads (`SB_TIME_GET`), 30 s for writes (`SB_TIME_WRITE`), per page of 1,000 rows; a timed-out / dropped read is tried once more; PATCH / PUT / DELETE / upserts only after a network error, plain inserts never (`sbCanRetry`).
- **Saves never lost**: `saveCalcState` → `runCalcSave(q)`, queue items `{ body, user }` (dropped if the person changed account); a failed save is kept unless a newer one waits, retried after 2 / 5 / 15 s, then parked (`q.parked`) and sent again on `online`, when the tab becomes visible or after any other save gets through (`calcSaveRetryParked`, `calcSaveRecovered`); one toast per failure run (`save_retry`, `SAVE_FAIL_NOTED`).
- **Budgets plan failure**: `BUDGET_PLAN_FAILED` = plan key; `workHtml(tag, cls)` shows "Couldn't work it out · ↻ Recalculate" (`budgets_plan_failed`, `.bud-fail`) where "Working out…" was; `budgetForceRecalc` clears it.
- **Redraws outside render()**: `pageErrorPanel(e)` (also render's catch) and `pageRedrawFailed(e, seq)`; `keepPlace` no longer re-throws; `.catch` on My Purchases / Budgets / the planner bars' `.then` redraws.
- `ensureUiVisibility` retries after a failed load (`UI_HIDDEN_FAILED`).
- Memory: `wirePageMax` keeps one listener pair per Max button (`PAGE_MAX_WIRED`); the planner worker reuses one Blob URL while its source is the same (`PLANNER_SRC`, `PLANNER_SRC_URL`).
- Logo = `icons/logo-64.webp` (owner picked the same-quality WebP, 8.0 → 5.5 KB; `icons/logo-64.png` kept in the repo as the backup, no longer linked). `LANG_FILES_VERSION` 74.

**Speed plan Step 3 — faster loading (owner approved, 7 Nov 2026; same data and pages, only less waiting):**
- **Menu categories in one read**: boot calls `loadBuffCategories(true)` → `loadBuffColumnsAtStart`: the buff table and its columns in menu order come in one request (`buff_tables?select=id,cs:buff_columns(…)&cs.order=sort_order.asc…`, was the table lookup, then the columns); on the Dashboard its own buff read (`dashFixedReads`) also brings `cs` (`BUFF_SORTED_COLS`, taken once by boot), so no extra read. No table (owner's first start) → the old `ensureBuffTable` path.
- **Language texts alongside the settings**: `bootLangPack()` starts a built-in language's pack (`LANG_BUILTIN` with a file) right after sign-in; `loadLangPack` shares one load per language (`LANG_PACK_LOADING`, `loadLangPackNow`), so `useLanguage` takes it. Admin-added languages still wait for the settings.
- **Shared loads**: `ensurePackLinks` (`PACK_LINKS_LOADING`; Budgets as a visitor read the owner's packs_links 3× at once), `ensureTradeRates` (`TRADE_RATES_LOADING`), `ensureSiteConfig` (`SITE_CONFIG_LOADING`), `ensureUiVisibility` (`UI_HIDDEN_LOADING`).
- `ensurePackTables`: the pack rows read starts before the links; the heroes read (`ngHeroRows()` = `dashCached("heroes")`, the Dashboard's same request) starts as soon as the links say new heroes' copies are needed.
- Small double reads: the Dashboard reads the Tundra Album / Collection Gallery levels through `keptRows` (same request and kept copy as their pages; were "album_levels" / "gallery_levels"); `ensureIslandCategories` uses the Islands page's ordered list (`islandCatRows()`, key "island_cats"), so Islands after the Dashboard only loads the islands (the islands read needs the categories, so it can't go out together); Heroes Special Skills reads the hero pictures together with the skills' buff rows.
- **Smaller settings read**: `scan_learned` (Scan's taught tiles, ~180 KB) is left out of the start-up site_config read (`SITE_CONFIG_LAZY`, `key=not.in.(…)`) and loaded when used (`ensureSiteConfigKey`: `scanLearnedLoad`, `scanLearnedSave`, Admin's "Forget"; a failed read throws, so nothing is saved over lessons not loaded; a settings reload keeps it). Read size 187 KB → 7.2 KB (gzip 112 → 2.1 KB).
- **First visit**: with no saved login, boot starts the public reads (settings, visibility, built-in language pack, and on the Dashboard the buff / VIP tables, heroes, star / widget stats: `dashPublicReads()`) while the guest sign-up is on the way (RLS: these are readable by everyone and give the same rows to a new guest); calc_state and other per-person reads still wait.
- Measured (iPhone 15 size, CPU ×4, +300 ms per request, median of 3, time to first page content): Dashboard visitor 2.98 → 2.88 s, owner 3.06 → 3.00 s; Chief Gear first 2.94 → 2.64 s (owner 2.97 → 2.58 s); Budgets visitor 2.66 → 2.26 s; My Purchases visitor 3.80 → 3.16 s; Arabic Dashboard 3.45 → 3.09 s; first visit 4.32 → 3.84 s (Arabic 4.83 → 3.87 s). 1–3 fewer reads per start. Page HTML identical on all 28 tabs (393 / 1280, owner + visitor, Arabic, first visit). Still read twice on the Dashboard / Budgets (left for later): `pet_levels`, the three expert node tables, Chief Gear `progression_tables` (Budgets).

**T12 Can do now fix (7 Nov 2026):**
- "Can do now" counted a whole week of Research Lab refining as Refined FC already in hand (the lab's hidden weekly trade went into `BAR_HAVE`), so with 10 Refined FC it said Skills II Attack 47 → 50 + Lethality up. Owner picked B: in `reachRefresh` the lab trade (`tr.lab`) now counts only this week's refinements still left: `rfcLabWeekLeft()` = (plan tries Mon–Sun − done) / plan tries, done = the "Game says" count (with the plan's days after `on`, `rfcLabWeek`) or, with no Game says, the plan's tries before today (as the This week box assumes); state from `RFC_LAB_STATE` or `RFC_LAB_RATE_ST` (the state `rfcLabApplyRate` last used; `ensureRfcLabRate` now passes `game` / `weekPick` too). Instant trades (FC → FC Shard) unchanged; Backpack boxes, short numbers, Budgets and Pack cost unchanged. The Can do now sheet adds "+N Refined FC from this week's Lab (M refinements left)" when that part is > 0 (`BAR_LAB[calcKey]`, T key `cando_lab_week`, `LANG_FILES_VERSION` 75). Owner's data (Game says 45 ≥ the plan's 26 a week): Lancer now "Skills II · Attack 47 → 48" (10 RFC, 10 a level), was 47 → 50 + Leth 40 → 42. The sheet line and `cando_lab_week` went with the Can do button (10 Nov 2026); `rfcLabWeekLeft` still limits the lab trade in `reachHaveConv`, which Free now and the page marks use.

**Speed plan Step 4 — something on screen at once (owner approved, 7 Nov 2026):**
- **supabase-js moved from the head to the end of the body**, right before app.js (same order, same SRI / crossorigin, same `document.write` vendor fallback: both stay plain parser-blocking scripts, which is what makes `document.write` work, so no `defer`). The head has a `<link rel=preload as=script>` for it with the same address / integrity / crossorigin (fetched as early as before; one request, no warning). Nothing inline uses `supabase` before it (the head scripts are viewport, menu style, clickjacking, `phoneCompact`). CSP unchanged. So the top bar and the loading layout paint while the two scripts download.
- **Loading screen**: `#content` starts with the same "Loading…" line as before (`<div class="empty boot-ld">`, owner, 7 Nov 2026: "loading same as before with better timing"; a shimmering card layout was built and dropped). `render()` keeps it on the first draw instead of writing its own, `pageDrawn()` removes it. `#topnavSettingsBtn:empty::before` (zero-width letter) keeps the top bar from growing 4 px before app.js fills the account button.
- **app.css stays render-blocking** on purpose: `phoneCompact` reads its rules in the head (and keys its saved copy on it), and without it the first paint would be unstyled and then jump. It is 72 KB gzip vs app.js 570 KB.
- Measured (393 px, CPU ×4, gzip sizes): +300 ms per request: first paint 1.47 → 1.09 s, page drawn ~2.56 → ~2.40 s; one shared 1.6 Mbps link + 150 ms: first paint ~1.6 → ~1.2 s (now the top bar + loading layout instead of an empty dark page), page ~5.6 → ~5.4 s. CLS unchanged (≤ 0.0008). Page HTML identical on all 28 tabs (393 / 1280, owner + visitor); CDN blocked or a changed CDN file → vendor copy, works; service worker second load / offline the same as before.

**Speed plan Step 5 — packed (minified) copies (owner approved, 7 Nov 2026; nothing on screen changes):**
- **After editing app.js / app.css / admin.js run `node tools/stamp.js`** (now also writes the .min copies; first time in a checkout: `cd tools && npm ci`). `node tools/stamp.js --check` fails (exit 1) when a .min copy is stale or a `?v=` / `ADMIN_BUILD` is wrong, so a forgotten run is caught. Never edit a .min file by hand; the readable files stay the source and the .min files are committed (GitHub Pages still has no build step).
- The site loads `app.min.js`, `app.min.css` (index.html: stylesheet, preload, script) and `admin.min.js` (`loadAdminCode`). Minifiers pinned in `tools/package.json` + lock: terser 5.51.2 (JS), csso 5.0.5 (CSS, `restructure: false`, so no rules are merged or moved). Terser keeps every top-level name (no toplevel mangling) and function / class names (`keep_fnames`, `keep_classnames`): the planner worker is built from functions' source text by name (`PLANNER_FN_NAMES`, `window[n].toString()`), admin.js and app.js share globals and HTML strings call globals. Only names inside functions are shortened; comments and spaces go. Each .min file starts with a one-line "Packed copy of …" comment.
- `ADMIN_BUILD` = SHA-256 (10 hex) of admin.min.js with the value as "0000000000", written into both admin.js and admin.min.js (`window.ADMIN_BUILD="…"` without spaces there); app.js links `admin.min.js?v=<that id>`. app.min.js / app.min.css `?v=` = SHA-256 of the .min file (what is served).
- `sw.js`: `VERSIONED` and `keepLinked` also match `.min.`; admin's id check accepts the packed form; storing a version drops older copies of the same file packed or not, and `activate` drops the old unpacked `app.js` / `app.css` / `admin.js` copies (the old worker had cached the new .min files as plain static files, so nothing else would have cleared them). Cache name unchanged.
- `phoneCompact`'s app.css link check is now `[href*="app.min.css"]`; its saved copy is rebuilt once (the key changes). Output the same as before except spaces inside `calc()` with `var()` (CSSOM keeps them as written): same rules, same order.
- Sizes (gzip): app.js 555.7 → 362.4 KB (−193 KB), app.css 69.2 → 56.4 KB, admin.js 75.8 → 59.7 KB (raw 1,931 → 1,245 KB, 383 → 325 KB, 305 → 232 KB).
- Measured (393 px, CPU ×4, median of 3): one shared 1.6 Mbps link + 150 ms: page drawn owner 5.67 → 4.60 s, visitor 5.60 → 4.72 s (first paint unchanged ~1.3 s); +300 ms per request: 2.40 → 2.22 s owner, 2.30 → 2.29 s visitor. Page HTML identical on all 28 tabs (393 / 1280, owner + visitor; also with the solver blocked), 0 errors; Admin opens from admin.min.js with a matching id; greedy Budgets / My Purchases plans identical; with a mocked solver all 26 solver models and the Budgets page byte-identical (with the real HiGHS the plan varies a little from run to run on old and new alike: 3 s time limit under load). Service worker: new install, upgrade from the old site (old copies dropped), wrong `?v=` not kept, offline as before.

**Speed plan Step 6 — Google + accessibility (owner approved, 7 Nov 2026; nothing on screen changes):**
- **Google**: index.html canonical, `og:url`, `og:image` / `twitter:image` now `https://www.wosrst.com/…` (wosrst.com redirects to www; Google already picked www); privacy.html has its own canonical. New `robots.txt` (allow all + sitemap line) and `sitemap.xml` (home + privacy). A `<noscript>` block at the top of the body (inline styles only, no script; CSP unchanged) says what the site is and links the Privacy Policy; it isn't drawn when JavaScript runs.
- **Pop-ups for keyboard / screen readers** (`a11ySync`, run with `syncScrollLock` from the same MutationObserver, once a frame): every shown overlay in `SCROLL_LOCK_SEL` gets `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal`, a name (`aria-labelledby` its title, ids `a11y-t<N>`) and `tabindex="-1"` on its box (`a11yDlgBox`: `[role=dialog]` / `.modal-box` child, or for `.cg-bd` the open `.cg-open` / `.rc-open` card); focus moves into the box (never into a typing box, so no phone keyboard; `preventScroll`), and when the last one closes focus goes back to the opener (`A11Y_RETURN`; a page that redrew finds the same control again by its id / first data-* attribute, `a11ySig`; `A11Y_LAST` = last control clicked / focused, used when the opener was already replaced). Attributes added this way are put back when the pop-up closes (`a11ySetAttr` / `a11yRestore`), since some boxes are plain cards on PC. New overlay kinds: add them to `SCROLL_LOCK_SEL` and they get this too.
- **Esc** closes the top pop-up with its own ✕ / `data-*close` / Close / Cancel button (`a11yCloseBtn`; Chief Gear / roster windows and Scan's example picture by their dim edge), only when no page code reacted to that Esc (no page change by the next task, `A11Y_MUTS`), the pop-up isn't one whose page handles Esc itself (`A11Y_ESC_OWN`: bottom bar, Packs window / sheets, My Purchases sheets, calendar, Research Lab, My packs, Scan) and nothing in it was changed and not saved (`a11yDirty`: typed, ticked or picked).
- **Clickable headers / cards** (`A11Y_KB_SEL`: T12 / T11 / Research Center group headers `.t12x-gh` with `aria-expanded`, the Dashboard roster cards on phones): `tabindex="0"` + `role="button"`, Enter / Space click them. The Dashboard stat boxes were already buttons.
- Names: Research Speed box `aria-label` (`set_speed`), Commander box `aria-labelledby="commanderLabel"`; the toast is `role="status"` `aria-live="polite"`.
- Tested (393 / 1280, owner + visitor, old vs new, 112 page views): page HTML identical once the added attributes are taken out, same text and widths, 0 errors; pop-ups (Chief Gear piece / Trades, Budgets Settings / Goals, Packs window, Experts skill, roster settings and cards, breakdown, phone Menu): focus moves in, Esc closes, focus returns, the page doesn't scroll.

**Islands Mythic shards / Mark of Revival tracker (owner's picks, 9 Nov 2026; PR #167; shops / Shining City / Tomes below, PR #170):**
- **Where**: every island category but Lighthouse (`ISL_SKIP_CATS`), Tree of Life left out (`ISL_SKIP_NAMES`; its bonuses switch on at their "Bonus N from level" (4 / 6 / 9 / 10), not step by step — kept on purpose); `islCatTracked`. Mythic = max level ≥ 10, null counts as 10 (`islIsMythic`); purple (max 5) = levels only (Now + Target, no settings) unless it is an Islands Guide building with a shop price.
- **Works like Heroes Gears**: ONE plan for all tracked buildings of every category (`sharedPlan` in the Islands page code); the category chips only filter the cards. Top card (same on every chip): stats in one row without "+N left" (`.sg-s2.n4`; Skin Gallery too), one "Hide ▴" in the Troops Stats header (shared `g1-fold`, calc_state `bar_hidden` "top:islandgallery"), a row "Target all [▾]" (every tracked building) + "price ›" (Chief Gear's `cg-bpbtn`; greyed "Nothing to buy" `isl_pc_none`), the planner pages' Backpack bar ("Hide boxes", `bar_hidden` key "islands") with always 4 fixed box slots (Life Essence, Mythic Island Shards, Mark of Revival, Glorious Tome; faded "—" when not needed), the plan details behind an (i) next to BACKPACK, the income bar under the boxes ("+2.4 shards a day · ✎ Edit income", `islIncomeSheet`). Same height on every chip.
- **Plan rule — free first, then the cheapest** (owner): by the target date (the Budgets / Pack cost target, `pack_target_budgets`) the free pot = Life Essence + shards in the Backpack + Storehouse copies + daily income up to the date; it pays first for buildings nothing can buy (shards-only and Islands Guide Life Essence shop copies), then for buildings also in the Mark of Revival shop; Mark-shop copies still missing by the date become Marks (≤ each building's shop limit), priced by the normal Pack cost (calcKey "islands", `islOpenPacks`) and counted in Budgets (page "islands", need = planned Marks). Result line: green "✓ By … all buildings at target" / amber "By …: N shards short → M Mark of Revival · $ price ›" / red "N copies can't be reached by … (shards only) · done ~…"; no date → "Done ~… · ~N weeks".
- **Islands unticked in Budgets** = no target: nothing is planned to buy ($ greyed, Mark box "—", Budgets 0); each Mark-shop building's pop-up shows the optional route "or N Marks ≈ QAR X ›" worked out for that building alone (`ISL_MK_OPT`, `islMkOptOpen` / `islMkOptRender`: a read-only "Pack cost · optional" window).
- **Rules** (Admin → Rules & Timeline, group `islands`; keys `islCopies`, `islTree`, `islShop`, `islCamp`): copies to reach Lv 2 … 10 = 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8, 10, 12, Lv 1 free (`ISL_COPIES`, `islCopiesBetween`); 10 shards = 1 copy; 60,000 Life Essence = 10 shards; shard shop 300 a week; Life Essence shop copies 15 a week per building (`ISL_RULES.copyLimit`); Mark of Revival: Marks for 1 copy `markPrice` (250, `ISL_MARK_PRICE`; a building can have its own price, empty = the rule); income: Tree of Life rate table (`ISL_TREE`, holds 5,000), 2 Lumber Camps (`ISL_CAMP`: workers 1, 1, 1, 2 … 2), Timbermill only when both camps are Lv 10, always Lv 1, 40 / h a worker, holds 2,000 (`islMillWorkers`), ally help 3 × 200; always counted as collected every 12 h (`ISL_COLLECT_H`, with a tip in the sheet).
- **Cards**: "→N" target badge; line = shards from the shared plan ("60 ⬢"; no finish date since PR #170, the line wraps instead of running out of the card), Marks / Tomes with the coin ("2,750"), split "60 ⬢ + 🪙 1,250", Life Essence shop copies "576K 🌿 · 6 wk", event / ranked "🎪 3 mo" / "🏆 10 mo"; Storehouse "📦N"; Mark-shop buildings get a small coin badge on the picture's bottom-left (no legend line, owner). Pictures (owner's own, used as sent): `icons/life-essence.png`, `icons/island-shard.png`, `icons/mark-of-revival.png`.

**Islands shard packs and Pack cost like every page (owner, 16 Nov 2026; step 31):**
- **Shard packs pay for building copies when cheapest**: the Myriad Bazaar steps with "Mythic Island Shards" (`ISL_SHARD_ITEM`) can pay a building copy instead of buying it with Mark of Revival / Glorious Tomes, through hidden shard → Mark / Tome conversions (`ISL_SHARD_TRADES`, `islShardTradesNow`, `islShardPacksOn`); they are a planning device only, so plans keep them out of every trade list (Pack cost, Budgets, My Purchases, the trade counts) and carry them as `islShards`; Pack cost shows one line "+N shards → M building copies (instead of buying them with Marks / Tomes)" (`pca_isl_copies`) and "Checked, not cheaper" (`pca_not_cheaper`) when a shard pack lost. Shards-only copies still short become a shards need, capped by what the shard packs can give (`islShardSupplyMax(days)`, `ISL_SH_MAX`); the result line says when no shard pack can be bought in time and when shards come next (`isl_rs_sh_*`, `islShardNextDay`). Free shards first: shards from packs other pages already buy go to shards-only buildings first (`ISL_FREE_SHARDS`, `islFreeShardsOf`, `islFreeShardsRefresh`); they're shown as "paid by <pages>" / "from packs your other pages buy", never as "free" (`pca_bought_for`, `pca_free_shards`, `isl_rs_free`).
- **Plan and Pack cost like every planner page**: the page and its Pack cost plan by the Pack cost window's own target date, whatever the Budgets tick (`PACK_TARGET_HOOKS`, `PACK_NEED_BY_DATE`, `PACK_PICK_HOOKS`, `ISL_VIEW_DAYS`); with no date, every target as soon as packs allow (`isl_rs_asap`). The Budgets need still follows the Budgets target and is saved even when Islands is unticked (the tick only decides whether it counts). Budgets splits shard packs between pages with main's rule (the Islands-only share rule was removed). Fastest says "Packs bought by <date>" (`pca_packs_by`; free income finishes later); Cheapest tries later dates up to a year after the fastest with the free income up to each date and keeps the cheapest that makes its date. The optional per-building "or N Marks" route is gone (`isl_mk_or`, `isl_mk_optional`, `isl_tm_or` removed).
- **A part-bought Myriad Bazaar run keeps its later steps** on sale for that run (option ladders get the same one-off group plain ladders had, `packPurchaseUse`): Pack cost lists them as "steps 5–9 · ends <date>" (`pack_steps_range`, `pca_ends`, `pca_next`). This reaches every page's plan: with the owner's data (greedy planner, solver blocked) Chief Charm Fastest $1,185 → $1,440, Experts Level $2,128 → $2,110, T12 Fastest $150 → $146 (the Bazaar's open steps join the offers; by target / cheapest unchanged).
- **Lighthouse unlock with Marks**: Lv 1 of Celestial Journey and Harbor of Hope costs 2,000 Mark of Revival each (first blueprint in the Mark of Revival shop, bought once), added to the Islands Mark need, Pack cost and Budgets; Starry Lighthouse keeps the free unlock (`ISL_LIGHT_UNLOCK`, `islLightUnlockMarks`, rule `islLightUnlock` in Admin → Rules & Timeline → Islands "Lighthouse unlock (Lv 1)"; 0 / empty = free). Lv 2+ unchanged.
- T keys `isl_rs_*`, `pca_*`, `pack_steps_range` in all 16 lang files (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 96). No live data changed.

**Pack-buying fixes (owner, 16 Nov 2026; step 33, built on step 31):**
- **Part-bought ladders keep the rest of the run**: ladders and option ladders (Myriad Bazaar, Dawn of Wisdom, Dawn Market, Artisans Trove, ordered Mix & Match, column-option packs) bought part way keep their later steps on sale for that run, the "last step ×N" repeats included (`packPurchaseUse`).
- **"Last step ×N" repeats**: a bought full ladder still offers the repeats left this run; "+ Will buy" saves them on the purchase as `extra` (in calc_state `pack_purchases`), counted in its gives / price (`packPurchaseExtra`, `packPurchaseGives(tb, row, from, extra)`, `packPurchasePrice`) and shown as "+ N ×" on the tier line (`purTierText`).
- **Previous copies**: a repeating pack bought in its previous copy no longer uses up the running copy (no duplicate left-over group). **Daily packs** (Daily Deals): a buy made today counts for today.
- **Packs with a reward per day** (Daily rewards set, or a gift on the first buy of each day like the Shining City Pack's +80 Tomes; `packRowDayReward`, `packDayRewardPool`): within each run the plan dates one buy a day for the pack as a whole across all its rows (cheapest rows first) and puts the rest on the run's last day, so the dates show as many gift days as the plan counts (`packPlanSchedule`); My budget re-dates them the same way and keeps it only when no week / month goes over the limit (`budgetSpendSimulate`). Hall of Heroes stays once a day; other rows with their own Max (Daybreak) can be bought several times the same day.
- No new texts; no live data changed.

**Islands shops, Shining City and Glorious Tomes (owner, 9 Nov 2026; PR #170):**
- **Where to buy copies**: the building pop-up has one switch per shop (Mark of Revival shop / Shining City, `ISL_SHOPS`, `islInShop`); the old Shops sheet is gone. The owner's picks are everyone's first choice: site_config `island_shops` = { mark: [names], shining: [names], allow: { mark, shining } }; a visitor who changes a switch keeps their own lists in calc_state `island_shards.shops` (`islShopOwn`, kept when the owner changes the defaults). Admin → Islands → "Shops: which buildings can be picked" sets `allow` per shop (only those get the switch). Never pickable (`islShopPickable`): event / ranked buildings, Lighthouse, Islands Guide, Tree of Life, purple. Without island_shops the Mark list comes from the old `marks` flag in `island_sources`.
- **Glorious Tome / Lucky Glorious Tome Chest**: Backpack items (`BP_ISLAND_ITEMS`, owner's pictures `icons/glorious-tome.png`, `icons/lucky-glorious-tome-chest.png`, pack `icons/shining-city-pack.png` + `-sm.png`). Chests that give a random amount have an odds table (Admin → Items, "By chance": amount, chance %, must add up to 100%; built-in `CHEST_ODDS`, saved as `item_registry[name].odds`, `chestOddsOf` / `chestOddsAvg`); its average (Lucky chest ≈ 20 Tomes) goes to Pack Resources' "Counts as".
- **Rules** (Rules & Timeline → Islands): `tomePrice` 600 Tomes a copy, `tomeMax` 3 copies an event, `shiningDays` 5, `tomeFreeDay` 80 free Tomes for the first buy of each event day (counted by the packs planner since the Shining City skins PR, see below). Shining City dates follow the Shining City Pack on the Packs page (`islShiningStarts`: the table titled "Shining City…", else one with a Glorious Tome column; monthly from its reset day); nothing found = no made-up date.
- **Plan**: free first, then the cheaper of Marks / Tomes per building; Tomes are a 4th Backpack box and a Budgets need like Marks. Event / ranked buildings: up to 6 copies a cycle. Islands unticked in Budgets: the pop-up shows the optional Marks and Tomes price for that building (`ISL_OPT_PRICE`, `ISL_MK_OPT`).
- **"$" removed** from every Pack cost button and title (the price text already has its currency). New texts `isl_wb_*`, `isl_sc_*`, `isl_tm_*` in every lang file (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 83).
- **Live data change** (9 Nov 2026, owner's OK): `pack_tables` row "Shining City Pack" (`11c20fcd-b6c6-40d1-a608-32712a3d921a`), hidden from visitors (`hideVisitors` in owner `packs_links`; reset 30, window 5, resetDay 2026-12-12 placeholder for the owner to set) — backup owner calc_state `backup_packs_links_20261109b`; undo: delete the row and its `packs_links` entry.

- **Skin Gallery**: the stats card header "Troops Stats · N of M owned" has Hide ▴ / Show ▾ (top right, like Islands), which folds ONLY the Shining City row (the Globe Level row always stays); saved in calc_state `bar_hidden` "top:skingallery". Under the Globe row one gold-edged row (layout S1, owner's pick): picture stack of the picked skins not owned yet (max 4 + gold "+N", tome badges) · "N skins · <next event date>" / "X Tomes" (gold) · "≈ QAR … ›" price (green "✓ in your Backpack" when the Backpack covers it). Price = the Pack cost window (calcKey `shining_skins`, `#shMkWrap`, like Islands). Nothing picked: slim "Shining City · Pick skins with the Shining City switch on a City Skin" (`sh_pick_hint`). Picked skins get only a tome badge on their grid picture.

Globe Level skins, skins outside the allow list and every other category stay one tap = owned (`skinTap`). The switch saves the same keys the sheet did. Never offered: Globe Level skins (`shiningGlobeSkin`, from `ALL_GLOBE_WIRED_NAMES_GLOBAL`) and skins outside the allow list: Admin → Skins → City Skin fold "Shining City: which skins can be picked" (`renderAdminShiningAllow` in admin.js; two A to Z groups "Can be in Shining City" / "Never in Shining City", a tap moves a tile to the other group, the list scrolls with the page on phones) → site_config `shining_skins_allow` = [names] (missing = all; "Allow all" deletes the row). Saved picks outside it are ignored (`shiningPickable`, `shiningPicked`, in app.js).
- **Rule**: `skinTomes` 10,000 Glorious Tomes a skin (Rules & Timeline → Islands, in `ISL_RULES`).
- **Budgets**: page `shining_skins` (tile "Shining City", picture = first picked skin, sheet "Shining City skins" with the skins, materials and pack cost); need = Glorious Tomes for the picked skins not owned (`shiningSkinsNeed`, worked out live), owned = Backpack Tomes + Lucky Glorious Tome Chests at their average (`shiningTomesIn`). Packs: every pack used for Islands that gives Glorious Tomes (`packTableServesPage` special case; no "Used for" tick of its own).
- **First-buy bonus in the planner**: in the Shining City Pack's event the first purchase of each day (any row, any price) gives `tomeFreeDay` (80) Glorious Tomes. `packShiningBonusOf` marks that pack's groups (`dayBonus` = { key, item, qty, days }); `packDayBonusList(groups, days)` / `packDayBonusGives(list, picks)` (in `PLANNER_FN_NAMES`): bonus = qty × min(event days × events within the plan − buys already used, the pack's buys). Exact plan: one integer per pack (≤ bonus days, ≤ its buys, free); greedy plan, max supply, `budgetMergedPlan` and the plan cache key count it too. Plans carry `bonus` = { item: amount }; the Pack cost card shows "+80 Tomes a day from the first buy · +N in this plan" (`pca_day_bonus`), never as a pack row. The Islands plan's old rough "80 × 5 days" estimate (`tomeFree`) is gone, so it isn't counted twice. Tested (USD): 1,000 Tomes $28.95 → $14.95; 30,000 Tomes cheapest $893.74 → $726.81 (solver), every other Budgets page unchanged.

**Goals, Free now and the dated plan (10–15 Nov 2026; PRs #176–#180 + step 19):**
- **Pop-overs always on screen** (#176): `placePop(pop, anchor, opts)` opens a small pop-over below its button when it fits, else above (class `pp-up`), else on the side with more room with a scroll inside, 8 px inside the screen; drawn `position:fixed` (so a sheet's / card's scroll box can't cut it; `ppClips`, `ppFixedHost`), follows its button on scroll / resize (`ppQueue`), hides when the button is out of sight or calls `opts.away()`; `popForget(pop)` undoes it. `placePopAuto(selector, anchorOf, opts)` places pops drawn as part of a page (MutationObserver); `<details>` menus via `PP_DETAILS` (Packs "⋯" `.pk-menu`, Admin "⋯ Category" `.admc-catpop`). Used by the (i) bubbles (`infoAttach`, Chief Gear's (i)), PC menu dropdowns, language / account panels, the category menu, Islands level pop-up, the goals' By pop-over (`.gbp`) and the page marks (`.mk-pop`). Look unchanged. Fix (10 Nov): a pop drawn while `keepPlace` puts the window's scroll back saw its button out of sight and closed at once (By on a goal near the bottom of a long goals window opened nothing); `away()` now runs only once the pop has been in sight.
- **Planner speed** (#177): see "Planner speed-ups 2–4" under Key code patterns (two planners, parts as they come in, `budget_plans_v2`, dated plan on the second planner).
- **New heroes window = design N1** (#178, owner): Packs → New heroes box and My Purchases' next-new-heroes "change ›" (`data-purngopen` → `openNewHeroesWindow(onClose)`, `#purNgSheet`, over My Purchases, no page change) both draw `renderPurNewGen`: answer card (next Gen, its Hall of Heroes hero, "in N days"), where the date comes from (own / state age) + Set date button that opens / closes the date box (`NG_SET_OPEN`; same saves as before: `PACK_PURCHASES.newGen`, `my_pack_dates`), then "Packs by Gen" (current and next Gens, each New Gen event's dates; tables of one Same-dates event = one chip). Texts `ng_*`.
- **Per-upgrade goals = U2** (#179, owner only like all of Order of goals): goals are single upgrades (a Chief Gear piece, charm, Heroes Gears track per set, T12 / T11 / Research Center node, pet, Pet Refine stat, expert level / skill, research path, a hero's stars or widgets, an Islands Mark / Shining City shop building's copies, a picked Shining City skin), each with its own goal level (− / + up to the page's target) and By; drag to order. Saved in calc_state `budget_goals.goals` = [{ id, page, key, level }] (+ `by`; old `order` / `minis` left untouched); goal ids `up:<id>`. `goalUpgrades(page)` lists them (live for Chief Gear, Charms, Heroes Gears, Hero shards, Shining City (`SHINING_GOAL_ROWS`); the pages in `GOAL_UP_SAVED` save theirs with their needs as `budget_needs[page].ups` when opened, `GOAL_UPS_MEMO`; `goalUpMissing` asks to open them once); `goalUpLevelIdx`; `budgetUpSegs` splits each page's need (owned pays the first goals). "+ Add goal" sheet (`.gu-sheet`: search, page chips, tick + goal level, "Save N goals"; texts `gu_*`). Research waiting for unmet unlock rules is noted on the goal. Card C (owner, 11 Nov; after C3 two-line cards the day before): name on one line with the Free now status small under it (`.c3-l1`, `cdaGoalChipHtml`), date at the right (two short lines with a year), stepper + By chip + ✕ on the second row (`.gu-r2`); the Goal dates table and explanations behind the sheet title's (i); fits 360 px.
- **"Rest of the plan" per page**: `budgetGoalList` adds a `rest:<pageKey>` row per ticked page (what is left after its picked goals), each with picture, done date and its own By; `"main"` = the main target picked on purpose (a date once set on a whole page `page:<key>` carries over until changed); `defaultOrder` = "Without" (`pg:<key>` whole pages). Notes: `gu_rest_cant` / `gu_rest_cant_own` (page can't make its date), `gu_rest_goal_later` (a goal dated after its page's rest: the row shows the goal's later day, amber). A page with nothing left after its goals gets no flag. "Today" (`pcl_today`) = reached with today's packs (still to buy); "Now" (`goal_now`) = nothing left to buy.
- **By when? = K4 pop-over** (`gbp_*`, `goalByPopClose`, placed by `placePopAuto(".gbp", …)`): your date, the main target, then every SvS / KOI / admin event / Lucky Wheel / new heroes for ~6 months by month in the Pack calendar's colours; "Pick another date" = a mini month with event dots. A picked event saves its own start day `ev:<kind>:<yyyy-mm-dd>`; old `svs` / `koi` / `gen` / `date:` values still read (`goalDeadlineDay`, `goalByKind`). Its list keeps its scroll on redraws. A goal's own date is used as it is, also after the main target.
- **Goal dates**: `budgetGoalDue` (own date, or a later same-page goal's earlier one); `budgetGoalDates(goals, pages, plan, calcKey, due, order)` (order = which goal takes what arrives first). With own dates (in `renderBudgetsPage`): `stageDay` (own date, null for a `late` page, else the main day), `partOf` (the dated plan's part for that day), `takeOrder` (by stage day, same day = list order), `boughtBy` (each goal capped by the day its part, and earlier parts needing the same items, are bought); a goal bought for the main target shows the planner's own date; the late note only where the plan bought it after the target.
- **Dated planner**: `budgetDeadlineFor` sends the stages to `plannerDeadlineLocal` (lane 1): stages in day order, each by its day with what earlier stages left of every pack's limits and their spare items; a stage that can't make its day is planned for its earliest; a `late` stage (pages the plain plan knows can't make the target, no date of their own) last, bought the cheapest way after the target. Returns the joined plan (one row per offer) and `parts[]` = { day, ok, earliest, total, done, late, picks, trades } (each part's own packs and trades).
- **Free now inside Budgets** (owner, and users / visitors when Admin → Visibility → "Free now (Can do now)" is on (`visitorCan("cando")`, PR #183, 15 Nov 2026; #179 had tied it to the owner only and the switch did nothing), replaces the Can do now page / button): a tile under the total (`cdaTileHtml`, `data-cdatile`, "N upgrades · N of M goals") opens `BUDGET_SHEET = "cando"` (`cdaSheetInner`: My order | Best value, upgrade order sheet `cdaOrderSheet`, "Include this week's packs", YOUR GOALS first with their states, then every page's card, single upgrades left out with a note where their items went, Get more → that page's Pack cost; texts `cdb_*`, `cda_*`). `CDA_PAGES` (Chief Gear, Charms, Heroes Gears per ticked set, Pets, T12, T11, Research Center, Experts Level / Research, Islands (Backpack only), Shining City; Pet Refine left out since 20 Nov 2026, owner: refining is luck, so no "can do" for it) each `build()` their tracks from their own loaders (`cdaBuildPage`, `cdaBarHave` = the page bar's amounts, chests, trades); one shared Backpack plan (`cdaPlan` → `reachPlan`), built once per Budgets visit (`cdaEnsure`, `CDA_BUILD_P`, cleared by `goToTab`), drawn by `cdaBudgetsAfterDraw` → `cdaFillBudgets` (tile, sheet, goal chips). Settings in calc_state `cando_pages` = { off, skip, packs }.
- **Marks on planner pages** (same switch as Free now, `visitorCan("cando")`, since PR #183): after a page draws, `mkCompute` works out the whole Backpack for that page alone (same tracks / Backpack / trades as Free now; Heroes Gears = the set shown) and `mkApply` puts a mark in each slot (`MK_SLOTS`: Chief Gear, Chief Charm, Heroes Gears, Pets, T12, T11, Research Center, Experts Level / Research, Islands, Skin Gallery; not Pet Refine, owner 20 Nov 2026): green `.mk.full` "✓ 16.8" = reaches the target, blue `.mk.part` "◑ now 16.1" = part way, none = can't go up. Tap → `.mk-pop` (placePop: how far, what stops it, `mkOpenBudgets` → Budgets → Free now); the title's (i) explains them (`mkInfo`); texts `mk_*`. Redone on change (`mkSoon`).

The wiki gives only the max values; every Gen's star table is the previous one × a fixed number (Gen 16 = 0.926 × Gen 17 at all 30 steps), so Gen 18 = Gen 17 × 2471.97 / 2301.84, step 30 set to the wiki's 2,471.97 (step 1 = 340.86); shards per step as every Gen (totals 10 / 40 / 115 / 300 / 600, matching the wiki); widget 61.75% per level. Unlock shards (owner, 17 Nov 2026: the 30 star steps are the same for every hero, 1,065 shards, plus 10 to unlock = 1,075): `heroShardPlan` adds `HERO_UNLOCK_SHARDS` (10) before the first step for a hero that isn't unlocked yet (not in the roster and no saved `player_heroes` row; not when set to Later); the row carries `unlock`, and `need`, `cum` (star mini targets), `next` and `reach` include it. **VIP packs + Natalia / Jeronimo (owner, 17 Nov 2026, from the owner's game screenshots; real pack tables since option A, split in three the same day):** three `pack_tables` rows of the owner, Used for Hero shards & widgets, shown and edited on the Packs page like any pack (amounts, prices, Used for, schedule, Count in Budgets). They were added live on 17 Nov 2026 right after PR #200 merged (owner's OK; the SQL is in the PR #200 description). **"Natalia VIP Packs"** (`b90d2374-33f5-46ad-9d6c-ce7fca9faa26`, VIP 1–6, $4.99 / 9.99 / 19.99 / 49.99 / 99.99 / 99.99 → Natalia Shards 20 / 40 / 80 / 180 / 360 / 375) and **"Jeronimo VIP Packs"** (`ead4cb05-be06-48be-bc3a-bd131b850be9`, VIP 7–12, $19.99 / 49.99 / 49.99 / 49.99 / 99.99 / 99.99 → Jeronimo Shards 75 / 150 / 150 / 150 / 280 / 300), group "Heroes packs": one row per VIP level, any order, each once (reset "once"), plus N × 60 minutes of Research / Troop / Construction Speedups and 500 gems a dollar (Hero XP and the gold item aren't in them: the site doesn't count them, owner). Each `packs_links` entry has `vipRows` (the VIP level of each row: [1 … 6] / [7 … 12]): `packOffersFor` drops rows above the tier picked on the VIP page (`vipRowLevel`, `vipTierNow()` = calc_state `vip_tier`; no tier = none), so Budgets / My Purchases never offer them (the Packs page table still shows every row); the tier is in `packBuiltSig` and `packTableGroups`' key. **"Natalia Reward Pack"** (`33aaeaf0-9141-4bdb-a391-886b5e6b85ed`, was "First Purchase Reward"), group "Triggered packs" but with NO trigger, start date or state-day lock (owner: on sale from day 1): the game gives Natalia for any first purchase (cheapest = a $0.99 Daily Deals row), two $0.99 steps bought in order (buy in order, once): step 1 = 10 Natalia Shards (her unlock), step 2 (the next day or later) = 10 Natalia Shards; Hero XP / keys / gems / books not counted; the planner keeps the order, not the one-day gap, and plans it from today. Its entry has `firstBuy: true` (`packIsFirstBuy`; also on `packLinkFor`'s link, so its Packs row reads "● on now", `pkbWhen`): `budgetForPage("hero_shards")` drops it unless Natalia still needs unlocking (`vipFirstBuyOpen`, from `HERO_PLAN_LAST`). The "Triggered packs" group only reads `link.trigger` for waiting / expert levels, so a pack there without one is a normal one-time pack. With it Natalia's packs give exactly her 1,075. Tested with the tables mocked: VIP 12 with Natalia + Jeronimo targets $656.86 (the same as one table); VIP 5 → Natalia rows 1–5 + the reward pack; no tier → the reward pack only ($1.98); Natalia unlocked → no reward pack. Pack items: "<hero> Shards" for the VIP pack heroes and every hero read so far are in the Packs column item list and name match (`packHeroShardNames`, in `packResourceChoices` / `packResourceByName`, not in Pack Resources); a hero's shards / widgets column header shows the hero's face in its corner (`.pk-hface`); the Packs page reads the heroes when a table has such a column and draws again once they're known. Natalia / Jeronimo (`VIP_PACK_HEROES`, `vipPackHero`) only get shards from these packs: they need the 10 unlock shards like the Hall of Heroes hero (same conditions), General Hero Shards never go to them (`fromGeneral` 0) and `heroShardBudget` gives them no General shard / Marks of Valor trades (nor counts them for the marks' weekly split or the VIP daily shards). Rules "Hero shards per star step (fallback)" (`shardChart`) is no longer shown in Admin (`hidden: true`, filtered out of the Rules hub; gone from the Heroes Rules chip); `HERO_SHARD_CHART` stays the silent fallback and a saved value still applies. Undo the data: the UNDO part of the PR #200 SQL (delete the three `pack_tables` rows and their `packs_links` entries, or copy back `packs_links` from owner calc_state `backup_vip_packs_20261117`).

**Natalia / Jeronimo stars from Heroes Special Skills (owner, 17 Nov 2026: "if they're maxed, all packs related to them are bought"):** `heroShardBudget` reads calc_state `hero_stars_jero` / `hero_stars_natalia` (`__shared__` 0–5, keys from `SPECIAL_HERO_META`) and passes `special` = { Jeronimo, Natalia: stars } to `heroShardPlan`; for a VIP pack hero its current level is the higher of those whole stars (step = stars × 6) and its `player_heroes` stars, in a roster slot or not (owner), and stars > 0 = unlocked (no 10 unlock shards). At 5★ they need no shards, so Budgets / My Purchases / Pack cost never plan their VIP packs, and `vipFirstBuyOpen` is false (no Natalia Reward Pack); `HERO_PLAN_LAST`, hero goals and stat gain follow (all read its rows). Changing stars there (tap or Max / Reset) calls `budgetInvalidate()`. Visitors use their own saved stars. One value in both places (owner, 17 Nov 2026): the Dashboard roster stars and the Heroes Special Skills stars of Natalia / Jeronimo are the same hero, so any change on either side (roster star tap / − +, special star tap, Max, Reset; up or down) sets the other: special N → roster row N (step N × 6; a row already on that whole star keeps its pieces) through `vipRosterSet` (`patchRowQueued`; a new row only when the hero sits in a roster slot, never for heroes not tracked), roster step s → special floor(s / 6) through `vipSpecialSet` (`saveCalcState`, other keys of the state kept; the Dashboard's `HERO_STARS` follows). Old data where they differ reads as the higher of the two (`rosterStarsOf`, the roster's `heroShardPlan` call gets `special: vipSpecialStarsMap()`, Dashboard `HERO_STARS`, the Special Skills page) until either side is saved. Helpers `VIP_HERO_STAR_KEY`, `vipSpecialStars`. Packs page "done" marks (owner, 17 Nov 2026: "like a trigger pack: when I max the hero it won't suggest it again"): the Natalia Reward Pack card / details sheet Now box show "✓ Natalia unlocked" (`pkd_first_done`) once she is unlocked (same rule: special stars > 0, a `player_heroes` row or a roster slot; `vipFirstBuyDone`), and Natalia / Jeronimo VIP Packs show "✓ <hero> maxed" (`pkd_hero_maxed`) at 5★ (the higher of both star values; `vipHeroMaxed`, `vipPackHeroOf(tableId)` from the link's `vipRows` + "<hero> Shards" column); grey like a passed trigger pack, otherwise unchanged ("● on now" / nothing). `pkbWhen(lk, id)` returns `done` / `doneText`; the Packs page awaits `vipHeroesCheck()` (reads heroes / player_heroes / roster only when a special star value isn't already enough; `VIP_TRACK`, `vipHeroTrack`). Texts English only. Widgets (owner): Natalia / Jeronimo get widgets planned only while in a Dashboard roster slot (from the roster's widget level, like any roster hero); as Budgets hero targets / extra only, widget need 0 (no Forge / widget packs). Other heroes and the Dashboard roster cards (which don't pass `special`) unchanged.

**Weekly cards count down (owner, 21 Nov 2026):** the "days left" typed for a stacking card (My Purchases → Weekly cards, the pack's own settings box) used to be saved without a date and counted from today every time, so it never went down (28 typed on Oct 27 still said 28 / "Running until Dec 18" on Nov 21). Now calc_state `my_cards_active` = { tableId: { d, on: "yyyy-mm-dd" } } (`on` = the game's UTC day it was typed, `packActiveEntry(n)`), and `packActiveDays(id)` = max(0, d − days since `on`), so `packLinkFor`, the box's value, the Running until / Not running lines, the planner, Budgets, My Purchases and Pack cost all count down; at 0 the card is not running (the entry stays; "Reset all" and typing 0 still remove it, `packActiveDaysSaved` = the saved d). Old entries without `on` get the day `my_cards_active` was last saved (`CALC_STATE_UPDATED`, stamped on load by `packCardsActiveStamp`, kept on the next save), else today; `loadCalcState`'s single-row fallback now reads `updated_at` too. The plan fingerprint already has today's day. Owner's data on Nov 21: both FC cards 3 days left, "Running until Nov 23 · next buy Nov 24"; Nov 25: not running.

**Pack sheet speedups in hours (17 Nov 2026):** in the Packs page's pack details sheet (`pkbChip` / `pkbChips`), items stored in minutes (names ending "(minutes)", e.g. the VIP packs' Research / Troop / Construction Speedups) show as a time (`pkbMinTxt`: "1h", "1.5h", "30m", over 99 h "5d 4h"); the chip's title keeps the full minutes. Display only: data, the editor's boxes and totals, prices and plans unchanged. Counted speedups ("Speedup 1h" × 4) stay counts.

**Trades window + daily trades (owner, 18 Nov 2026; steps 58 + 61, design C of step 53):**
- **Daily trade limits**: a trade rule can have `perDay` (trades a day; it wins over the weekly `max`). `tradeRatesFor` turns it into `max` = perDay × 7 (every later week) and `wk1` = perDay × the days left in this game week (Mon–Sun, UTC, today included, `tradeDaysLeftInWeek()`); `tradeWeekCap(r, i)` (lots allowed in week i, 0 = this week), `tradeCapLots(r, weeks)`, `tradeWeeksForLots(r, lots)` are what the planner, Pack cost, the free trades and Can do / page marks use. The planner worker is handed the page's day (`today` in its message → `TRADE_TODAY`; `tradeDaysLeftInWeek` is in `PLANNER_FN_NAMES`), the plan cache key carries the days left, and the Budgets plan fingerprint is `v: 5` (phones work the plans out again once). Admin → Rules & Timeline → trades table has a "Per day (0 = none; wins over weekly)" column (English only, like the other column labels).
- **Steel → FC Shard** (T12 / T11 built-in trades): 5,000 Steel = 1 FC Shard, 20 a day; Fire Crystal → FC Shard is now 500 a day (was 500 a week).
- **Trades window** (the Trades sheet on phones, the side card on PC, every planner page): title "⇄ Trades" (`trade_title` "Trades", was "Trade items"), the "Use trades in Pack cost" switch (`trd_use`, same `data-tradeapply`), "Still short after trades" chips (`trd_short`, "✓ Nothing short"), then two boxes: (1) **Trade this week** (blue): one line per trade to make now ("Trade 1,600 FC → +2,080 FC Shard", "N trades left this week" / "limit reached", a total "+N … from trades"); a rule with `perDay` gets two small bars, Day and Week (`.trd-mbr` / `.trd-bar`, amber when full); (2) **Trade rules** (grey): one card per item you get with its rules ("10 Fire Crystal → 13 · 500/day"), no short / enough status; Hide / Show (`g1-fold`), folded at first (`BAR_DEFAULT_HIDDEN`, `barFoldDefault`), saved on the account for all pages in calc_state `bar_hidden` key `trd:rules` both ways (true = hidden, false = shown; nothing saved = hidden). The calculator stays open under them (`trd_calc`). Texts `trd_*` in all 16 lang files; `trade_apply_short`, `trade_card_line2`, `trade_none`, `trade_after`, `trade_max_week`, `trade_capped`, `trade_calc` removed (and the old `pc_free_count` leftovers) (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 108).
- Live data change (18 Nov 2026, owner's OK): owner calc_state `trade_rates.rates.t12_research` Fire Crystal → FC Shard perDay 500 / max 3,500 and Steel → FC Shard perDay 20 / max 140. Backup: owner calc_state `backup_trade_rates_20261118` (undo: copy it back).

**Pages open on what still has work (owner, 18 Nov 2026; step 62):** entering a page (from another tab or on load, not a redraw) starts on the first card / troop / set below its target. `render()` sets `PAGE_ENTRY_SEQ` (its seq when the tab changes, −1 on a same-page redraw); a page asks `pageEntered(key)` once per entry, `pagePickIdx(key, names, fresh, ...works)` gives the card to show (the person's pick this visit first, then on entry the first name passing each test in turn, else the card shown before / the first), `pagePickSet(key, name)` records a pick by tap / swipe / tab (`PAGE_PICK` = { name, user }; troop switches / the Heroes Gears set in `PAGE_USER_PICKED`, set by `wirePlxTroopSwitch` and the set dropdown). Experts Level / Experts Research: the first counted expert with work, else the first with work; Pets Level, Pet Refine: the first pet with work; Chief Charm: the first troop with a charm below its target (`PLX_TROOP.charm`); Heroes Gears: a set with nothing left moves to the first set with work (`heroGearSetLeft`), then the first troop with a track below its target; T12 / T11: the first troop with research time left; Research Center: the first group with research time left (Shared first, as the tabs; none → Infantry as before). Redraws (Max / Reset, level changes) keep the card shown; leaving and coming back keeps the person's pick for the visit. Owner decisions (18 Nov 2026): Chief Gear stays as it was (no scroll to its first piece: it is one page); the research pages pick the troop. No new texts.

**Chief Gear Legendary T7 / T8 (owner, 18–19 Nov 2026; step 64):** 40 rows after Legendary T6 ★3 in the "Chief Gear" progression table (levels 151–190) from the owner's own prediction (checked 18 Nov 2026): +1.7% a step (T7 263.50%, T7 ★3 289.00%, T8 297.50%, T8 ★3 323.00%); Alloy / Polish / Design 72,000 / 660 / 110 a step, +4,000 / 30 / 5 at each new star or tier (100,000 / 870 / 145 for T8 ★3; the landing row of a star keeps the step before's price); Amber 20, 24 from T7 ★3 · 1; Power +40,000 on the first step after a star, +41,000 on the others (T7 6,324,000, T8 7,140,000, T8 ★3 7,752,000). One piece T6 ★3 → T8 ★3 = 3,440,000 Alloy, 30,600 Polish, 5,100 Design, 880 Amber. Set bonus rows `chief_gear_set_bonus` Legendary T7 27% / T8 30% (T4 18 / T5 21 / T6 24 go up by 3); a tier with no set bonus row counts as the highest known tier below it (`computeChiefGearSetBonus`, `tierIdxAt`), so a new tier never takes the bonus away. They come as **Upcoming** (site_config `upcoming_levels.progression_rows` = their 40 fixed ids): pickable as targets (amber, "· Upcoming"), Max / the caps / Dashboard max stop at T6 ★3 (`chiefGearCapIdx` via `releasedLastIdx`), hidden from visitors until shown in Admin → Visibility. Code: `chiefGearLook` gives "t7" / "t8" (pictures `icons/chief-gear/<piece>-t7.webp` / `-t8.webp`, from the owner's pictures, the tile's own bottom edge kept), `CHIEF_GEAR_TIER_SHORT` "Red T7" / "Red T8". **The data is added by the SQL in the step 64 PR after the merge, with the owner's OK** (also scratchpad `t7t8.sql`): backup in owner calc_state `backup_chief_gear_t7t8_20261119` (the old `upcoming_levels`, the set bonus rows, the new ids), then the 40 rows, the 2 set bonus rows, the Upcoming ids; UNDO = delete those 40 + 2 ids and take the 40 out of `upcoming_levels`. When the game releases them: switch the Red T7 / Red T8 groups off in Admin → Visibility → Chief Gear and correct any numbers.

**Upcoming in Admin → Visibility (owner, 18–19 Nov 2026; step 64):** Visibility has one fold per part: Chief Gear, Chief Charm, Heroes Gears, Experts, Pets, all the same design (`vupFoldHtml`, `vupWholeFold`; picture, "N upcoming", then two switch rows and one Upcoming switch per group / expert / pet). **Visitors see upcoming** = ui_visibility `upcoming:<chief_gear|charm|hero_gear|experts|pets>`, hidden by default (`UI_HIDDEN_DEFAULTS`): while off, visitors don't get the upcoming rows at all (`upcomingCut` in each loader: Chief Gear, Charm, Heroes Gears; lists, Set all, Budgets, Pack cost; a saved level past them reads as the last level left, `charmShownLevel`, the saved state isn't rewritten) and an upcoming expert / pet is hidden like a hidden one (`isHiddenFromVisitors`). The owner always sees everything, tagged Upcoming. **Count upcoming** = site_config `upcoming_count` = { part: true } (`upcomingCountOn`): on = that part's upcoming things count like released ones for everyone (Max, Dashboard max, caps, Budgets, Free now; upcoming experts / pets start ticked), still tagged Upcoming (`charmIdxUpcoming`, `heroIdxUpcoming`); off / missing = as before. **Level groups** (`vupParts`, owner's wording: "a group always ends at the level itself"): Chief Gear one group per tier ending at its last whole star (Red T7 = T6 ★3.1 … T7 ★3); Chief Charm one per whole level (Lv 17 = 16.1 … 17, a new Lv 19 = 18.1 … 19); Heroes Gears Mastery 1 – 20, Enhancement 0 – 100, red gear (Mithril) 101 – 200 (`VUP_PHASE_ENDS`), levels added later (also 20.1, 100.1, 200.1 sub-steps) form one "NEW" group; tapping a Heroes Gears group opens its levels: "Split after Lv N" adds a phase end, "Join with <previous>" removes one (site_config `upcoming_phases` = { ends: { mastery, enhance } }, `vupSplitSheet`; the joined group keeps the earlier day). A group switch saves its row ids into `upcoming_levels` (`vupSetGroup`). Experts / Pets: whole items only (experts.upcoming, `upcoming_pets`), no level groups. **State Timeline stops**: switching a group / expert / pet on adds a Rules → Unlocks row with `auto` (`lv:<table>:<first id>` / `<kind>:<name>`) and an empty day (`STATE_UNLOCKS_WAIT`: display only, never locks or changes anything; Admin shows it with "auto · from Upcoming" and a "set day ›" button, the 📅 chip in Visibility goes there); switching off removes it while its day is empty; a split / join renames it (`vupRelabel`). The State Timeline shows stops with a day on that day and the rest (empty days, upcoming experts / pets whose day has passed or isn't set, upcoming levels no stop covers, `tlUpcomingLevels`) in a dashed "Coming later" card (`tl_later`), with the part's picture (`tlAutoIcon`) and a "New pet" / "New expert" tag; for visitors only what that part's "Visitors see upcoming" shows (`unlockShown`). At merge: Abyssal Shelldragon (an upcoming pet) disappears for visitors from Pets Level / Pet Refine / the timeline until Visitors see upcoming is switched on for Pets. Texts `vup_*`, `rl_auto_sub`, `rl_set_day`, `up_auto_*`, `tl_later*`, `tl_new_*`, `vis_*_up_desc` (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 112).

**Pet Refine marks in Pack cost and Trades (owner, 19–20 Nov 2026; steps 68 + 70):** (1) Pet Refine has no Free now card and no page marks (owner, 20 Nov 2026: refining is luck): it left `CDA_PAGES` and `MK_SLOTS`; goals on Pet Refine get no Free now status. (2) Pack cost on Pet Refine has a switch under the plan boxes "Buy the marks as Advanced | Common" (`.pca-mk`, `data-pcamarks`, each side with its own plan's price, worked out with `PET_BUYAS_FORCE` set to the other side): packs that sell either (`packMarksChoiceTables`, e.g. Custom Pet Chest) are bought as the picked one (`petMarksBuyAs()`; `packPreferAdvancedMarks` keeps only Common when "common"; the pick is in `packGroupsFor` / `packTableGroups` keys), saved per person in calc_state `pet_refine_marks.buyAs` ("adv" default / "common"); the pack row shows the Advanced ones and "choose Advanced in the game" (`pca_mk_game`); a green "Same price: 1 Advanced = 8 Common in this pack (2 or 16 … 40 or 320)" line only when both plans cost the same (`pca_mk_same`, `pca_mk_or`). Advanced Marks count as needed items there. (3) 500 Gems → 1 Common Mark is now a trade on one switch, "Trade Gems for Common Marks" (`marks_gems_trade`, `.trd-gems`, same `data-markspackgems` and saved `pet_refine_marks.packGems`, now on unless set to false, `petGemsTradeOn`), inside the Trades window (it shows even with no other trade), covering the Backpack's and the packs' gems; off = the trade is left out (`tradeRatesFor("pets_refine")`), on = shown in Trades and in Pack cost's Trades card; it also sets that page's "Use trades in Pack cost" (gems are its only trade) and redraws the page (`REFINE_REFRESH`). The old "Pack gems count as marks" line under the boxes is gone (`marks_pack_gems*` texts now unused). (4) The BACKPACK row on Pet Refine: ⚙ Calculator (icon only), ⇄ Trades (icon only under 380 px) and the Pack cost price (`.rf-bpb`); they fit at 360 / 393 / 585 px. (5) Every page's Pack cost Trades box has even padding and equal space above / below (`.pca-tc`). `LANG_FILES_VERSION` 114.
