**Pages open on the last done card (owner, 20 Nov 2026; step 71):** when nothing on a page has work left, entering it opens the LAST card that is done (at its target, with some progress) instead of the first: Experts Level / Research (last counted expert with a level / path above 0), Pets Level, Pet Refine, Chief Charm (last troop with a charm above 0), Heroes Gears (the last set in use, `heroGearSetUsed`, then its last troop), T12 / T11 (last troop with research), Research Center (last group with research). `pagePickIdx(key, names, fresh, ...works, { done })` takes an optional last argument `{ done: predicate(s) }` (tried in order, last match wins; none = the last card) through `pageLastDone`; work first and the person's own pick this visit are unchanged. No new texts.

**Phone Menu: new Hub cards, bottom bar and Marketplace (owner approved, 19–20 Nov 2026; step 72; PC menu unchanged):** Hub cards come from `mmModel()` (built from the real menu `#topnavGroups`, so Admin renames, moved pages and pages hidden for visitors follow): **Overview** with a **PLANNING** part (Marketplace, Budgets, My Purchases), **Gears** with an **Experts** and a **Pets** tile (chips Level / Research and Level / Refine), then every other group as before; no "N to go" bar on the cards any more. `MM_PAIRS` = pages shown as one tile (Marketplace = Packs + My packs; Experts; Pets): the tile opens the page used last (localStorage `mm_last_<market|experts|pets>`, noted in `pageDrawn` by `mmPairNote`), the chips open each page; a pair with only one page shown (visitors) is a plain tile. Helpers `mmTileHtml`, `mmRowsHtml` (plain tiles 3 a row, 2 a row for 1 / 2 / 4, chip tiles 2 a row), `mmCardHtml(card)`, `mmCardTabs`. **Bottom bar** = Home · Upgrade (Gears with Experts / Pets, Research) · Plan (Overview without the Dashboard: Backpack, State Timeline, Planning; the Pack calendar lights Plan) · Bonus (every other card) · **Me** (`mbCards(sec)`; `MB_SECS`): Me is a sheet (`mbMeHtml`): account card (initial, Commander name with ✎ for guests too, same save as the top bar's account panel; guests get "Guest", a sign-in line and Sign in / Create account), Language (list folds open, `MB_LANG_OPEN`), Menu style (Hub | Bottom bar), Admin (owner), About / Contact / Privacy, Log out; the Me button shows the person's initial (`.mb-av`). **Marketplace switch**: on Packs and My packs (every screen size) the page title reads "Marketplace" with an "All packs | My packs (N)" switch under it (`pgSwSync`, `PG_SW_PAIRS`, `.pg-sw`, `data-pgswgo` → `goToTab`; both tab ids / routes stay; only when both pages are shown). Texts `mb_bonus`, `mb_me`, `me_*`, `mm_marketplace`, `mm_chip_*`, `pgsw_*`; removed (no longer used): `ms_note`, `ms_pc_note`, `ms_hub_d`, `ms_bar_d`, `mb_packs`, `mb_more` (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 116).

**Experts and Pets: one page each (owner approved the final mock-ups, 20 Nov 2026; step 73):**
- **Two tabs, one page:** Experts = `expertspage` (Level) + `expertsresearchpage` (Research), Pets = `petsdata` (Level) + `refinepage` (Refine), titled "Experts" / "Pets" (`SIDE_PAIRS`, `sideTitle`, T keys `mrg_*`). A two-way switch sits on the card's top edge (`sideSwitchHtml`, `.crs-sw`, `data-sidego`); flipping (`sideGo`) keeps the same card (its pick is copied), adds no history step and is drawn as the same page. A visitor with one side hidden gets no switch.
- **Which side opens** (owner, 20 Nov 2026): entering the page (Menu, link, `last_tab`, reload, either old tab) opens Level, unless every Level card shown (counted or not) is already at its released max (Experts: level and every skill; Pets: level), then Research / Refine (`sideEntryTab`, in `render()` when it isn't a redraw). A flip during the visit keeps the side. Which card opens is unchanged (`pagePickIdx`).
- **Carousel:** the card follows the finger and snaps to the next / previous one (`wireIndexSwipe`, `CRS_ENTER` slide-in), the neighbours' edges show at both sides as tall as the card (`--ch`), dots under it (`.crs-dots`); vertical scroll and pinch zoom are left alone.
- **One card header on both sides** (`expertHeadHtml`, `petHeadHtml`): picture, name, talent (experts) / Upcoming, one stat line (Level: the level stat; Research: "Lethality +0.00% · Lancers only"; Pets Level: "ATK · DEF +x% · of max +y%"; Refine: "Refine 0 of 6 stats at max", `xh_rf_at_max`), Max + Count at the top right with each side's own data attributes (Pets Level got a per-pet Max, `data-petmaxone`: level and target to the highest open level). Gone: Research's big picture and footer Count / Max, Refine's tier row. Since step 76 (20 Nov 2026) there is no Count on any card: Max only (an Experts Research card gets a "Not counted" tag when Count research is off).
- **One Troops Stats header on both sides** (`xhCardHtml`, `xhExpDraw`, `xhPetsDraw`; worked out from the saved states, the side shown passes its live copy): the legend "● Level ● Research / Refine" in the title row (`.xh-leg`), Infantry / Lancer / Marksman tabs (`PLX_TROOP` `xh_exp` / `xh_pets`). Experts: 4 boxes ATK / DEF / LETH / HP = Level's old "All" (`xhExpLevel`: every counted expert's level stat + Romulus's / Gareth's troop skills) + that troop's Research (`xhExpResearch`: the general experts + the troop's own expert, `expertsForTroop`), a two-colour bar and the two parts under it; under them a folded "Event bonuses" line ("all max ✓" or amber "N not at max · <event>", `XH_EV_OPEN`) that opens a table SvS / Foundry / Frostfire Mine / Tundra Trade × ATK DEF LETH HP (each event's own skill + talent vs its max, "—" where it gives nothing). Pets: ATK / DEF from Pets Level (blue edge) and LETH / HP from Pet Refine for the picked troop (gold edge). Replaces Experts Level's event tabs, Research's "All troops" tabs, Pets Level's 2 boxes and Refine's troop switch. T keys `xh_*`.
- **One Hide / Show per page:** `g1TopKey` = `top:experts` / `top:pets` in calc_state `bar_hidden`; until first tapped it follows the old per-side keys (`top:expertspage` …, `g1FoldVal`), which are dropped once it is tapped.
- **The card doesn't move on a flip:** both sides' top boxes are drawn the same height (Backpack boxes one size on phones: Experts `min-height` 100 px, Pets 93 px, non-sliding rows padded like sliding ones), and `sideHold` keeps the switch tab exactly where it was while the new side draws (every frame and on each content change: builds the top box at once, grows the box with a min-height or scrolls, undoes its own scroll first; stops after ~1.5 s still or a touch / wheel / key; fold taps clear its min-heights, `sideHoldStop`). `render()` skips its scroll restore while a hold runs. Measured on the owner's data: 0 px at 393 and 585, Backpack open / hidden / events open, at the top and half way.
- **Backpack:** Experts Level = one row, Speedups box first, then Affinity, Books, General Sigils and every expert's sigils (the old "Expert sigils" fold and the research time line above the boxes are gone); every sigil box (both sides) shows the "<Name> Sigils" item picture, General Sigils its own (`expertSigilTilesInner`). Experts Research = the sigil boxes. Pets Level: taller boxes with 40 px pictures and a ⚙ calculator button (it flips to Refine and opens the calculator there, `REFINE_CALC_PENDING`); Refine: ⚙ + Trades + Pack cost (gems switch is in Trades since step 70). Since step 76 both Experts sides draw this same row (see "Step 76").
- **Cards:** Pets Level shows 4 Backpack-style boxes (need / −short or ✓, `fillPetRes` from `BAR_EFF_OWNED.pets`); Experts Research has the sigils strip at the card's bottom (have / need, short, paths to go, General Sigils left, `er_sig_*`).
- **Budgets needs of the side not shown** (coordinator / owner, 20 Nov 2026): entering the page on either side also saves the other side's Budgets needs (with its mini targets and goal steps) from the saved states, quietly after the draw (`sideBgNeeds` → `sideBgNeedsPets` / `sideBgNeedsExperts`, same sums as the pages; Experts Level also saves `experts_level_sigil_need`), so Budgets never uses old Research / Refine needs. Change these together with the pages' own sums.
- **Page Max removed** (owner, 20 Nov 2026) on both pages, both sides; each card keeps its own Max. The shared Max / Reset helpers stay for the other pages.
- Removed T keys (no longer used): `plx_all`, `pets_summed_desc`, `tier_suffix`, `exp_all_d`, `exp_svs_d`, `exp_foundry_d`, `exp_frost_d`, `exp_tundra_d`, `exp_tabs_note`, `exp_ev_off`, `experts_research_desc`, `experts_sigils_summary`, `rf_stats_info`, `er_paths_to_go`, `er_path_to_go_1`, `exp_have_n`, `exp_general_n`, `exp_sig_short`. `LANG_FILES_VERSION` 117.
- **Experts Level card, level part = L2 halves** (owner approved, 20 Nov 2026; step 78): a level not at max is one slim bordered box (`.exk-lvr.exk-lv2`): line 1 "LEVEL [now] → [target]" (`.exk-lv2-r1`, the same `select[data-expcur|expdes]`), line 2 two equal halves (`.exk-g2.exk-lv2-r2`) = the same `data-expbox` buttons (Compass / Affinity, the expert's Sigils: picture, need, red "−N" short with "N short" as its title, or a green ✓; "✓ done" when nothing is needed). About 151 → 84 px at 393 px. Under 380 px smaller text / pictures so "558.8K −349.7K" fits. The maxed line, pop-ups, saves and the rest of the card are unchanged. No new texts.

**Research pages Backpack order + Step 76: one Experts Backpack, stat sources, no Count ticks (owner approved the previews, 20 Nov 2026; steps 75 + 76, one PR):**
- **Research pages' Backpack order** (step 75): T12 / T11 / Research Center boxes go Speedups, Refined FC, FC Shards, Fire Crystal, Steel, Meat, Wood, Coal, Iron (only those the page uses); the Custom Resource Chest boxes show only while they cover a short resource (after the resources), FC Chest / FC Crate stay last. Plans and totals unchanged.
- **Speedups box size** (owner): in a sliding Backpack row the shared Speedups box (Experts and the research pages, `.bs-aff.bs-spd`) is as wide as the row minus Affinity (1.5 boxes + 3 px), Books, General Sigils and the three gaps, so on Experts Level the visible row ends exactly at General Sigils' right edge (585 px: 180 px); never under 100 px (393 px phones). Pure CSS (`flex-basis:max(100px, calc(…))`), so it follows resizes; the research pages use the same formula and look the same. Inside: the Affinity layout (two pictures, the times under them, the "Speedups 36d 2h · 138d short" line on up to 2 lines). On PC (no sliding) it keeps its 2 grid cells.
- **One Experts Backpack** (both sides draw the same row, `expBpDraw(o)`, moved out of the Level page): Speedups, Affinity, Books, General Sigils, then each expert's sigils (item pictures). Each sigil box shows what you have against Level + Research together (`expBpNeed(lv, rs, inv, shown, lvOn, rsOn)`: Level from the page or `expLevelNeedsFrom(lv, un, skillRows, affRows)` (the same sums as Experts Level / `sideBgNeedsExperts`, on a copy), Research from the page or `expResearchSigNeed(D)`; `xhExpData` now also returns `skillRows` / `affRows`); General Sigils = what the experts' own leave over both sides. Sigil boxes are tappable (`data-expsig`, `expertSigilTilesInner(…, tap)`): the amount pop-up with Needed / You have / Short and "Level N · Research M" (`exp_sig_split`), Save / Use Backpack like the other boxes. The Research side's row starts slid to General Sigils (`data-bsstart` on the row, done by `bsSlideFit` once per place, `BS_START_DONE`). Budgets / Pack cost / `experts_level_sigil_need` are unchanged (each side still records only its own needs: no double count). `expertSigilTilesHtml` was removed.
- **Stat source pop-up = design B**: tapping a Troops Stats box on either Experts side (`.xh-cb[data-xhsrc]`, also Enter / Space) opens a bottom sheet (`#xsSheet.modal-overlay.xs-ov`, centred window on PC; ✕, tap outside, Esc; closed by `render()`): troop + stat, total "of max · left", the Level / Research split bar and legend, then Expert levels / Skills / Research, each with a subtotal and one line per source (picture, name · source, now / max, bar, "✓ max" or "+N left"). Built from the header's own code: `xhExpLevel` now returns `src` (each expert's level and Romulus / Gareth skills, same sums as `now` / `max`), `xhExpResearch(D, troop, ignoreOff)` returns `per` (each expert's paths summed). `xhSrcSheetHtml`, `xhSrcOpen`, `XH_EXP_D`. Texts `xs_*`.
- **Count ticks removed** (owner): no "Count" on Experts Level / Research cards, Pets Level / Refine cards or the T11 / T12 settings. `unlockedFor` ignores saved true / false (state age decides: open in the person's state = counted, not yet = not counted; upcoming experts / pets as before) and only obeys `"off"`; `RESEARCH_PAGE_COUNTED` = open in the state (`stateNotYet("page", k)`), calc_state `research_page_counted` ignored. `expertHeadHtml` / `petHeadHtml` take a `tag` instead of `count`.
- **"Count research" switch** (owner): in the Experts page title row on BOTH sides (`plxShellHtml` `headExtra`, `rsCountSwitchHtml`, `.exr-sw`), but it only changes Experts Research. Saved per person in calc_state `experts_research_unlocked` = `{ __off: true }` (off) or `{}` (on, the default); every reader passes that state through `rsUnNorm` (marks every expert `"off"`): the Experts header / pop-up, the Research page, `ensureExpertsResearchDataGlobal` (Dashboard + breakdown), Budgets stat gain, Free now, `sideBgNeedsExperts`. Off: research leaves the header's totals and max (its part struck through, legend "Research off"), the Dashboard and its breakdown, Budgets (research needs saved empty at once, `rsCountSet` → `sideBgNeedsExperts("experts_research")`) and Free now; the research cards are dimmed (`.exr-offc`) with a "Not counted" tag. On = as before.
- Removed T keys: `expert_count_tick`, `expert_count_tip`, `pet_count_tip`, `count_toward_dashboard`, `set_count`. New: `exr_*`, `exp_sig_split`, `exp_sig_gen_note`, `xs_*` (all 16 languages). `LANG_FILES_VERSION` 120.
- Tested (owner + visitor, 360 / 393 / 585 / 1280, vs origin/main): 0 errors, no sideways scroll; only Experts, Pets, T12, T11, Research Center differ; Dashboard and Budgets identical with the switch on; the card stays put on a side flip (0 px at 393 and 585).

**Set all window and the Heroes Gears window (owner, 20–21 Nov 2026; step 77):**
- **Set all = "B + A + Now settable"** (Chief Gear, Chief Charm, Heroes Gears): the page's "⇅ Set all" button (`setAllBtnHtml(attr)`: `data-cgsa` / `data-chsa` / `data-hgsa`) opens a bottom sheet in `#gearSheet` (`openSetAllSheet(o)`, `.sal-*` CSS): a strip of the pieces with a tick each (all ticked first; now → target per piece, amber when its Now changes; `data-saltick`), then NOW and TARGET with the same pickers as the page's piece window (Chief Gear tier / ★ / stage `cgPickRows`, Chief Charm level pictures + "Stage of Lv" `chPickRows`, Heroes Gears Mastery buttons + stage `hgMastRows` and the Enhance bar). Now starts on "Keep each piece" with green counts of how many ticked pieces sit at each value (`.sal-n`); a picked Now is set EXACTLY on the ticked pieces (also lower, like the old dropdown) and the target is pulled up to it; Target shows the shared target when every ticked piece has the same one, "Keep each target" appears once a target is picked. A summary line says what changes (`sal_sum_none` when nothing), then Cancel / "Apply to N pieces" (off until something changes). Chief Charm works per troop (that troop's 6 charms). Apply saves through each page's own save (calc_state `chief_gear`, `charm`, the Heroes Gears set key), nothing is saved before. Each track: { key, lab, cur(id), html(role, sel, lo, counts, api), pick(role, kind, val, sel, lo), wire?(box, api) }, picker buttons `data-salp="<track>|<role>|<kind>|<val>"`. No Max in the window.
- **Heroes Gears piece window** (`openHgSheet` → `drawSheet`): Mastery = H1 (Now and Target rows of whole levels 0 … 20 + a stage row for the sub-levels, `data-hgm`; tapping a piece's own whole level keeps its stage). Enhance = T1 (`enhGridHtml`, `wireEnhGrid`, `enhPaint`, `enhTicks`, `enhPct`, `enhAt`): one row per Now / Target (label + value, − bar +), a shared row of tick numbers (0 · 100 · 120 … 200, evenly spaced: 0 → 100 is one step, then 20s), gold flags over the bars for the milestones with their gain (`msFlags`, from `heroGearMilestones`; the stat only when every piece listed gets the same one), the hint `hg_enh_hint`. The bars are real controls: drag a handle (pointer events, touch and mouse, `touch-action:pan-y` so scrolling up / down still works; a cancelled drag puts the value back) or tap a bar = that row level by level, − / + = exactly one level (holding repeats, `wireHoldStep`), values paint at once and save when let go; a tapped tick number moves the row touched last (`HG_ENH_MEM`, Target when the window opens). Target never below Now; the upper limit is the table's last level, past the open cap amber. The milestone chips under the bars are gone; needs, Can do, ‹ ›, the stat line stay; "Done" is full width.
- **No Max in the upgrade windows** (owner, 21 Nov 2026: the page has its own Max): Chief Gear and Chief Charm windows lost their Target Max, Heroes Gears its section Max and "Max this piece"; the page-level Max / Reset buttons are unchanged.
- Old Set all CSS (`.sa-wrap`, `.sa-box`, `.sa-row`, `.cg-sabox` …), the Heroes Gears steppers' CSS (`.gs-stp`, `.lvchip`, `.hg-msc` …) and the T keys `current_upper`, `target_upper`, `hg_max_piece` removed; new `sal_*`, `hg_enh_hint` in all 16 lang files (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 121).

**Budgets page tiles in your own order (owner picked A, 22 Nov 2026):** a line "Pages · ⇅ Arrange" sits right above the page tiles, under the goal-dates rail (`.b3-pgw` box with `arrangeHostAttr("budget_pages")`, `.b3-pgh` + `arrangeBtnHtml(B3_ARR_KEY)`, hint `.b3-pghint` shown only while on). Arrange on: each tile shows a ⋮⋮ handle at its top left (`dsHandleHtml("data-b3drag")`), the tick boxes are hidden (back on Done), a tile tap opens nothing, and tiles are dragged in the 2-a-row grid with `dragSort(h, { arrange: B3_ARR_KEY, row: ".b3-pt", grid: true })`; the drop saves the new order per person in calc_state `budget_page_order` = { order: [calcKeys] } (`BUDGET_PAGE_ORDER`, loaded with Budgets' other states, saved without waiting) and shows "✓ Order saved" (`dsSaved`). `b3PageOrdered(list)`: saved pages first in that order, pages not in it keep their built-in place after them; the same order on PC. Display only: plan, prices, ticks, totals unchanged. T keys `b3_pages`, `b3_arrange_hint` (all 16 lang files, `LANG_FILES_VERSION` 124).

**Best upgrades advisor (owner's picks 22–23 Nov 2026; owner only, Admin → Visibility → Visitor access "Best upgrades", `visitorCan("bestupgrades")`, default off):** a Planning page (tab `bestupgrades`, menu button `data-access="bestupgrades" data-ownertab`, picture 📈 through `QUICK_EMOJI` like the Backpack's 🎒; visitors are sent to the Dashboard) and a "Best next here" card on Chief Gear, Chief Charm, T12, T11, Research Center, Heroes Gears, Pets Level, Experts Level / Research, Islands and Skin Gallery (`ADV_CARD_TABS`, `advCardInject` after `render()`, under the top box / stats card; "See all ›" = that page's steps ranked, `advOpenPageSheet`). All code is the "Best upgrades advisor" section of app.js (names `adv*` / `ADV_*`).
- **Item values ("the new way")**: each item is valued from one chosen step of the owner's pack tables, read live (`ADV_PACK_SPECS`, `advOwnValues` → `ADV_OWN`, `ADV_OWN_FROM` = the pack shown in the sheet as "valued from …"): (step price − gems × 0.00016 (`ADV_GEM_USD`) − the other items of the step at their own values) ÷ amount; a choice pack counts one slot × 3 (`slot`); `most` = the step with the most of the item (Glorious Tome: Shining City Pack, gems counted 0; Mythic Island Shards: Myriad Bazaar Heroes Gears). Links (`ADV_PACK_LINKS`): Research speedups = speedups, Charm Designs = Guides, Alloy = Polish ÷ 100, Amber = 10 Design Plans, FC Shard = FC ÷ 1.3, Refined FC = FC × 18.56, Fiery Heart = Sail ÷ 10, Mythic Gear = 62.5 Lucky Gear Chests, XP Component = XP 100 ÷ 100, Manual / Potion / Serum = Pet Chest ÷ 7 / 2 / 1; Affinity = Sail ÷ 1,000. An item with no pack falls back silently to the built-in list (`ADV_VALUES`, mean of its values, `advListVal`). Shown in USD. Cleared with the kept pack tables (`dashDataChanged("pack_tables")`).
- **Steps**: every row is a track's NEXT single step (Chief Gear the next star row, a charm the next stage, one T12 / T11 / Research Center / research-path / expert / skill / island / lighthouse level, a skin as one item); each track also lists its later steps (`nth` ≥ 1: "After this …" in the row sheet, the To target view). Gain = what it adds to every troop + stat (1% on one troop = 1; all-troops counts 3 times). Chief Gear's set bonuses: the 3-piece one to the piece cheapest to bring to the new tier, the 6-piece one shared by the pieces still below it. T12 research with no stat that a step waits for is charged as a share (except what's in the unlock / march rows: `ADV_T12_PRIO`). Only what can still be got: Shining City City Skins the person can pick (`shiningPickable`, not owned, 10,000 Glorious Tomes each), owned Mythic island buildings by shards / the person's Mark of Revival / Shining City shops (cheapest), lighthouses (Contracts); never Globe Level skins (`advIsGlobe`), other skins, event / ranked buildings. Built once per visit (`advSteps`, `ADV_STEPS` keyed by `CALC_SAVE_SEQ`; `advInvalidate` on skins / pack-table changes; `advSoon` redraws a card after a save).
- **Ranking**: $ per 1%, weighted by the person's upgrade order (calc_state `upgrade_order`, rank 1 ×1.5 down to rank 12 ×0.5, `advWeights`), or plain ("Priority | Pure value", kept in localStorage `adv_set`). Same-price same-bonus skins are one row ("City Skin ×24"). Pills green < $5, amber $5–20, red > $20 (`.pur-cmp` tags / `.trd-sc`).
- **Troop unlocks and march capacity first**: T12 Troop Unlock slots ("Needed for T12 troops"), T12 Troop Skill ("Part of T12 troops"), T11 Helios <troop> Unlock + every Helios Training level ("Needed for T11 troops", Training also +100 march / −5% training cost a level), then march capacity cheapest per +100 (T12 Solar Supremacy +100 a level, Research Center Regimental Expansion I–VI and T11 Flame Squad from the owner's game values: `ADV_MARCH_RC`, `ADV_MARCH_T11`, `ADV_MARCH_T12_SOLAR`) sit at the top of the list with a neutral pill ($ / "$X / +100"), never mixed into the % ranking; Best next is the first of them while any is left; with none left a green "✓ Troop unlocks and march capacity done" line (`advPriority`, `ADV_PRIO`, `advPriorityTracks` / `ADV_PRIO_TT`, sheet `advOpenPrioSheet`). The upgrade order sheet (`cdaOrderSheet(opts)`, opened from "⇅ Order" on the Sort by line) shows two fixed rows on top there ("Troop unlocks", "March capacity", 🔒 always first, not dragged / reset) and each row's weight; Free now's own sheet is unchanged.
- **Next step | To target** (`ADV_SET.view`): To target = one row per track from now to the target saved on its page (no target = the page's open max; tracks at target left out): "now → target", "#n · +gain · $total · N steps", a green / blue bar (`.pc-bar`) and the average $ per 1% (total ÷ weighted or plain gain); its sheet lists every step (long middles fold). Single items / one step: no bar, no "1 step", no "avg". Skins read "<skin> · buy" with "10,000 Tomes ($333)"; To target lists only the person's picked Shining City skins (`shiningPicked`). Page chips, Best next and the cards follow Next step.
- **Gap from max** card: the Dashboard's own numbers, saved when the Dashboard draws (`advGapSet`, localStorage `adv_gap_v1:<user>`).
- **Value by type** (`advRefTypes`, `advOpenTypesSheet`): a reference only (events / offers), every step of each whole table: Chief Gear, Charm, T12, T11, Research Center, Heroes Gears, Pets, Experts Level / Skills / Research, island building copies at $10 / by shards / Mark shop / Shining City, lighthouses, City Skin $240, Frame $5, Teleport $35, Shining City skins (Tomes), Prestige Appearance (Royal North + Winter Bloom profile = 10 × Emblem of Exquisiteness Pack $99.99, gems and 160 h of speedups taken off, `ADV_PRESTIGE`); range cheapest–dearest step, pill = whole-table cost ÷ whole gain. Never Globe skins, MVP, Name Card, Rally, Private Chat. Fixed prices in `ADV_FIXED_PRICES`.
- Not included: Pet Refine (no cost table for its stages). T keys `adv_*` + `nav.p:bestupgrades` in all 16 lang files (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 125).

**Backpack box "You have": Research Lab buttons and trade rows (owner picked B, 23 Nov 2026; step 86):** in the box pop-up's "You have" card (`haveFromHtml`, every planner page) the Research Lab row (T12's Refined FC box) has two buttons under it: "✓ On track" (`data-bshvlabok`, `rfcLabOnTrack`) saves calc_state `rfc_lab.game` = { wk, count: the plan's tries Mon → today (`planSoFar`), on: today } (other `rfc_lab` fields kept; the page redraws the pop-up, the button then reads "On track · today" and is greyed, `rfcLabOnTrackNow`), and "Update in Lab ›" (`data-bshvlab`) closes the pop-up and opens the Lab with its Game says window (`RFC_OPEN_GAME`), with a grey line under them. Each real trade row reads as the trade to make: "Trade 2,170 Fire Crystal →" with the amount it gives on the right (+2,821) and "not done yet · fits today's limit" (a rule with `perDay` when the trades fit one day's limit) / "… fits this week's limit" (weekly `max`) / "not done yet" (no limit); Gems / Advanced Marks / chests keep their old lines. T keys `bs_hv_lab_*`, `bs_hv_trade_do`, `bs_hv_trade_todo`, `bs_hv_trade_fit_week`, `bs_hv_trade_fit_day`; `bs_hv_trade`, `bs_hv_trade_week`, `bs_hv_traded` removed (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 126).

**Gen 15 heroes in Budgets goals (owner approved the previews, 23–24 Nov 2026; step 84; Budgets goals are owner only like the rest of Order of goals):**
- **Heroes not out yet as goals**: heroes of the next Gen (`heroGenNextPlannable`; `heroShardPlan` rows carry `notOut` = { gen, date, rel, forge }, from `heroGenNotOut` / `goalNotOutInfo`) can be planned before they come out: each hero's stars and widgets are goals (`goalUpgrades("hero_shards")`), plus two group goals per Gen, "All Gen N heroes · stars" (`gen<g>|s`) and "· widgets" (`gen<g>|w`). A hero that must be unlocked (the Gen's Hall of Heroes hero, Natalia / Jeronimo) has a 0★ = "Unlocked" level (`gu_unlocked` / `gu_locked`). A hero or group can be a goal more than once at other levels; hero and group goals on the same shards chain smaller first. In the goals list these rows sit under a "Gen N · out <date>" heading (`gu_out`, (i) `gu_out_info`), with a source line (`gu_src_*`: General shards / Value Hero Pack / Exclusive Gear Forge from <date> / Mystery Points saved before <date>, `srcLine`). The By pop-over (`goalByPopHtml`) greys every day before the release ("Gen N comes out <date>. Earlier days can't be picked", `gbp_lock*`) and adds Release / Forge pack stops (`gbp_release`, `gbp_forge`); a goal's date is never before its release (or the Forge when widgets wait for it).
- **Saving up before release**: the plan's hero trades for a not-out hero open only from its release week (`wk0` on a trade rule: no lots before week wk0, `r.max` a week after it; `tradeWeekCap`, `tradeWeeksForLots`, the plan cache key carries it): Mystery Points → its widgets, and for the Gen's Hall of Heroes hero Marks of Valor → its own shards. So Mystery Points, Marks and General shards bought or collected before are kept for that day; Widget Chests / Gen widgets don't go to them before release.
- **Hero targets fold** (inside the goals card, shown while Heroes is ticked): heroes at their targets fold into one "N heroes at target" line (`ht_done_1` / `ht_done_n`, `BUDGET_HT_DONE_OPEN`), then "Still to go", then the next Gen's heroes ("Gen N · not out yet · <date>", `ht_add_next`); Push and the "Each new Gen: push 2 heroes first" switch were removed (saved `push` / `pushGen` left in calc_state `hero_targets`, unused); "+ Add a hero" (heroes out now, then the next Gen) starts a hero at its Gen's targets; "+ Add as goals" adds the Gen's two group goals (`ht_add_goals*`).
- **Free now**: a not-out hero's upgrades show grey "from <date>" (`cdb_from`, `cdb_out*`, `cda_hero_from`); its shards wait in the Backpack. Hero upgrades count as "hero upgrades" (`cda_u_heroes`), widgets "Widget a → b" (`cda_hero_widget`).
- **Dated planner: partial stages** (`plannerDeadlineLocal`, `plannerDeadlineCover` in `PLANNER_FN_NAMES`): a stage that can't make its date now buys what it can by then (`partial`, `got` / `need`) and passes the rest on; a goal row shows "⚠ By <date>: a of b shards / widgets" or "By <date>: p% of it" (`gu_part_by`, `gu_part_pct`; the date line `bud_dates_part`). The leftover stays with its own page: a later goal of that page that needs it, else the page's own rest date, else after the target; never another page's date (each part keeps every page's share). The joined plan keeps the parts' order, so an earlier date's buys get the first days, and Daily Deals' one-a-day dates are worked out per part.
- **Goal dates**: green ✓ when on time, amber ⚠ when after the goal's By.
- **Budgets page sheet**: "Packs to buy" grouped by date; a pack this page shares shows this page's count with "of N for all pages" (`bud_of_all`); a hero trades line names the hero ("Hank widgets", "General shards", `bud_tr_*`). `budgetShares` counts a page's trade only when the plan really makes it (hidden rules always count).
- **Data change while planning**: `planKey` carries `PACK_GROUPS_GEN` (the same fix as main's #230; one copy), so a goal changed mid-plan restarts the plan instead of "Working out…" for ever.
- Fingerprints: Budgets plan `v: 7` (phones work the plans out again once).
- Unused T keys removed: `ht_desc`, `ht_push`, `ht_stars`, `ht_pushgen`, `ht_pushgen_sub`, `ht_gen_pick`. 43 new texts in all 16 lang files (`LANG_FILES_VERSION` 127).

**Start-up read skips backup rows + one copy of each language file (owner approved, 24 Nov 2026; step 91; nothing on screen changes):**
- `preloadCalcStates` reads calc_state with `calc_name=not.like.backup*` (no "_" in the pattern: LIKE's "_" matches any letter): the owner's undo backups (`backup_…`, 486 of the 558 KB the owner's start-up read carried on 24 Nov 2026) are left out; no page reads them. `loadCalcState` / `calcFlagSet` read a name the bulk read leaves out (`calcNotPreloaded`: starts with "backup") on its own, so a backup can still be read by name. Admin → Backup & Restore does its own `select=*` read of the owner's calc_state, so the file and the restore comparison still hold every backup row. A new kind of state that should stay out of the start-up read goes into `calcNotPreloaded` and the query.
- `sw.js`: `lang/<code>.json?v=N` is still cache-first, but storing a new `?v=` drops the older copies of that file (before, every old version stayed in the cache for ever). Cache name unchanged (`wosrst-v2`); other files unchanged.

## Restore point before the big jump (owner, 7 Nov 2026)
The owner is starting a big change. Everything up to PR #144 is the last known-good site: if the big change goes wrong, go back to the `main` commit that merged PR #144 (`git log origin/main --merges -1 --grep "#144"`) with a revert PR (never force-push `main`).
- Code: index.html + app.css + app.js + admin.js, sw.js cache `wosrst-v2`, `LANG_FILES_VERSION` 73; run `node tools/stamp.js` after any app.js / app.css / admin.js edit (since Speed plan Step 5 it also writes the .min copies the site loads).
